import Admonition from '@theme/Admonition';

<Admonition type="note" title="">

* This page lists public types of the `Raven.Client` assembly that are not described in a dedicated article.  
  Most of them are result, progress, state and helper types of the features described elsewhere in this documentation,
  or base classes and interfaces of types that are described.

* The listing is generated from the compiled assembly. For each type it shows the namespace, the kind, the base type and interfaces,
  and the members that the type declares itself (members that a type inherits are shown on the base type).
  Two kinds of comments appear above the declarations:
  * Lines that start with `///` are the XML documentation comments of the source code (the summary and the exceptions it documents).
    The authors of the code wrote them; the markup is removed.
  * Lines that start with `//` are short descriptions written for this page by reading the source code of the member, because the code has no XML comment for it.
    Many of them restate what the declaration says. A first reviewer wrote them from the source, and a second, independent reviewer then checked each one against the source again; the second review corrected about 3% of them and removed claims that the code does not show.
    They were produced by reading the code, not by running it against a server.
  A type is listed here because it is public in the assembly, which does not mean that a public API creates or returns it:
  for example, `AiAgentTruncateChat` is public, but the only constructor and property of the client that accept it are `internal`.

* The page also covers the public types and members of the `Sparrow`, `Raven.Embedded` and `Raven.TestDriver` assemblies, in the last two sections.
  The `Raven.Embedded` and `Raven.TestDriver` assemblies have no XML documentation comments, so every description of their members is a `//` description written by reading the code.

* The page has four parts:
  * [Types that have no dedicated article](#types-that-have-no-dedicated-article): each type is listed with all of its declared members.
  * [Members that no article mentions](#members-that-no-article-mentions): types that are described in an article, but that declare public members whose names appear nowhere else in this documentation.
    Only those members are listed, by their signatures, with the description from the source code where it has one.
  * [Types of the Sparrow and Raven.Embedded assemblies that have no dedicated article](#types-of-the-sparrow-and-ravenembedded-assemblies-that-have-no-dedicated-article): the same listing for those assemblies.
  * [Members of the Sparrow, Raven.Embedded and Raven.TestDriver assemblies that no article mentions](#members-of-the-sparrow-ravenembedded-and-raventestdriver-assemblies-that-no-article-mentions): the same listing of members for those assemblies.

* Types and members that are described in an article are not repeated here. To find them, search this documentation for the name.

</Admonition>

## Types that have no dedicated article

### `Raven.Client`

```csharp
/// Defines special states for subscription change vectors.
public enum Constants.Documents.SubscriptionChangeVectorSpecialStates
{
    /// Indicates that the change vector should not be changed.
    DoNotChange,
    /// Indicates that the subscription should start from the last document.
    LastDocument,
    /// Indicates that the subscription should start from the beginning of time.
    BeginningOfTime,
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents`

```csharp
/// Contains implementation of some IDocumentStore operations shared by DocumentStore implementations
public abstract class DocumentStoreBase : IDocumentStore
{
    // Returns the AiOperations instance for this store, creating it on first access.
    AiOperations AI { get; }
    /// The client certificate to use for authentication
    X509Certificate2 Certificate { get; set; }
    /// Gets the conventions.
    DocumentConventions Conventions { get; set; }
    /// The default database name
    string Database { get; set; }
    // Returns the HiLo identifier generator created at initialization. Throws InvalidOperationException if a
    // custom AsyncDocumentIdGenerator convention was set, so the default generator was not created.
    IHiLoIdGenerator HiLoIdGenerator { get; }
    // Gets or sets the store identifier. When not set explicitly, the getter returns the URLs joined by commas,
    // followed by the database name in the form (DB: name) if a database is set.
    string Identifier { get; set; }
    // Returns the MaintenanceOperationExecutor for this store, creating it on first access. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the store is not initialized.
    MaintenanceOperationExecutor Maintenance { get; }
    // Returns the OperationExecutor for this store, creating it on first access. Throws InvalidOperationException
    // if the store is not initialized.
    OperationExecutor Operations { get; }
    // Returns the DatabaseSmuggler for this store, creating it on first access.
    DatabaseSmuggler Smuggler { get; }
    // Gets the DocumentSubscriptions instance created for this store in the constructor.
    DocumentSubscriptions Subscriptions { get; }
    // Returns the TimeSeriesOperations instance for this store, creating it on first access.
    TimeSeriesOperations TimeSeries { get; }
    /// Gets or sets the Urls
    string[] Urls { get; set; }
    /// Whether the instance has been disposed
    bool WasDisposed { get; }
    /// Setup the context for aggressive caching.
    IDisposable AggressivelyCache(string database);
    /// Setup the context for aggressive caching.
    ValueTask<IDisposable> AggressivelyCacheAsync(string database);
    // Enables aggressive caching on the request executor of the given or default database for the cache duration,
    // using the mode from the AggressiveCache convention. Disposing the returned object restores the previous
    // setting.
    IDisposable AggressivelyCacheFor(TimeSpan cacheDuration, string database);
    // Enables aggressive caching on the database's request executor for the given duration and mode, and unless
    // the mode is DoNotTrackChanges waits for a changes listener that evicts cache items. Disposing the result
    // restores the previous setting.
    IDisposable AggressivelyCacheFor(TimeSpan cacheDuration, AggressiveCacheMode mode, string database);
    // Asynchronous version of AggressivelyCacheFor that uses the mode from the AggressiveCache convention. Returns
    // a disposable that restores the previous caching setting.
    ValueTask<IDisposable> AggressivelyCacheForAsync(TimeSpan cacheDuration, string database);
    // Asynchronously enables aggressive caching for the given duration and mode, and unless the mode is
    // DoNotTrackChanges connects a changes listener that evicts cache items. Disposing the result restores the
    // previous setting.
    ValueTask<IDisposable> AggressivelyCacheForAsync(TimeSpan cacheDuration, AggressiveCacheMode mode, string database);
    // Creates a BulkInsertOperation for the given or default database with no options. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the store is not initialized.
    BulkInsertOperation BulkInsert(string database, CancellationToken token);
    // Creates a BulkInsertOperation for the given or default database using the supplied options and cancellation
    // token. Throws InvalidOperationException if the store is not initialized.
    BulkInsertOperation BulkInsert(string database, BulkInsertOptions options, CancellationToken token);
    // Creates a BulkInsertOperation for the default database using the supplied options and cancellation token.
    BulkInsertOperation BulkInsert(BulkInsertOptions options, CancellationToken token);
    /// Subscribe to change notifications from the server
    IDatabaseChanges Changes(string database);
    // Returns a changes subscription bound to the given database and node tag, reusing an existing one for the
    // same options. Throws ArgumentNullException if nodeTag is null or whitespace.
    ISingleNodeDatabaseChanges Changes(string database, string nodeTag);
    // Clears the aggressive caching setting on the request executor of the given or default database. Disposing
    // the returned object restores the previous setting.
    IDisposable DisableAggressiveCaching(string database);
    // Disposes the store: raises BeforeDispose, disposes cache-eviction listeners and changes, returns unused HiLo
    // ranges, disposes subscriptions, sets WasDisposed, raises AfterDispose, then disposes request executors and
    // the certificate if the convention says so.
    void Dispose();
    // Synchronously executes the index creation task against the given or default database by waiting on
    // ExecuteIndexAsync.
    void ExecuteIndex(IAbstractIndexCreationTask task, string database);
    // Executes the index creation task through its ExecuteAsync method using the store conventions and the given
    // database. Throws InvalidOperationException if the store is not initialized.
    Task ExecuteIndexAsync(IAbstractIndexCreationTask task, string database, CancellationToken token);
    // Synchronously creates all the given indexes by waiting on ExecuteIndexesAsync.
    void ExecuteIndexes(IEnumerable<IAbstractIndexCreationTask> tasks, string database);
    // Creates all the given indexes using the store conventions by delegating to the overload that takes
    // conventions.
    Task ExecuteIndexesAsync(IEnumerable<IAbstractIndexCreationTask> tasks, string database, CancellationToken token);
    // Builds the index definitions from the tasks and sends them in a single PutIndexesOperation to the database.
    // Returns a completed task if there are none. Uses store conventions when conventions is null.
    Task ExecuteIndexesAsync(IEnumerable<IAbstractIndexCreationTask> tasks, DocumentConventions conventions, string database, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns the RequestExecutor for the given or default database, creating and caching one per database name.
    // Uses a single-node executor when the DisableTopologyUpdates convention is set. Throws if the store is not
    // initialized.
    RequestExecutor GetRequestExecutor(string databaseName);
    // Initializes the store: validates that URLs are set and valid and that any certificate has a private key,
    // sets up the default HiLo generator unless a custom one exists, freezes the conventions and marks the store
    // initialized. Returns this store.
    IDocumentStore Initialize();
    // Opens an asynchronous session on the default database with default SessionOptions.
    IAsyncDocumentSession OpenAsyncSession();
    // Opens an asynchronous session on the given database.
    IAsyncDocumentSession OpenAsyncSession(string database);
    // Opens an asynchronous session using the supplied SessionOptions. Throws if the store is not initialized or
    // has been disposed.
    IAsyncDocumentSession OpenAsyncSession(SessionOptions sessionOptions);
    // Opens a synchronous session on the default database, applying the
    // DisableAtomicDocumentWritesInClusterWideTransaction convention.
    IDocumentSession OpenSession();
    // Opens a synchronous session on the given database, applying the
    // DisableAtomicDocumentWritesInClusterWideTransaction convention.
    IDocumentSession OpenSession(string database);
    // Opens a synchronous session using the supplied SessionOptions. Throws if the store is not initialized or has
    // been disposed.
    IDocumentSession OpenSession(SessionOptions sessionOptions);
    // Sets the default timeout of the request executor for the given or default database and returns a disposable
    // that restores the previous timeout.
    IDisposable SetRequestTimeout(TimeSpan timeout, string database);
    // Private backing event for OnBeforeRequest, copied to each request executor when it is created.
    event EventHandler<BeforeRequestEventArgs> _onBeforeRequest;
    // Private backing event for OnFailedRequest, copied to each request executor when it is created.
    event EventHandler<FailedRequestEventArgs> _onFailedRequest;
    // Private backing event for OnSucceedRequest, copied to each request executor when it is created.
    event EventHandler<SucceedRequestEventArgs> _onSucceedRequest;
    // Private backing event for OnTopologyUpdated, copied to each request executor when it is created.
    event EventHandler<TopologyUpdatedEventArgs> _onTopologyUpdated;
    // Event raised during Dispose after WasDisposed is set to true and before the request executors and
    // certificate are disposed.
    event EventHandler AfterDispose;
    // Event raised at the start of Dispose before resources are released.
    event EventHandler BeforeDispose;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session after an entity is converted to a
    // document.
    event EventHandler<AfterConversionToDocumentEventArgs> OnAfterConversionToDocument;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session after a document is converted to an
    // entity.
    event EventHandler<AfterConversionToEntityEventArgs> OnAfterConversionToEntity;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session after SaveChanges completes.
    event EventHandler<AfterSaveChangesEventArgs> OnAfterSaveChanges;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session before an entity is converted to a
    // document.
    event EventHandler<BeforeConversionToDocumentEventArgs> OnBeforeConversionToDocument;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session before a document is converted to
    // an entity.
    event EventHandler<BeforeConversionToEntityEventArgs> OnBeforeConversionToEntity;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session before an entity is deleted.
    event EventHandler<BeforeDeleteEventArgs> OnBeforeDelete;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session before a query is executed.
    event EventHandler<BeforeQueryEventArgs> OnBeforeQuery;
    // Event forwarded to every request executor created by the store and raised before a request is sent. Adding
    // or removing handlers throws InvalidOperationException after initialization.
    event EventHandler<BeforeRequestEventArgs> OnBeforeRequest;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised by the session before an entity is stored.
    event EventHandler<BeforeStoreEventArgs> OnBeforeStore;
    // Event forwarded to every request executor created by the store and raised when a request fails. Adding or
    // removing handlers throws InvalidOperationException after initialization.
    event EventHandler<FailedRequestEventArgs> OnFailedRequest;
    // Event raised by the store each time a session is created, with the new session in the event args.
    event EventHandler<SessionCreatedEventArgs> OnSessionCreated;
    // Event copied to each session created by the store; raised when the session is disposing.
    event EventHandler<SessionDisposingEventArgs> OnSessionDisposing;
    // Event forwarded to every request executor created by the store and raised when a request succeeds. Adding or
    // removing handlers throws InvalidOperationException after initialization.
    event EventHandler<SucceedRequestEventArgs> OnSucceedRequest;
    // Event forwarded to every request executor created by the store and raised when the topology is updated.
    // Adding or removing handlers throws InvalidOperationException after initialization.
    event EventHandler<TopologyUpdatedEventArgs> OnTopologyUpdated;
}
```

```csharp
/// Extensions to the LINQ syntax
public static class LinqExtensions
{
    // Creates an aggregation query over the source that computes the given facet, by wrapping the source in an
    // AggregationQuery and adding the facet.
    static IAggregationQuery<T> AggregateBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, FacetBase facet);
    // Creates an aggregation query over the source from the given facets, adding them one after another. Returns
    // null if the facets sequence is empty.
    static IAggregationQuery<T> AggregateBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, IEnumerable<FacetBase> facets);
    // Invokes the builder on a new FacetBuilder and creates an aggregation query from the facet the builder
    // produced.
    static IAggregationQuery<T> AggregateBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Action<IFacetBuilder<T>> builder);
    // Creates an aggregation query that uses the facet setup stored in the document with the given id. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException if the id is null.
    static IAggregationQuery<T> AggregateUsing<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string facetSetupDocumentId);
    // Asynchronously returns whether the query yields any results. Throws InvalidOperationException if the source
    // provider is not an IRavenQueryProvider.
    static Task<bool> AnyAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Applies the predicate as a Where filter and asynchronously returns whether any results match. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException for a null source and InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query
    // provider.
    static Task<bool> AnyAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, bool>> predicate, CancellationToken token);
    /// Project into a different type.
    static IQueryable<TResult> As<TResult>(IQueryable queryable);
    // Asynchronously returns the number of results of the query as an int. Throws ArgumentNullException for a null
    // source and InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Task<int> CountAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Applies the predicate as a Where filter and asynchronously returns the number of matching results as an int.
    // Throws ArgumentNullException for a null source and InvalidOperationException for a non-Raven provider.
    static Task<int> CountAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, bool>> predicate, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns a Lazy int whose value is the query result count, executed lazily through the Raven query provider.
    // Throws InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<int> CountLazily<T>(IQueryable<T> source);
    // Returns a Lazy Task of int that yields the query result count, executed lazily through the Raven query
    // provider. Throws InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<Task<int>> CountLazilyAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> source, CancellationToken token);
    /// Filter allows querying on documents without the need for issuing indexes. It is meant for exploratory queries
    ///    or post query filtering. Criteria are evaluated at query time so please use Filter wisely to avoid
    ///    performance issues.
    static IRavenQueryable<T> Filter<T>(IRavenQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, bool>> predicate, int limit);
    /// 
    static IRavenQueryable<T> Filter<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, bool>> predicate, int limit);
    // Asynchronously returns the first result of the query. Throws ArgumentNullException for a null source and
    // InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Task<TSource> FirstAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Applies the predicate as a Where filter and asynchronously returns the first matching result. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException for a null source and InvalidOperationException for a non-Raven provider.
    static Task<TSource> FirstAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, bool>> predicate, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously returns the first result of the query, or the default value if there is none. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Task<TSource> FirstOrDefaultAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Applies the predicate as a Where filter and asynchronously returns the first matching result or the default
    // value. Throws InvalidOperationException for a non-Raven provider.
    static Task<TSource> FirstOrDefaultAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, bool>> predicate, CancellationToken token);
    /// Creates a dynamic query which does an aggregation of data grouped by entire content of an array. The group by
    ///    key will be calculated by hashing all values of the array. Underneath an auto map-reduce index will be
    ///    created to handle such query.
    static IRavenQueryable<IGrouping<IEnumerable<TKey>, TSource>> GroupByArrayContent<TSource, TKey>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, IEnumerable<TKey>>> fieldSelector);
    /// Creates a dynamic query which does an aggregation of data grouped by individual values of an array.
    ///    Underneath a fanout auto map-reduce index will be created to handle such query.
    static IRavenQueryable<IGrouping<TKey, TSource>> GroupByArrayValues<TSource, TKey>(IQueryable<TSource> source, Expression<Func<TSource, IEnumerable<TKey>>> fieldSelector);
    // Converts the expression to a property path using the session conventions and adds an include for that path
    // to the query.
    static IRavenQueryable<TResult> Include<TResult>(IQueryable<TResult> source, Expression<Func<TResult, object>> path);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and adds an include for it, appending the document id prefix of
    // the TInclude collection, per the session conventions, in parentheses after the path.
    static IRavenQueryable<TResult> Include<TResult, TInclude>(IQueryable<TResult> source, Expression<Func<TResult, object>> path);
    // Adds an include for the given path to the query by creating a query call expression on the source provider.
    static IRavenQueryable<TResult> Include<TResult>(IQueryable<TResult> source, string path);
    // Invokes the action on a new IncludeBuilder to configure the includes, then adds those includes to the query.
    static IRavenQueryable<TResult> Include<TResult>(IQueryable<TResult> source, Action<IQueryIncludeBuilder<TResult>> includes);
    // Marks the query as an intersect query by creating a query call expression on the source provider.
    static IRavenQueryable<T> Intersect<T>(IQueryable<T> self);
    // Returns a Lazy enumerable that executes the query lazily through the Raven query provider. Throws
    // ArgumentException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<IEnumerable<T>> Lazily<T>(IQueryable<T> source);
    // Returns a Lazy enumerable that executes the query lazily and invokes onEval with the results when evaluated.
    // Throws ArgumentException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<IEnumerable<T>> Lazily<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Action<IEnumerable<T>> onEval);
    // Returns a Lazy Task of an enumerable that executes the query lazily and asynchronously through the Raven
    // query provider. Throws ArgumentException for a non-Raven provider.
    static Lazy<Task<IEnumerable<T>>> LazilyAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> source);
    // Returns a Lazy Task of an enumerable that executes the query lazily and asynchronously and invokes onEval
    // with the results. Throws ArgumentException for a non-Raven provider.
    static Lazy<Task<IEnumerable<T>>> LazilyAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Action<IEnumerable<T>> onEval);
    /// Returns the number of query results as int64 type.
    static long LongCount<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source);
    // Asynchronously returns the number of results of the query as a long. Throws ArgumentNullException for a null
    // source and InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Task<long> LongCountAsync<TSource>(IQueryable<TSource> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns a Lazy long whose value is the query result count, executed lazily through the Raven query provider.
    // Throws InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<long> LongCountLazily<T>(IQueryable<T> source);
    // Returns a Lazy Task of long that yields the query result count, executed lazily through the Raven query
    // provider. Throws InvalidOperationException if the provider is not a Raven query provider.
    static Lazy<Task<long>> LongCountLazilyAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> source, CancellationToken token);
    // Adds a more-like-this clause using the given MoreLikeThisBase object to the query.
    static IRavenQueryable<T> MoreLikeThis<T>(IQueryable<T> source, MoreLikeThisBase moreLikeThis);
    // Invokes the builder on a new MoreLikeThisBuilder and adds the resulting more-like-this clause to the query.
    static IRavenQueryable<T> MoreLikeThis<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Action<IMoreLikeThisBuilder<T>> builder);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders ascending by that field using the named sorter.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, string sorterName);
    // Orders the query ascending by the given field path using the named sorter.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, string sorterName);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders ascending by it using the given OrderingType. The
    // ordering defaults to String.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, OrderingType ordering);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders ascending by it with the given nulls ordering and
    // OrderingType.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders the query ascending by the given field path using the given OrderingType, which defaults to String.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders the query ascending by the given field path with the given nulls ordering and OrderingType.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderBy<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders descending by that field using the named sorter.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, string sorterName);
    // Orders the query descending by the given field path using the named sorter. Throws ArgumentNullException if
    // the sorter name is null or whitespace.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, string sorterName);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders descending by it using the given OrderingType. The
    // ordering defaults to String.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, OrderingType ordering);
    // Converts the expression to a property path and orders descending by it with the given nulls ordering and
    // OrderingType.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders the query descending by the given field path using the given OrderingType, which defaults to String.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders the query descending by the given field path with the given nulls ordering and OrderingType.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string path, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Builds a dynamic spatial field with the factory function and orders by distance from the given latitude and
    // longitude, ascending.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Func<DynamicSpatialFieldFactory<T>, DynamicSpatialField> field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by the distance of the dynamic spatial field from the given latitude and longitude.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the field path in the expression,
    // with a round factor of 0.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the field path in the expression,
    // using the given round factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the named field, with a round factor
    // of 0.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the named field, using the given
    // round factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Builds a dynamic spatial field with the factory function and orders ascending by its distance from the shape
    // given as WKT.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Func<DynamicSpatialFieldFactory<T>, DynamicSpatialField> field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by the distance of the dynamic spatial field from the shape given as WKT.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the WKT shape for the field path in the expression.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the shape given as WKT.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistance<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Builds a dynamic spatial field with the factory function and orders by distance from the given latitude and
    // longitude, descending.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Func<DynamicSpatialFieldFactory<T>, DynamicSpatialField> field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by the distance of the dynamic spatial field from the given latitude and longitude.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the field path in the expression,
    // with a round factor of 0.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the field path in the expression,
    // using the given round factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the named field, with a round factor
    // of 0.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude for the named field, using the given
    // round factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);

    // factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, Expression<Func<T, object>> path, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the shape given as WKT, with a round factor of 0.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the shape given as WKT, using the given round factor.
    static IOrderedQueryable<T> OrderByDistanceDescending<T>(IQueryable<T> source, string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Orders the query by relevance score ascending by creating a query call expression on the source provider.

    // Orders the query by relevance score descending by creating a query call expression on the source provider.
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.AI`

```csharp
/// Manages AI agents and conversation interactions in a specific RavenDB database.
public class AiOperations
{
    // Creates the instance for the given store and database. Uses the store default database if databaseName is
    // null, validates the store is not null, and creates a maintenance executor for that database.
    AiOperations(IDocumentStore store, string databaseName);
    /// Opens a conversation with an AI agent.
    IAiConversationOperations Conversation(string agentId, string conversationId, AiConversationCreationOptions creationOptions, string changeVector);
    /// Creates or updates an AI agent configuration (with the given schema) on the database.
    AiAgentConfigurationResult CreateAgent(AiAgentConfiguration configuration);
    /// Creates or updates an AI agent configuration (with the given schema) on the database.
    AiAgentConfigurationResult CreateAgent<TSchema>(AiAgentConfiguration configuration, TSchema sampleObject);
    /// Asynchronously creates or updates an AI agent configuration (with the given schema) on the database.
    Task<AiAgentConfigurationResult> CreateAgentAsync<TSchema>(AiAgentConfiguration configuration, TSchema sampleObject, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously creates or updates an AI agent configuration (with the given schema) on the database.
    Task<AiAgentConfigurationResult> CreateAgentAsync(AiAgentConfiguration configuration, CancellationToken token);
    /// Deletes an AI agent from the database.
    AiAgentConfigurationResult DeleteAgent(string agentId);
    /// Asynchronously deletes an AI agent from the database.
    Task<AiAgentConfigurationResult> DeleteAgentAsync(string agentId, CancellationToken token);
    /// Returns a 'AiOperations' for a different database.
    AiOperations ForDatabase(string databaseName);
    /// Retrieves the AI agent configuration for a specific agent.
    AiAgentConfiguration GetAgent(string agentId);
    /// Retrieves the AI agent configuration for a specific agent asynchronously.
    Task<AiAgentConfiguration> GetAgentAsync(string agentId, CancellationToken token);
    /// Retrieves all AI agents and their configurations.
    GetAiAgentsResponse GetAgents();
    /// Retrieves all AI agents and their configurations.
    Task<GetAiAgentsResponse> GetAgentsAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Reads messages from an AI conversation. Returns the most recent messages by default.
    AiConversationMessagesResult GetConversationMessages(string conversationId);
    /// Reads messages from an AI conversation with full control over paging and filtering.
    AiConversationMessagesResult GetConversationMessages(GetConversationMessagesOptions parameters);
    /// Reads messages from an AI conversation. Returns the most recent messages by default.
    Task<AiConversationMessagesResult> GetConversationMessagesAsync(string conversationId, CancellationToken token);
    /// Reads messages from an AI conversation with full control over paging and filtering.
    Task<AiConversationMessagesResult> GetConversationMessagesAsync(GetConversationMessagesOptions parameters, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base class for bulk insert operations that stores the execute task and operation id, ensures the
// stream before the first store, and throws BulkInsertAbortedException on failures.
public abstract class BulkInsertOperationBase<T>
{
    // Abstract method that aborts the bulk insert operation. Implemented by derived classes.
    Task AbortAsync();
    // Abstract method that stores the given entity under the given id in the bulk insert. Implemented by derived
    // classes.
    Task StoreAsync(T entity, string id);
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base class for query commands that send a POST request to the queries endpoint of the database. Its
// constructor derives the timeout from the index query non-stale wait timeout and disables aggressive caching
// when waiting for non-stale results.
public abstract class AbstractQueryCommand<TResult, TParameters> : RavenCommand<TResult>
{
    // Returns true, marking the command as a read request.
    bool IsReadRequest { get; }
    // Builds a POST request to the database queries endpoint with a queryHash parameter, and optional
    // metadataOnly, debug=entries, ignoreLimit and tag parameters. Content comes from GetContent.
    HttpRequestMessage CreateRequest(JsonOperationContext ctx, ServerNode node, out string url);
}
```

```csharp
// Result of the get conflicts command, holding the document id, the conflict entries, the largest etag and the
// total number of results.

{

    // Gets or sets the largest etag; the source comment describes it as the etag of the conflict itself.
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.Commands.Batches`

```csharp
// Abstract base for batch commands that carry a time series operation for one document. Its constructor
// requires a non-blank document id and time series name and creates a TimeSeriesOperation with that name.
public abstract class TimeSeriesCommandData : ICommandData
{
    // Always returns null.
    string ChangeVector { get; }
    // Optional flag that, when it has a value, is written to the command JSON as FromEtl.
    bool? FromEtl { get; set; }
    // The id of the document that owns the time series; set from the constructor argument.
    string Id { get; set; }
    // The name of the time series; set from the constructor argument.
    string Name { get; set; }
    // The TimeSeriesOperation holding the appends, deletes or increments of this command.
    TimeSeriesOperation TimeSeries { get; }
    // Abstract property that returns the CommandType of the command; derived classes return TimeSeries or
    // TimeSeriesWithIncrements.
    CommandType Type { get; }
    // Does nothing.
    void OnBeforeSaveChanges(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session);
    // Builds a DynamicJsonValue with Id, TimeSeries (the operation as JSON) and Type, plus FromEtl when it has a
    // value.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
    // Ignores the conventions and context arguments and returns the result of the parameterless ToJson.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson(DocumentConventions conventions, JsonOperationContext context);
}
```

```csharp
    /// Gets all metadata values cast to the specified type.

```

```csharp
/// Records the statically-known shape of a single index class so the RavenDB analyzers can reason about it from
///    any assembly. Emitted per index type at assembly level by the analyzer package's source generator; it is
///    not meant to be written by hand.
public sealed class RavenIndexMetadataAttribute : Attribute
{
    // Creates the attribute and stores the given index class type in IndexType.
    RavenIndexMetadataAttribute(Type indexType);
    /// Number of AddMap / AddMapForAll call sites across the constructors in the chain.
    int AddMapCount { get; set; }
    /// Whether any of those AddMap call sites sits inside a loop, in which case the call count understates how many
    ///    maps are registered at runtime.
    bool AddMapInLoop { get; set; }
    /// Whether the generator could read the index completely. False when the shape cannot be determined statically:
    ///    a JavaScript index, dynamic field creation via CreateField, a block-bodied map lambda, or a base class
    ///    whose own metadata is missing or unanalyzable. Consumers must treat every other value here as unknown when
    ///    this is false, rather than as an empty set, so that an unreadable index never produces a false report.
    bool Analyzable { get; set; }
    /// Whether any constructor in the chain assigns the Map property.
    bool AssignsMap { get; set; }
    /// Whether 'MapFields', 'StoredFields' and 'StoreAllFields' could be read. False when the map projection or a
    ///    storage directive has a shape the generator cannot follow (a block-bodied lambda, dynamic field creation
    ///    via CreateField, a computed field name). The remaining values stay valid, since they describe how the
    ///    index defines itself rather than which fields it produces.
    bool FieldsKnown { get; set; }
    /// The index class this metadata describes.
    Type IndexType { get; }
    /// Whether any constructor in the chain assigns the Reduce property. A map-reduce index serves every projected
    ///    field from its index entries, so stored-field rules do not apply.
    bool IsMapReduce { get; set; }
    /// Field names produced by the map projection across the chain. Empty when the index projects nothing statically
    ///    knowable.
    string[] MapFields { get; set; }
    /// Whether StoreAllFields(FieldStorage.Yes) was called anywhere in the chain, which makes every map-projection
    ///    field stored regardless of 'StoredFields'.
    bool StoreAllFields { get; set; }
    /// Field names explicitly stored across the chain, via Store(…), Stores[…] = FieldStorage.Yes, or
    ///    StoresStrings[…] = FieldStorage.Yes. Being part of the map projection does not make a field stored.
    string[] StoredFields { get; set; }
    /// Whether the chain writes to AdditionalSources or AdditionalAssemblies, shipping C# for the server to compile.
    ///    A helper called from the map may then be translatable after all, so rules about untranslatable calls must
    ///    stand down for such an index.
    bool UsesAdditionalCode { get; set; }
    /// Whether any of those call sites is AddMapForAll, which maps every collection derived from its type argument
    ///    and therefore has no single-map equivalent.
    bool UsesAddMapForAll { get; set; }
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.Linq`

```csharp
// Describes a field to fetch in a LINQ projection, with a name and an optional alias. Instances are compared
// by name and alias.
public sealed class FieldToFetch
{
    // Stores the name and stores the alias only when it differs from the name; otherwise the alias is null.
    FieldToFetch(string name, string alias);
    // The alias of the field, or null when it equals the name.
    string Alias { get; }
    // The name of the field to fetch.
    string Name { get; }
    // Returns true when the other object is a FieldToFetch of the same type with equal Name and ordinally equal
    // Alias.
    bool Equals(object obj);
    // Returns a hash code combining the ordinal hash codes of Name and Alias, treating null as 0.
    int GetHashCode();
}
```

```csharp
/// Generate a new document query
public interface IDocumentQueryGenerator
{
    /// Gets the conventions associated with this query
    DocumentConventions Conventions { get; }
    // Returns the InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session associated with the query generator.
    InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations Session { get; }
    /// Create a new query for
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> AsyncQuery<T>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
    /// Generates a query inspector
    RavenQueryInspector<TS> CreateRavenQueryInspector<TS>();
    /// Create a new query for
    IDocumentQuery<T> Query<T>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
/// Extension for the built-in 'IQueryProvider' allowing for Raven specific operations
public interface IRavenQueryProvider : IQueryProvider
{
    /// The action to execute on the customize query
    Action<IDocumentQueryCustomization> CustomizeQuery { get; }
    /// Set the fields to fetch
    HashSet<FieldToFetch> FieldsToFetch { get; }
    /// Gets the name of the index.
    string IndexName { get; }
    // Gets or sets a flag indicating whether the query provider handles a project-into query.
    bool IsProjectInto { get; set; }
    // Returns the Type of the original query.
    Type OriginalQueryType { get; }
    /// Get the query generator
    IDocumentQueryGenerator QueryGenerator { get; }
    /// Callback to get the results of the query
    void AfterQueryExecuted(Action<QueryResult> afterQueryExecuted);
    /// Convert the Linq query to a lazy-count Lucene query and provide a function to execute when it is being
    ///    evaluated
    Lazy<int> CountLazily<T>(Expression expression);
    /// Convert the Linq query to a lazy-count Lucene query and provide a function to execute when it is being
    ///    evaluated
    Lazy<Task<int>> CountLazilyAsync<T>(Expression expression, CancellationToken token);
    /// Customizes the query using the specified action
    void Customize(Action<IDocumentQueryCustomization> action);
    /// Change the result type for the query provider
    IRavenQueryProvider For<TS>();
    /// Convert the Linq query to a lazy Lucene query and provide a function to execute when it is being evaluated
    Lazy<IEnumerable<T>> Lazily<T>(Expression expression, Action<IEnumerable<T>> onEval);
    // Converts the LINQ expression to a lazy asynchronous query and returns a Lazy of Task of IEnumerable; the
    // onEval action is invoked when it is evaluated.
    Lazy<Task<IEnumerable<T>>> LazilyAsync<T>(Expression expression, Action<IEnumerable<T>> onEval);
    /// Convert the Linq query to a lazy-count Lucene query and provide a function to execute when it is being
    ///    evaluated
    Lazy<long> LongCountLazily<T>(Expression expression);
    /// Convert the Linq query to a lazy-count Lucene query and provide a function to execute when it is being
    ///    evaluated
    Lazy<Task<long>> LongCountLazilyAsync<T>(Expression expression, CancellationToken token);
    // Converts the LINQ expression to an IAsyncDocumentQuery of T.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> ToAsyncDocumentQuery<T>(Expression expression);
    // Converts the LINQ expression to an IDocumentQuery of TResult.
    IDocumentQuery<TResult> ToDocumentQuery<TResult>(Expression expression);
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base for database statistics. It holds counts of indexes, documents, revisions, document conflicts,
// tombstones, conflicts, attachments, counter entries and time series segments, plus an array of index
// information.
public abstract class AbstractDatabaseStatistics<TIndexInformation>
{
    /// Total number of attachments in database.
    long CountOfAttachments { get; set; }
    /// Total number of conflicts in database.
    long CountOfConflicts { get; set; }
    /// Total number of counter-group entries in database.
    long CountOfCounterEntries { get; set; }
    /// Total number of documents in database.
    long CountOfDocuments { get; set; }
    /// Total number of documents conflicts in database.
    long CountOfDocumentsConflicts { get; set; }
    /// Total number of indexes in database.
    int CountOfIndexes { get; set; }
    /// Total number of revision documents in database.
    long CountOfRevisionDocuments { get; set; }
    /// Total number of time-series segments in database.
    long CountOfTimeSeriesSegments { get; set; }
    /// Total number of tombstones in database.
    long CountOfTombstones { get; set; }
    /// Statistics for each index in database.
    TIndexInformation[] Indexes { get; set; }
}
```

```csharp
// Operation result describing a failure; holds the exception type name, message, full exception text and HTTP
// status code.
public sealed class OperationExceptionResult : IOperationResult
{
    // Parameterless constructor used for deserialization.
    OperationExceptionResult();
    // Copies the exception type full name, message and ToString text into Type, Message and Error, stores the
    // status code and the persistence flag, and records the limit type for a LicenseLimitException.
    OperationExceptionResult(Exception exception, HttpStatusCode statusCode, bool shouldBePersistent);
    // The full text of the exception, taken from Exception.ToString.
    string Error { get; set; }
    // The message of the exception.
    string Message { get; set; }
    // Returns whether the result should be persisted; set from the constructor argument and false for the
    // parameterless constructor.
    bool ShouldPersist { get; }
    // The HTTP status code associated with the result, for special results such as 409.
    HttpStatusCode StatusCode { get; set; }
    // The full type name of the exception.
    string Type { get; set; }
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue with Type, Message, Error, StatusCode as an integer and LimitType.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
// Operation result that holds a message and a list of OperationExceptionResult items.
public sealed class OperationMultipleExceptionsResult : IOperationResult
{
    // Creates an instance with a null message and an empty exceptions list.
    OperationMultipleExceptionsResult();
    // Creates an instance with the given message and an empty exceptions list.
    OperationMultipleExceptionsResult(string message);
    // The list of OperationExceptionResult items, initialized empty.
    List<OperationExceptionResult> Exceptions { get; }
    // The message given in the constructor, or null.
    string Message { get; }
    // Always false because it has no setter and is never assigned.
    bool ShouldPersist { get; }
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue with Message and Exceptions, the latter as an array of each exception result
    // JSON.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base for a per-shard, per-node operation result that holds shard number, node tag and a wrapped
// result of type TResult. It cannot be merged.
public abstract class ShardNodeOperationResult<TResult> : IShardNodeOperationResult<TResult>
{
    // Always returns false.
    bool CanMerge { get; }
    // Message of the result; null because it is only set to null in the constructor.
    string Message { get; }
    // Gets or sets the tag of the node that produced the result.
    string NodeTag { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the wrapped operation result of type TResult.
    TResult Result { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the number of the shard that produced the result.
    int ShardNumber { get; set; }
    // Abstract property that derived classes implement to state whether the result should be persisted.
    bool ShouldPersist { get; }
    // Always throws NotSupportedException.
    void MergeWith(IOperationResult result);
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue with ShardNumber, NodeTag and the JSON of Result.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
/// Flags describing what differs between two AI settings/configurations. These distinctions help determine
///    whether embeddings must be regenerated or if a simple configuration update is sufficient.

{

    /// Identifier value changed.
    Identifier,
    /// Embedding dimensionality changed.
    EmbeddingDimensions,
    /// Model name/version changed in a way that affects embedding structure.
    ModelArchitecture,
    /// Convenience combination representing structure-impacting changes.
    EmbeddingStructure,
    /// Endpoint/URL configuration changed.
    EndpointConfiguration,
    /// Authentication-related settings changed (API key, org/project, etc.).
    AuthenticationSettings,
    /// Convenience combination representing connection-related changes.
    ConnectionConfig,
    /// Provider-specific deployment changed (e.g., Azure deployment name).
    DeploymentConfiguration,
    /// Indicates that previously generated embeddings should be regenerated.
    RequiresEmbeddingsRegeneration,
    /// All changes.
    All,
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.Operations.CdcSink.Schema`

```csharp
/// One source-side column as the CDC schema-discovery endpoint sees it. Field names mirror 'CdcColumnMapping' so
///    Studio can drop the response straight into its mapping model.
public class CdcSinkSourceColumn : IDynamicJson
{
    // Creates an empty CdcSinkSourceColumn instance.
    CdcSinkSourceColumn();
    /// True when CDC can actually capture this column. False when the source-side type has no CDC mapping
    ///    (PostgreSQL extension OIDs the streaming code would throw on) or when the column is not enrolled in the
    ///    SQL Server CDC capture list.
    bool IsCdcCapturable { get; set; }
    /// True when this column participates in the source table's primary key.
    bool IsPrimaryKey { get; set; }
    /// Source column name. Matches 'Column'.
    string Name { get; set; }
    /// Raw source-side type string (e.g. "varchar", "bigint", "jsonb"). For MySQL this is DATA_TYPE (without
    ///    precision); the fuller COLUMN_TYPE form is not surfaced here today.
    string NativeType { get; set; }
    /// What CDC will store this column as by default. Json for PostgreSQL jsonb/json and MySQL json; Attachment for
    ///    PostgreSQL bytea and SQL Server varbinary/image; Default otherwise.
    CdcColumnType SuggestedType { get; set; }
    /// Human-readable reason set when 'IsCdcCapturable' is false. Null on capturable columns.
    string UnsupportedReason { get; set; }
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue with Name, NativeType, SuggestedType as a string, IsPrimaryKey, IsCdcCapturable
    // and UnsupportedReason.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
/// Foreign-key reference from one source table to another, surfaced by the CDC schema-discovery endpoint so
///    Studio can suggest linked tables for the CDC mapping UI.
public class CdcSinkSourceForeignKey : IDynamicJson
{
    // Creates a foreign key description with empty Columns and ReferencedColumns lists.
    CdcSinkSourceForeignKey();
    /// FK column(s) on the source ("child") table.
    List<string> Columns { get; set; }
    /// PK column(s) on the referenced table.
    List<string> ReferencedColumns { get; set; }
    /// Schema of the referenced ("parent") table.
    string ReferencedSchema { get; set; }
    /// Name of the referenced ("parent") table.
    string ReferencedTable { get; set; }
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue with Columns, ReferencedSchema, ReferencedTable and ReferencedColumns.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
/// Top-level response of POST /admin/cdc-sink/schema. Carries the source-side schema annotated with CDC-specific
///    capturability hints so Studio can drive the CDC mapping UI without a second round-trip.
public class CdcSinkSourceSchema : IDynamicJson
{
    // Creates a source schema with empty Tables and Errors lists.
    CdcSinkSourceSchema();
    /// Source database / catalog name (whatever connection.Database returns for MS SQL / MySQL / PostgreSQL; the
    ///    Oracle owner for Oracle — Oracle is not a CDC source today but the field stays generic).
    string CatalogName { get; set; }
    /// Whole-request failures (validation, missing connection-string, source DB unreachable) and connection-level
    ///    verification blockers (e.g. PostgreSQL wal_level not logical, the connecting user lacking the privilege to
    ///    provision CDC with no infrastructure in place). Per-table issues live on the individual
    ///    'UnsupportedReason' / 'Warnings' / column-level 'UnsupportedReason' fields instead.
    List<string> Errors { get; set; }
    /// Whether the connecting user has sufficient privileges to provision the CDC infrastructure (PostgreSQL
    ///    replication slot/publication, SQL Server sp_cdc_enable_*). When false an administrator must set CDC up
    ///    out-of-band. Distinct from per-table 'IsCdcEnabled', which reports whether CDC is already active.
    bool HasPermissionToSetup { get; set; }
    /// True when nothing blocks setting up CDC against this source — i.e. there are no 'Errors'. Warnings do not
    ///    affect success.
    bool Success { get; }
    /// Discovered source tables, each annotated with CDC capturability hints.
    List<CdcSinkSourceTable> Tables { get; set; }
    /// Non-fatal connection-level verification findings (e.g. SQL Server Agent not running, CDC infrastructure
    ///    already present under a reduced-privilege account). Per-table warnings live on 'Warnings'.
    List<string> Warnings { get; set; }
    // Returns the schema as a DynamicJsonValue built from its properties, including catalog name, tables and
    // errors.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
/// One source-side table as the CDC schema-discovery endpoint sees it. Field names mirror 'CdcSinkTableConfig'
///    so Studio can populate the CDC mapping form with minimal transformation.
public class CdcSinkSourceTable : IDynamicJson
{
    // Creates a source table description with an empty Columns list.
    CdcSinkSourceTable();
    /// Source columns of this table, in discovery order.
    List<CdcSinkSourceColumn> Columns { get; set; }
    /// Foreign keys leaving this table. Studio uses these to suggest 'CdcSinkLinkedTableConfig' entries.
    List<CdcSinkSourceForeignKey> ForeignKeys { get; set; }
    /// True when CDC tracking is active for this table at the source. SQL Server: present in cdc.change_tables.
    ///    PostgreSQL / MySQL: always true (publication / binlog membership is a database-level concern; surfaced
    ///    elsewhere).
    bool IsCdcEnabled { get; set; }
    /// Names of the columns that make up this table's primary key.
    List<string> PrimaryKeyColumns { get; set; }
    /// SQL table name. Matches 'SourceTableName'.
    string SourceTableName { get; set; }
    /// SQL schema. Matches 'SourceTableSchema'.
    string SourceTableSchema { get; set; }
    /// Set when the entire table cannot be CDC-captured. Null on usable tables.
    string UnsupportedReason { get; set; }
    /// Non-fatal, table-scoped verification findings that don't make the table unusable but affect captured data
    ///    quality — e.g. PostgreSQL REPLICA IDENTITY that won't carry row-identifying columns on DELETE.
    List<string> Warnings { get; set; }
    // Returns the table as a DynamicJsonValue built from its properties, including schema, table name and columns.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.Operations.ETL`

```csharp
// Holds the persisted state of an ETL process for one configuration and transformation, including last
// processed etags per database id, change vector and node tag.
public sealed class EtlProcessState : IDatabaseTaskStatus
{
    // Initializes LastProcessedEtagPerDbId to an empty dictionary and ChangeVector to null.
    EtlProcessState();
    // Gets or sets the change vector stored in the ETL process state.
    string ChangeVector { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the name of the ETL configuration this state belongs to.
    string ConfigurationName { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the time of the last ETL batch, or null if none is recorded.
    DateTime? LastBatchTime { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the last processed etag for each database id.
    Dictionary<string, long> LastProcessedEtagPerDbId { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the tag of the node that owns this ETL process state.
    string NodeTag { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the set of document ids whose time series were skipped because the time series arrived without
    // its document, so they are loaded together with the document later.
    HashSet<string> SkippedTimeSeriesDocs { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the name of the transformation this state belongs to.
    string TransformationName { get; set; }
    // Returns the state item key in the form values/databaseName/etl/configurationName/transformationName, with
    // configuration and transformation names lowercased.
    static string GenerateItemName(string databaseName, string configurationName, string transformationName);
    // Returns the last processed etag stored for the given database id, or 0 if there is none. The node tag
    // argument is not used.
    long GetLastProcessedEtag(string dbId, string nodeTag);
    // Returns a DynamicJsonValue containing configuration name, transformation name, last processed etags per
    // database id, change vector, node tag, skipped time series documents and last batch time.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base for external replication tasks; stores task id, name, connection string name and mentor node
// settings and implements the database task interface.
public abstract class ExternalReplicationBase : ReplicationNode, IDatabaseTask
{
    // Gets or sets the name of the connection string used to reach the destination.
    string ConnectionStringName { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the tag of the mentor node preferred to run the task.
    string MentorNode { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the name of the replication task.
    string Name { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the task is pinned to its mentor node.
    bool PinToMentorNode { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the task id. The setter throws InvalidOperationException if GetHashCode was already invoked on
    // the instance.
    long TaskId { get; set; }
    // Throws ArgumentNullException for ConnectionStringName if it is null or empty.
    void AssertValidReplication();
    // Returns the result of IsEqualTo for the other instance.
    bool Equals(ExternalReplicationBase other);
    // Returns true for the same reference; false for null or a different runtime type; otherwise compares using
    // IsEqualTo.
    bool Equals(object obj);
    // Returns the string External Replication to followed by the connection string name.
    string GetDefaultTaskName();
    // Returns the hash code of TaskId and marks the instance so TaskId can no longer be changed.
    int GetHashCode();
    // Returns the MentorNode value.
    string GetMentorNode();
    // Returns a ulong key computed from hashes of Database and ConnectionStringName combined with TaskId.
    UInt64 GetTaskKey();
    // Returns the Name value.
    string GetTaskName();
    // Returns true if other is an ExternalReplicationBase with the same connection string name and database name
    // (case-insensitive) and the same TaskId.
    bool IsEqualTo(ReplicationNode other);
    // Returns the value of PinToMentorNode.
    bool IsPinnedToMentorNode();
    // Always returns false.
    bool IsResourceIntensive();
    // Removes the first task in the list whose TaskId equals taskId; does nothing if none matches.
    static void RemoveExternalReplication<T>(List<T> replicationTasks, long taskId);
    // Returns the same JSON as ToJson by default; virtual so derived classes can customize the audit form.
    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
    // Returns the base JSON extended with TaskId, Name, MentorNode, PinToMentorNode and ConnectionStringName.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
}
```

```csharp
// Result of a revert-revisions operation; extends OperationResult with the count of documents reverted.
public sealed class RevertResult : OperationResult
{
    // Initializes a new RevertResult with default values.
    RevertResult();
    // Always returns true.
    bool CanMerge { get; }
    // Gets or sets the number of documents that were reverted.
    int RevertedDocuments { get; set; }
    // Returns a new RevertResult copying reverted documents count, etag barriers, scanned counts, last processed
    // etags, node tags and warnings.
    IOperationProgress Clone();
    // If result is a RevertResult, adds its RevertedDocuments to this one and merges the base result; otherwise
    // does nothing.
    void MergeWith(IOperationResult result);
    // If progress is a RevertResult, adds its RevertedDocuments to this one and merges the base progress;
    // otherwise does nothing.
    void MergeWith(IOperationProgress progress);
    // Returns the base JSON extended with RevertedDocuments.
    DynamicJsonValue ToJson();
    // Adds a warning with id Revert Collection Fail No. followed by an incremented failed-collection counter,
    // using the given message.
    void WarnAboutFailedCollection(string message);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for an index query exposing the non-stale wait timeout.
public interface IIndexQuery

    // Gets the timeout used when waiting for non-stale results, or null.
    TimeSpan? WaitForNonStaleResultsTimeout { get; }
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base for index queries holding query text, parameters, tag, projection behavior and non-stale wait
// settings, with equality over query, non-stale settings and projection behavior.
public abstract class IndexQueryBase<T> : IIndexQuery, IEquatable<IndexQueryBase<T>>
{
    // Gets or sets the projection behavior for the query, or null.
    ProjectionBehavior? ProjectionBehavior { get; set; }
    /// Actual query that will be performed (Lucene syntax).
    string Query { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the query parameters of type T.
    T QueryParameters { get; set; }
    /// User-defined query tag. Sent to the server as the tag query-string parameter.
    string Tag { get; set; }
    /// When set to true> server side will wait until result are non stale or until timeout
    bool WaitForNonStaleResults { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the timeout used when waiting for non-stale results, or null.
    TimeSpan? WaitForNonStaleResultsTimeout { get; set; }
    // Returns true if the same reference, or if Query, WaitForNonStaleResultsTimeout, WaitForNonStaleResults and
    // ProjectionBehavior are equal; false for null.
    bool Equals(IndexQueryBase<T> other);
    // Returns true for the same reference, false for null or a different runtime type, otherwise compares as an
    // IndexQuery.
    bool Equals(object obj);
    // Returns a hash combining Query, WaitForNonStaleResultsTimeout and ProjectionBehavior.
    int GetHashCode();
    // Returns the Query string.
    string ToString();
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base for query results holding results, includes (documents, counters, revisions, time series,
// compare exchange), index information, staleness, etag, node tag and timings.
public abstract class QueryResultBase<TResult, TInclude>
{
    /// Gets or sets the Compare Exchange Values included in the result.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CompareExchangeValueIncludes { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets the Counters included in the result.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CounterIncludes { get; set; }
    /// The names of all the counters that the server was asked to include in the result, by document id.
    Dictionary<string, string[]> IncludedCounterNames { get; set; }
    /// The paths that the server included in the results
    string[] IncludedPaths { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets the document included in the result.
    TInclude Includes { get; set; }
    /// The index used to answer this query
    string IndexName { get; set; }
    /// The last time the index was updated. This can be used to determine the freshness of the data.
    DateTime IndexTimestamp { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets a value indicating whether the index is stale. Value: - true - if index is stale - false -
    ///    otherwise
    bool IsStale { get; set; }
    /// The timestamp of the last time the index was queried
    DateTime LastQueryTime { get; set; }
    /// Tag of a cluster node which responded to the query
    string NodeTag { get; set; }
    /// The ETag value for this index current state, which include what docs were indexed, what document were
    ///    deleted, etc.
    long ResultEtag { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets the document resulting from this query.
    TResult Results { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets the Revisions included in the result.
    BlittableJsonReaderArray RevisionIncludes { get; set; }
    /// Gets or sets the TimeSeries included in the result.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject TimeSeriesIncludes { get; set; }
    /// Detailed timings for various parts of a query (Lucene search, loading documents, transforming results) - if
    ///    requested.
    QueryTimings Timings { get; set; }
}
```
