### `Raven.Client.Documents.Session`

```csharp
/// A query against a Raven index
public abstract class AbstractDocumentQuery<T, TSelf> : IDocumentQueryCustomization, IAbstractDocumentQuery<T>
{
    // Returns the query's session cast to IAsyncDocumentSession.
    IAsyncDocumentSession AsyncSession { get; }
    // Gets the collection name passed to the query when it was constructed.
    string CollectionName { get; }
    /// Gets the document convention from the query session
    DocumentConventions Conventions { get; }
    // Gets or sets the token describing the fields to fetch (projection) for the query.
    FieldsToFetchToken FieldsToFetchToken { get; set; }
    /// The index to query
    string IndexName { get; }
    // Returns true when the first select token of the query is a distinct token.
    bool IsDistinct { get; }
    // Returns true when the query has at least one group by token.
    bool IsDynamicMapReduce { get; }
    // Gets whether the query was created as a project-into query; set from the constructor argument and false by
    // default.
    bool IsProjectInto { get; }
    // Gets or sets the prefix used when naming generated query parameters; the default is p.
    string ParameterPrefix { get; set; }
    // Gets the QueryOperation created for this query, set by the query execution code.
    QueryOperation QueryOperation { get; }
    /// Gets the session associated with this document query
    IDocumentSession Session { get; }
    // When the query has an includes alias, adds the alias to counter and time series include paths and, if the
    // given alias is null, uses the includes alias and applies it to where tokens. Returns the alias to use;
    // returns the given alias unchanged if there is no includes alias.
    string AddAliasToIncludesTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Prepends the given alias to the field names of the current filter tokens. Throws InvalidOperationException
    // if the alias is null or empty.
    void AddFromAliasToFilterTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Prepends the given alias to the field names of the current order by tokens. Throws InvalidOperationException
    // if the alias is null or empty.
    void AddFromAliasToOrderByTokens(string fromAlias);
    /// Adds an alias to the FieldName of each where token
    void AddFromAliasToWhereTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Adds a named query parameter after trimming a leading dollar sign from the name. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if a parameter with that name was already added.
    void AddParameter(string name, object value);
    // Adds the given type to the query's collection of root types.
    void AddRootType(Type type);
    // Registers a callback invoked with the QueryResult after the query executes and returns the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization AfterQueryExecuted(Action<QueryResult> action);
    // Registers a callback invoked with each streamed blittable result after a stream query executes and returns
    // the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization AfterStreamExecuted(Action<BlittableJsonReaderObject> action);
    // Adds a facet select token for the given facet to the query. Throws InvalidOperationException if the query
    // already contains a select token that is not a facet.
    void AggregateBy(FacetBase facet);
    // Adds a facet select token that refers to the facet setup document with the given id.
    void AggregateUsing(string facetSetupDocumentId);
    /// Add an AND to the query
    void AndAlso();
    /// Wraps previous query with clauses and adds an AND operator to the given query
    void AndAlso(bool wrapPreviousQueryClauses);
    // Registers a callback that receives the generated IndexQuery before it is sent, and returns the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization BeforeQueryExecuted(Action<IndexQuery> action);
    /// Specifies a boost weight to the previous where clause. The higher the boost factor, the more relevant the
    ///    term will be.
    void Boost(decimal boost);
    /// Simplified method for closing a clause within the query
    void CloseSubclause();
    // Adds a where clause requiring the field to contain all of the given values; an empty value list adds a true
    // token. Throws if the method is not supported in the current filter state.
    void ContainsAll(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values);
    // Adds a where clause matching documents whose field contains any of the given values, using an in operator.
    // Throws if the method is not supported in the current filter state.
    void ContainsAny(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid in memory where clause on IDocumentQuery, because that is nearly always
    ///    a mistake.
    int Count(Func<T, bool> predicate);
    // Adds a distinct token at the start of the select tokens. Throws InvalidOperationException if the query is
    // already distinct.
    void Distinct();
    /// Specifies a fuzziness factor to the single word term in the last where clause
    void Fuzzy(decimal fuzzy);
    // Builds the query text, generates an IndexQuery from it, invokes the before-query callback with it and
    // returns it.
    IndexQuery GetIndexQuery();
    // Converts a member expression into a query field path, removing the first path segment, adding .Length for
    // array length expressions, and mapping it through the conventions' property name lookup.
    string GetMemberQueryPath(Expression expression);
    // Returns the query path for the expression by calling GetMemberQueryPath.
    string GetMemberQueryPathForOrderBy(Expression expression);
    /// Gets the fields for projection
    IEnumerable<string> GetProjectionFields();
    // Adds group by tokens for the given field names with no grouping method, delegating to the overload that
    // takes name and method tuples.
    void GroupBy(string fieldName, string[] fieldNames);
    // Adds group by tokens for the given fields and their group by methods. Throws InvalidOperationException if
    // the query is not dynamic or is a raw query, and marks the query as group by.
    void GroupBy(ValueTuple<string, GroupByMethod> field, ValueTuple<string, GroupByMethod>[] fields);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid Linq group by clause on IDocumentQuery
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, T>> GroupBy<TKey>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid Linq group by clause on IDocumentQuery
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, TElement>> GroupBy<TKey, TElement>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, Func<T, TElement> elementSelector);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid Linq group by clause on IDocumentQuery
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, T>> GroupBy<TKey>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, IEqualityComparer<TKey> comparer);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid Linq group by clause on IDocumentQuery
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, TElement>> GroupBy<TKey, TElement>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, Func<T, TElement> elementSelector, IEqualityComparer<TKey> comparer);
    // Adds a group by count select token with the given projected name and marks the query as group by. Throws if
    // the query is a raw query.
    void GroupByCount(string projectedName);
    // Adds a group by key select token for the field and projected name, resolving group by field aliases, and
    // marks the query as group by. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void GroupByKey(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Adds a group by sum select token for the field with the given projected name and marks the query as group
    // by. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void GroupBySum(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Registers highlighting for the field with the given fragment length and count, adds a highlighting token
    // with optional options parameter, and returns the result in the out parameter.
    void Highlight(string fieldName, int fragmentLength, int fragmentCount, HighlightingOptions options, out Highlightings highlightings);
    // Adds the given document path to the set of included documents. Asserts that the session is not a non-
    // tracking session.
    void Include(string path);
    // Converts the expression to a property path using the conventions and adds it to the included documents.
    void Include(Expression<Func<T, object>> path);
    // Applies the include builder to the query, adding its documents, counters, time series, revisions and compare
    // exchange values to the includes; does nothing if the builder is null.
    void Include(IncludeBuilder includes);
    /// Explanations gives context how document was matched by query and provide information about how the score was
    ///    calculated.
    /// Throws InvalidOperationException: Explanations should be included only once in the query.
    void IncludeExplanations(ExplanationOptions options, out Explanations explanations);
    // Marks query timings as included and returns the QueryTimings object in the out parameter.
    void IncludeTimings(out QueryTimings timings);
    // Raises the session's before-query event, generates the IndexQuery and returns a new QueryOperation built
    // from it and the query's fetch and tracking settings.
    QueryOperation InitializeQueryOperation();
    // Adds an intersect marker token after the last where or close subclause token and marks the query as
    // intersect. Throws InvalidOperationException if the last token is not suitable.
    void Intersect();
    /// Called externally to raise the after query executed callback
    void InvokeAfterQueryExecuted(QueryResult result);
    /// Called externally to raise the after stream executed callback
    void InvokeAfterStreamExecuted(BlittableJsonReaderObject result);
    // Appends the operator if needed and returns a MoreLikeThisScope for the query.
    MoreLikeThisScope MoreLikeThis();
    /// Negate the next operation
    void NegateNext();
    // Sets the flag that disables caching for this query and returns the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization NoCaching();
    // Sets the flag that disables entity tracking for this query and returns the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization NoTracking();
    /// Simplified method for opening a new clause within the query
    void OpenSubclause();
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The fields are the names of the fields to sort, defaulting to
    ///    sorting by ascending. You can prefix a field name with '-' to indicate sorting by descending or '+' to
    ///    sort by ascending
    void OrderBy(string field, string sorterName);
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The fields are the names of the fields to sort, defaulting to
    ///    sorting by ascending. You can prefix a field name with '-' to indicate sorting by descending or '+' to
    ///    sort by ascending
    void OrderBy(string field, OrderingType ordering);
    // Adds an ascending order by token for the field with the given nulls ordering and ordering type. Throws if
    // the query is a raw query.
    void OrderBy(string field, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The fields are the names of the fields to sort, defaulting to
    ///    sorting by descending. You can prefix a field name with '-' to indicate sorting by descending or '+' to
    ///    sort by ascending
    void OrderByDescending(string field, string sorterName);
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The fields are the names of the fields to sort, defaulting to
    ///    sorting by descending. You can prefix a field name with '-' to indicate sorting by descending or '+' to
    ///    sort by ascending
    void OrderByDescending(string field, OrderingType ordering);
    // Adds a descending order by token for the field with the given nulls ordering and ordering type. Throws if
    // the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByDescending(string field, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude on a dynamic spatial field, with default
    // nulls ordering. Throws if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude on a dynamic spatial field, using the
    // given nulls ordering. Throws ArgumentNullException if the field is null and InvalidOperationException if the
    // query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and the latitude and longitude, with default
    // nulls ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and the latitude and longitude, using the
    // given nulls ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and coordinates with the given round factor
    // and default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token with latitude and longitude parameters, and a round factor
    // parameter when it is not zero, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the WKT shape on a dynamic spatial field, with default nulls ordering.
    // Throws if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the WKT shape on a dynamic spatial field with the given nulls ordering and
    // the field's round factor. Throws ArgumentNullException if the field is null and InvalidOperationException if
    // the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape, with default nulls ordering and
    // no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape, using the given nulls ordering
    // and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape with the given round factor and
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Adds an ascending order by distance token with a WKT shape parameter, and a round factor parameter when it
    // is not zero, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude on a dynamic spatial field, with default
    // nulls ordering. Throws if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude on a dynamic spatial field with the
    // given nulls ordering. Throws ArgumentNullException if the field is null and InvalidOperationException if the
    // query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and the latitude and longitude, with default
    // nulls ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and the latitude and longitude, using the
    // given nulls ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and coordinates with the given round factor
    // and default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token with latitude and longitude parameters, and a round factor
    // parameter when it is not zero, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance from the WKT shape on a dynamic spatial field, with default nulls ordering.
    // Throws if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders descending by distance from the WKT shape on a dynamic spatial field with the given nulls ordering
    // and the field's round factor. Throws ArgumentNullException if the field is null and
    // InvalidOperationException if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape, with default nulls ordering and
    // no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape, using the given nulls ordering
    // and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token for the named field and WKT shape with the given round factor and
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Adds a descending order by distance token with a WKT shape parameter, and a round factor parameter when it
    // is not zero, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending order by score token. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByScore();
    // Adds a descending order by score token. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByScoreDescending();
    /// Add an OR to the query
    void OrElse();
    // Adds the value as a query parameter and returns its name prefixed with a dollar sign.
    string ProjectionParameter(object value);
    /// Specifies a proximity distance for the phrase in the last search clause
    void Proximity(int proximity);
    /// Order the search results randomly
    void RandomOrdering();
    /// Order the search results randomly using the specified seed this is useful if you want to have repeatable
    ///    random queries
    void RandomOrdering(string seed);
    // Sets the query to the given raw query text. Throws InvalidOperationException if any select, where, order by,
    // group by or filter tokens were already added.
    void RawQuery(string query);
    /// Perform a search for documents which fields that match the searchTerms. If there is more than a single term,
    ///    each of them will be checked independently.
    void Search(string fieldName, string searchTerms, SearchOperator operator);
    /// Skips the specified count.
    void Skip(long count);
    // Adds the spatial criteria as a where token on the dynamic spatial field. Throws InvalidOperationException if
    // the query is not dynamic.
    void Spatial(DynamicSpatialField dynamicField, SpatialCriteria criteria);
    // Adds the spatial criteria as a where token on the named field after validating the field name.
    void Spatial(string fieldName, SpatialCriteria criteria);
    /// Provide statistics about the query, such as total count of matching records
    void Statistics(out QueryStatistics stats);
    // Adds a suggest select token for the suggestion term or terms. Throws ArgumentNullException if null,
    // NotSupportedException for an unknown suggestion type, and InvalidOperationException if where or order by
    // tokens exist, a non-suggest select token exists, or the last suggest is for the same field.
    void SuggestUsing(SuggestionBase suggestion);
    /// Takes the specified count.
    void Take(long count);
    // Marks query timings as included, returns the QueryTimings in the out parameter, and returns the query.
    IDocumentQueryCustomization Timings(out QueryTimings timings);
    // Returns the query text built with compatibility mode set to false.
    string ToString();
    // Sets the default operator of the query. Throws InvalidOperationException if a where clause was already
    // added.
    void UsingDefaultOperator(QueryOperator operator);
    // Reads field and value settings from the accessor interfaces and adds a vector search where token with the
    // text, texts, embeddings or document id value as a query parameter. Throws InvalidOperationException if no
    // value is supplied.
    void VectorSearch(IVectorEmbeddingFieldFactoryAccessor fieldFactoryAccessor, IVectorFieldValueFactoryAccessor fieldValueFactoryAccessor, float? minimumSimilarity, int? numberOfCandidates, bool isExact);
    // Casts the field and value factories to their concrete types and adds a vector search where token with the
    // given similarity, candidates count and exact flag.
    void VectorSearch(IVectorFieldFactory<T> fieldFactory, IVectorFieldValueFactory valueFactory, float? minimumSimilarity, int? numberOfCandidates, bool isExact);
    // Enables waiting for non-stale results; if already enabled, extends the stored timeout when the given timeout
    // is larger or none is set, otherwise uses the given timeout or the conventions default.
    void WaitForNonStaleResults(TimeSpan? waitTimeout);
    /// This function exists solely to forbid in memory where clause on IDocumentQuery, because that is nearly always
    ///    a mistake.
    IEnumerable<T> Where(Func<T, bool> predicate);
    /// Matches fields where the value is between the specified start and end, inclusive
    void WhereBetween(string fieldName, object start, object end, bool exact);
    // Adds an ends-with where token for the field and value, with exact matching set to false.
    void WhereEndsWith(string fieldName, object value);
    // Adds an ends-with where token for the field and value, using the exact flag for the where options.
    void WhereEndsWith(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches value
    void WhereEquals(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches value
    void WhereEquals(string fieldName, MethodCall method, bool exact);
    /// Matches value
    void WhereEquals(WhereParams whereParams);
    // Adds an exists where token for the field after validating the field name.
    void WhereExists(string fieldName);
    /// Matches fields where the value is greater than the specified value
    void WhereGreaterThan(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is greater than or equal to the specified value
    void WhereGreaterThanOrEqual(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Check that the field has one of the specified value
    void WhereIn(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is less than the specified value
    void WhereLessThan(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is less than or equal to the specified value
    void WhereLessThanOrEqual(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Filter the results from the index using the specified where clause.
    void WhereLucene(string fieldName, string whereClause, bool exact);
    /// Not matches value
    void WhereNotEquals(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Not matches value
    void WhereNotEquals(string fieldName, MethodCall method, bool exact);
    /// Not matches value
    void WhereNotEquals(WhereParams whereParams);
    /// Matches fields where Regex.IsMatch(filedName, pattern)
    void WhereRegex(string fieldName, string pattern);
    // Adds a starts-with where token for the field and value, with exact matching set to false.
    void WhereStartsWith(string fieldName, object value);
    // Adds a starts-with where token for the field and value, using the exact flag for the where options.
    void WhereStartsWith(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    // Adds a true token to the current where tokens, appending an operator and applying negation if needed.
    void WhereTrue();
    /// Sets a user-defined tag on the query request.
    void WithTag(string tag);
}
```

```csharp
// Group by query wrapper over an AsyncDocumentQuery that forwards select and filter calls to the wrapped
// query.

{
    // Creates a wrapper that stores the given AsyncDocumentQuery.
    AsyncGroupByDocumentQuery(AsyncDocumentQuery<T> query);
    // Applies the filter builder to the wrapped query in filter mode with the given limit and returns this group
    // by query.
    IAsyncGroupByDocumentQuery<T> Filter(Action<IFilterFactory<T>> builder, int limit);
    // Adds a group by count select token with the projected name to the wrapped query and returns that query.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> SelectCount(string projectedName);
    // Adds a group by key select token for the field and projected name to the wrapped query and returns this
    // group by query.
    IAsyncGroupByDocumentQuery<T> SelectKey(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Adds a group by sum select token for each given field to the wrapped query and returns that query. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException if the first field is null.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> SelectSum(GroupByField field, GroupByField[] fields);
}
```

```csharp
// Session implementation for reading counters of a document asynchronously, using a per-session counters
// cache.
public sealed class AsyncSessionDocumentCounters : SessionCountersBase, IAsyncSessionDocumentCounters
{
    // Creates the counters accessor for the document id. Throws ArgumentNullException if the id is null or
    // whitespace.
    AsyncSessionDocumentCounters(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, string documentId);
    // Creates the counters accessor for the tracked entity, resolving its document id from the session; throws if
    // the entity is not in the session.
    AsyncSessionDocumentCounters(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, object entity);
    // Returns all counter values of the document, using the session cache and sending a GetCountersOperation to
    // the server only when counters are missing from the cache.
    Task<Dictionary<string, long?>> GetAllAsync(CancellationToken token);
    // Returns the value of one counter, from the session cache when present, otherwise by sending a
    // GetCountersOperation; returns null if the counter is not found.
    Task<long?> GetAsync(string counter, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns a dictionary of values for the given counters, using the session cache and metadata where possible,
    // otherwise sending a GetCountersOperation for them.
    Task<Dictionary<string, long?>> GetAsync(IEnumerable<string> counters, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Session implementation for reading and appending entries of a time series of a document, with typed values.
public sealed class AsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries<TValues> : SessionTimeSeriesBase, IAsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries, IAsyncSessionDocumentIncrementalTimeSeries, IAsyncSessionDocumentRollupTypedTimeSeries<TValues>, IAsyncSessionDocumentTypedTimeSeries<TValues>, IAsyncSessionDocumentTypedIncrementalTimeSeries<TValues>
{
    // Creates the time series accessor for the document id and time series name.
    AsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, string documentId, string name);

    // sending a GetTimeSeriesOperation to the server; includes are applied to the request.

}
```

```csharp
// Base class that tracks compare exchange values and includes in a session and prepares their commands. Its
// constructor throws if the session is not in ClusterWide transaction mode.
public abstract class ClusterTransactionOperationsBase : IClusterTransactionOperationsBase
{
    // Clears the tracked compare exchange values, the compare exchange includes and the missing atomic guard map.
    void Clear();
    // Registers a new compare exchange value for the key with the item in the session and returns it. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException if the key is null.
    CompareExchangeValue<T> CreateCompareExchangeValue<T>(string key, T item);
    // Registers deletion of the compare exchange value using its key and index. Throws ArgumentNullException if
    // the item is null.
    void DeleteCompareExchangeValue<T>(CompareExchangeValue<T> item);
    // Registers deletion of the compare exchange value with the given key and index. Throws ArgumentNullException
    // if the key is null.
    void DeleteCompareExchangeValue(string key, long index);
}
```

```csharp
/// Abstract implementation for in memory session operations
public abstract class DocumentSessionAttachmentsBase : AdvancedSessionExtensionBase
{
    /// Copies specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    SaveChanges is called
    void Copy(object sourceEntity, string sourceName, object destinationEntity, string destinationName);
    /// Copies specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    SaveChanges is called
    void Copy(string sourceDocumentId, string sourceName, string destinationDocumentId, string destinationName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for deletion. The attachment will be deleted when SaveChanges is
    ///    called
    void Delete(object entity, string name);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for deletion. The attachment will be deleted when SaveChanges is
    ///    called
    void Delete(string documentId, string name);
    /// Returns the attachments info of a document
    AttachmentName[] GetNames(object entity);
    /// Moves specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    SaveChanges is called
    void Move(object sourceEntity, string sourceName, object destinationEntity, string destinationName);
    /// Moves specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    SaveChanges is called
    void Move(string sourceDocumentId, string sourceName, string destinationDocumentId, string destinationName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for rename. The attachment will be renamed when SaveChanges is
    ///    called
    void Rename(string documentId, string name, string newName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for rename. The attachment will be renamed when SaveChanges is
    ///    called
    void Rename(object entity, string name, string newName);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session
    void Store(string documentId, string name, Stream stream, string contentType);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session
    void Store(object entity, string name, Stream stream, string contentType);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session using the provided parameters
    void Store(string documentId, StoreAttachmentParameters parameters);
}
```

```csharp
/// Implements Unit of Work for accessing the RavenDB server
public sealed class DocumentSessionRevisions : DocumentSessionRevisionsBase, IRevisionsSessionOperations, ILazyRevisionsOperations
{
    // Creates the revisions operations object for the given session by passing it to the base constructor.
    DocumentSessionRevisions(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session);
    // Returns this instance as the lazy revisions operations interface.
    ILazyRevisionsOperations Lazily { get; }
    // Loads the revision with the given change vector through a GetRevisionOperation, sending a request only when
    // the operation needs one, and returns it as T.
    T Get<T>(string changeVector);
    // Loads the revisions with the given change vectors through a GetRevisionOperation and returns them as a
    // dictionary keyed by change vector.
    Dictionary<string, T> Get<T>(IEnumerable<string> changeVectors);
    // Loads the revision of the document with the given id for the given date through a GetRevisionOperation and
    // returns the first revision found, or the default value.
    T Get<T>(string id, DateTime date);
    // Sends a GetRevisionsCountOperation request for the document id and returns the number of revisions.
    long GetCountFor(string id);
    // Returns a list of the document's revisions as T, skipping start entries and returning up to pageSize entries
    // (defaults 0 and 25).
    List<T> GetFor<T>(string id, int start, int pageSize);
    // Returns a list of metadata dictionaries of the document's revisions, skipping start entries and returning up
    // to pageSize entries (defaults 0 and 25).
    List<IMetadataDictionary> GetMetadataFor(string id, int start, int pageSize);
}
```

```csharp
/// Implements Unit of Work for accessing the RavenDB server
public sealed class DocumentSessionRevisionsAsync : DocumentSessionRevisionsBase, IRevisionsSessionOperationsAsync, ILazyRevisionsOperationsAsync
{
    // Creates the async revisions operations object for the given async session by passing it to the base
    // constructor.
    DocumentSessionRevisionsAsync(AsyncDocumentSession session);
    // Returns this instance as the lazy async revisions operations interface.
    ILazyRevisionsOperationsAsync Lazily { get; }
    // Asynchronously loads the revision with the given change vector through a GetRevisionOperation, sending a
    // request only when needed, and returns it as T.
    Task<T> GetAsync<T>(string changeVector, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously loads the revisions with the given change vectors and returns them as a dictionary keyed by
    // change vector.
    Task<Dictionary<string, T>> GetAsync<T>(IEnumerable<string> changeVectors, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously loads the revision of the document with the given id for the given date and returns the first
    // revision found, or the default value.
    Task<T> GetAsync<T>(string id, DateTime date, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously sends a GetRevisionsCountOperation request for the document id and returns the number of
    // revisions.
    Task<long> GetCountForAsync(string id, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously returns a list of the document's revisions as T, skipping start entries and returning up to
    // pageSize entries (defaults 0 and 25).
    Task<List<T>> GetForAsync<T>(string id, int start, int pageSize, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously returns a list of metadata dictionaries of the document's revisions, skipping start entries
    // and returning up to pageSize entries (defaults 0 and 25).
    Task<List<IMetadataDictionary>> GetMetadataForAsync(string id, int start, int pageSize, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Wraps a DocumentQuery and provides the group-by operations SelectKey, SelectSum, SelectCount and Filter,
// each delegating to the wrapped query.
public sealed class GroupByDocumentQuery<T> : IGroupByDocumentQuery<T>
{
    // Stores the given DocumentQuery as the wrapped query.
    GroupByDocumentQuery(DocumentQuery<T> query);
    // Invokes the builder with a FilterFactory created for the query and the given limit, with filter mode enabled
    // on the query, and returns this instance.
    IGroupByDocumentQuery<T> Filter(Action<IFilterFactory<T>> builder, int limit);
    // Adds a group-by count select token with the optional projected name to the wrapped query and returns that
    // query.
    IDocumentQuery<T> SelectCount(string projectedName);
    // Adds a group-by key select token with the optional field name and projected name to the wrapped query and
    // returns this instance.
    IGroupByDocumentQuery<T> SelectKey(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Throws ArgumentNullException if field is null. Adds a group-by sum for the field and for each additional
    // field to the wrapped query and returns that query.
    IDocumentQuery<T> SelectSum(GroupByField field, GroupByField[] fields);
}
```

```csharp
/// Mostly used by the linq provider
public interface IAbstractDocumentQuery<T>
{
    /// Get the queried collection name.
    string CollectionName { get; }
    /// Get the document conventions used for this query.
    DocumentConventions Conventions { get; }
    /// Get the queried index name.
    string IndexName { get; }
    /// Determines if query is a dynamic map-reduce query.
    bool IsDynamicMapReduce { get; }
    // Gets or sets the prefix used to name the query parameters. The default value is the string p.
    string ParameterPrefix { get; set; }
    // Returns fromAlias unchanged when the query has no includes alias. Otherwise, uses the includes alias when
    // fromAlias is null, adds it to where tokens, and to the paths of counter and time series include tokens;
    // returns the alias.
    string AddAliasToIncludesTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Adds the alias to the field name of each where token in the current filter tokens. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the alias is null or empty.
    void AddFromAliasToFilterTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Adds the alias to the field name of each order-by token in the current order-by tokens. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the alias is null or empty.
    void AddFromAliasToOrderByTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Adds the alias to the field name of each where token in the current where tokens. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the alias is null or empty.
    void AddFromAliasToWhereTokens(string fromAlias);
    // Adds the given type to the query's collection of root types.
    void AddRootType(Type type);
    // Appends a facet token for the given facet to the select tokens. Throws InvalidOperationException if the
    // select tokens already contain a token that is not a facet token.
    void AggregateBy(FacetBase facet);
    // Appends a facet token that refers to the facet setup document with the given id to the select tokens.
    void AggregateUsing(string facetSetupDocumentId);
    /// Add an AND to the query
    void AndAlso();
    /// Specifies a boost weight to the previous where clause. The higher the boost factor, the more relevant the
    ///    term will be.
    void Boost(decimal boost);
    /// Simplified method for closing a clause within the query
    void CloseSubclause();
    /// Performs a query matching ALL of the provided values against the given field (AND)
    void ContainsAll(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values);
    /// Performs a query matching ANY of the provided values against the given field (OR)
    void ContainsAny(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values);
    // Adds a distinct token at the start of the select tokens. Throws InvalidOperationException if the query is
    // already distinct.
    void Distinct();
    /// Specifies a fuzziness factor to the single word term in the last where clause
    void Fuzzy(decimal fuzzy);
    /// Gets field names of query result projection.
    IEnumerable<string> GetProjectionFields();
    // Groups the query by the given field names, using GroupByMethod.None for each, by calling the tuple-based
    // GroupBy overload.
    void GroupBy(string fieldName, string[] fieldNames);
    // Adds a group-by token for each given field name and method, and marks the query as group by. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the query is not dynamic or is a raw query.
    void GroupBy(ValueTuple<string, GroupByMethod> field, ValueTuple<string, GroupByMethod>[] fields);
    // Adds a group-by count select token with the optional projected name and marks the query as group by. Throws
    // if the query is a raw query.
    void GroupByCount(string projectedName);
    // Adds a group-by key select token with the field name and projected name, resolving a name that matches a
    // group-by alias, and marks the query as group by. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void GroupByKey(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Adds a group-by sum select token for the validated field name and projected name and marks the query as
    // group by. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void GroupBySum(string fieldName, string projectedName);
    // Registers highlighting for the field, adds a highlighting token with the fragment length, fragment count and
    // an options query parameter when options is not null, and returns the Highlightings through the out
    // parameter.
    void Highlight(string fieldName, int fragmentLength, int fragmentCount, HighlightingOptions options, out Highlightings highlightings);
    // Adds the given path to the set of included document ids of the query, after asserting that includes are
    // allowed in the session.
    void Include(string path);
    // Converts the expression to a property path using the conventions and adds it as a document include.
    void Include(Expression<Func<T, object>> path);
    // Does nothing if includes is null. Otherwise adds the builder's document, counter, time series, revision and
    // compare exchange value includes to the query.
    void Include(IncludeBuilder includes);
    // Marks the query as intersect and adds an intersect marker token if the last where token is a where token or
    // a close-subclause token. Otherwise throws InvalidOperationException.
    void Intersect();
    // Appends an operator if needed, adds a MoreLikeThisToken to the where tokens and returns a MoreLikeThisScope
    // for it.
    MoreLikeThisScope MoreLikeThis();
    /// Negate the next operation
    void NegateNext();
    /// Simplified method for opening a new clause within the query
    void OpenSubclause();
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The field is the name of the field to sort, defaulting to sorting
    ///    by ascending.
    void OrderBy(string field, string sorterName);
    /// Order the results by the specified fields The field is the name of the field to sort, defaulting to sorting
    ///    by ascending.
    void OrderBy(string field, OrderingType ordering);
    // Adds an ascending order-by token for the validated field with the given nulls ordering and ordering type.
    // Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderBy(string field, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Adds a descending order-by token for the field using the named sorter. Throws ArgumentException if
    // sorterName is null or whitespace, and throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByDescending(string field, string sorterName);
    // Adds a descending order-by token for the field with the given ordering type and default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDescending(string field, OrderingType ordering);
    // Adds a descending order-by token for the validated field with the given nulls ordering and ordering type.
    // Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByDescending(string field, NullsOrdering nulls, OrderingType ordering);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude using the dynamic spatial field, with
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given latitude and longitude using the dynamic spatial field and its
    // round factor. Throws ArgumentNullException if field is null and InvalidOperationException if the query is
    // not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given WKT shape using the dynamic spatial field, with default nulls
    // ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by distance from the given WKT shape using the dynamic spatial field and its round factor.
    // Throws ArgumentNullException if field is null and InvalidOperationException if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistance(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude, with no round factor
    // and default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude, with the given nulls
    // ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude using the round
    // factor, with default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Adds an ascending distance order-by token for the field, passing latitude, longitude and the round factor
    // (when not zero) as query parameters, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape, with no round factor and default
    // nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape, with the given nulls ordering and
    // no round factor.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders ascending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape using the round factor, with
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Adds an ascending distance order-by token for the field, passing the WKT shape and the round factor (when
    // not zero) as query parameters, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistance(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude using the dynamic spatial field, with
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given latitude and longitude using the dynamic spatial field and its
    // round factor. Throws ArgumentNullException if field is null and InvalidOperationException if the query is
    // not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given WKT shape using the dynamic spatial field, with default nulls
    // ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders descending by distance from the given WKT shape using the dynamic spatial field and its round factor.
    // Throws ArgumentNullException if field is null and InvalidOperationException if the query is not dynamic.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(DynamicSpatialField field, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude, with no round factor
    // and default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude, with the given nulls
    // ordering and no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given latitude and longitude using the round
    // factor, with default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor);
    // Adds a descending distance order-by token for the field, passing latitude, longitude and the round factor
    // (when not zero) as query parameters, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, double latitude, double longitude, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape, with no round factor and default
    // nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape, with the given nulls ordering and
    // no round factor.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Orders descending by distance of the named field from the given WKT shape using the round factor, with
    // default nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor);
    // Adds a descending distance order-by token for the field, passing the WKT shape and the round factor (when
    // not zero) as query parameters, with the given nulls ordering.
    void OrderByDistanceDescending(string fieldName, string shapeWkt, double roundFactor, NullsOrdering nulls);
    // Adds an ascending score order-by token to the query. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByScore();
    // Adds a descending score order-by token to the query. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void OrderByScoreDescending();
    /// Add an OR to the query
    void OrElse();
    // Adds the value as a query parameter and returns its parameter name prefixed with a dollar sign.
    string ProjectionParameter(object value);
    /// Specifies a proximity distance for the phrase in the last where clause
    void Proximity(int proximity);
    // Disables caching for the query and adds a random order-by token. Throws if the query is a raw query.
    void RandomOrdering();
    // Calls RandomOrdering without a seed when seed is null or whitespace. Otherwise disables caching and adds a
    // random order-by token created with the seed.
    void RandomOrdering(string seed);
    /// Perform a search for documents which fields that match the searchTerms. If there is more than a single term,
    ///    each of them will be checked independently.
    void Search(string fieldName, string searchTerms, SearchOperator operator);
    /// Skips the specified number of query results.
    void Skip(long count);
    // Adds the where token produced by the criteria for the dynamic spatial field, appending an operator and
    // negation as needed. Throws InvalidOperationException if the query is not dynamic.
    void Spatial(DynamicSpatialField field, SpatialCriteria criteria);
    // Adds the where token produced by the criteria for the validated field name, appending an operator and
    // negation as needed.
    void Spatial(string fieldName, SpatialCriteria criteria);
    // Adds a suggest select token for the suggestion's term or terms, with an options parameter when options are
    // not default. Throws ArgumentNullException for null and InvalidOperationException if where, order-by or other
    // select tokens exist.
    void SuggestUsing(SuggestionBase suggestion);
    /// Takes the specified number of query results.
    void Take(long count);
    /// Returns a 'String' that represents this instance.
    string ToString();
    // Adds a vector search where token for the field named by the factory, using text, texts, embeddings or a
    // document id from the value factory as a query parameter. Throws InvalidOperationException if none is
    // provided.
    void VectorSearch(IVectorFieldFactory<T> textFieldFactory, IVectorFieldValueFactory textValueFactory, float? minimumSimilarity, int? numberOfCandidates, bool isExact);
    /// Instruct the query to wait for non-stale results. This shouldn't be used outside of unit tests unless you are
    ///    well aware of the implications.
    void WaitForNonStaleResults(TimeSpan? waitTimeout);
    /// Matches fields where the value is between the specified start and end, inclusive
    void WhereBetween(string fieldName, object start, object end, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields which ends with the specified value.
    void WhereEndsWith(string fieldName, object value);
    /// Matches fields which ends with the specified value.
    void WhereEndsWith(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches documents with value in specified field equal to value provided in parameter.
    void WhereEquals(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches evaluated method
    void WhereEquals(string fieldName, MethodCall method, bool exact);
    /// Matches value
    void WhereEquals(WhereParams whereParams);
    // Adds an Exists where token for the validated field name to the current where tokens, appending an operator
    // and negation as needed.
    void WhereExists(string fieldName);
    /// Matches fields where the value is greater than the specified value
    void WhereGreaterThan(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is greater than or equal to the specified value
    void WhereGreaterThanOrEqual(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Check that the field has one of the specified value
    void WhereIn(string fieldName, IEnumerable<object> values, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is less than the specified value
    void WhereLessThan(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Matches fields where the value is less than or equal to the specified value
    void WhereLessThanOrEqual(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Not matches value
    void WhereNotEquals(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    /// Not matches evaluated method
    void WhereNotEquals(string fieldName, MethodCall method, bool exact);
    /// Not matches value
    void WhereNotEquals(WhereParams whereParams);
    /// Matches fields where Regex.IsMatch(filed, pattern)
    void WhereRegex(string fieldName, string pattern);
    /// Matches fields which starts with the specified value.
    void WhereStartsWith(string fieldName, object value);
    /// Matches fields which starts with the specified value.
    void WhereStartsWith(string fieldName, object value, bool exact);
    // Adds a true token to the current where tokens, appending an operator and negation as needed.
    void WhereTrue();
}
```

```csharp
/// This is used as an abstraction for the implementation of a query when passing to other parts of the query
///    infrastructure. Meant to be internal only, making this public to allow mocking / instrumentation.
public interface IAbstractDocumentQueryImpl<T>
{
    // Gets or sets the token that holds the fields to fetch for the query projection.
    FieldsToFetchToken FieldsToFetchToken { get; set; }
    // Gets whether the query was created as a project-into query.
    bool IsProjectInto { get; }
    // Raises the session's before-query event, builds the index query, and returns a new QueryOperation using the
    // index name, fields to fetch token, tracking setting and project-into flag.
    QueryOperation InitializeQueryOperation();
}
```

```csharp
// Interface of the async session streaming operations. It declares StreamAsync overloads for queries and id
// prefixes, and StreamIntoAsync overloads that write query results to a stream.
public interface IAsyncAdvancedDocumentsSessionOperations
{
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of an async document query and returns an async enumerator
    // of StreamResult of T. The entities are not tracked by the session.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> query, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of an async document query, returning the
    // StreamQueryStatistics through the out parameter, and returns an async enumerator of StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of an async raw document query and returns an async
    // enumerator of StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<T> query, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of an async raw document query, returning the
    // StreamQueryStatistics through the out parameter, and returns an async enumerator of StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of a LINQ query and returns an async enumerator of
    // StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> query, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over the results of a LINQ query, returning the StreamQueryStatistics
    // through the out parameter, and returns an async enumerator of StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(IQueryable<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously creates a stream over documents whose ids start with the prefix, optionally filtered by a
    // matches pattern, with start, pageSize and startAfter arguments, and returns an async enumerator of
    // StreamResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<StreamResult<T>>> StreamAsync<T>(string startsWith, string matches, int start, int pageSize, string startAfter, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously writes the results of an async document query into the given output stream.
    Task StreamIntoAsync<T>(IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> query, Stream output, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously writes the results of an async raw document query into the given output stream.
    Task StreamIntoAsync<T>(IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<T> query, Stream output, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface declaring async query operations such as ToListAsync, ToArrayAsync, FirstAsync, SingleAsync,
// AnyAsync, CountAsync and their lazy variants, each taking a cancellation token.
public interface IAsyncDocumentQueryBase<T>
{
    /// Checks if query returns any results.
    Task<bool> AnyAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Gets the total count of query results.
    Task<int> CountAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Registers the lazy-count query and returns its lazy instance that will be evaluated on request.
    Lazy<Task<int>> CountLazilyAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Returns first result of the query.
    /// Throws InvalidOperationException: Results are empty.
    Task<T> FirstAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Returns first result of the query, or default value for queried type if there are none.
    Task<T> FirstOrDefaultAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Registers the lazy query and returns its lazy instance that will be evaluated on request.
    Lazy<Task<IEnumerable<T>>> LazilyAsync(Action<IEnumerable<T>> onEval);
    /// Gets the total count of query results as int64.
    Task<long> LongCountAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Registers the lazy-count query and returns its lazy instance that will be evaluated on request.
    Lazy<Task<long>> LongCountLazilyAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Returns single result of the query.
    /// Throws InvalidOperationException: There is not exactly one result.
    Task<T> SingleAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Returns single result of the query, or default value for given type if sequence is empty. Throws if there is
    ///    more than one result.
    /// Throws InvalidOperationException: There is more than a single result.
    Task<T> SingleOrDefaultAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Executes the query and returns the results as an array.
    Task<T[]> ToArrayAsync(CancellationToken token);
    /// Executes the query and returns the results as a list.
    Task<List<T>> ToListAsync(CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
/// It gives the ability to construct queries with the usage of 'IAsyncDocumentQuery`1' interface
public interface IAsyncDocumentQueryBuilder
{
    // Creates an async document query for T using the index of the index creator type TIndexCreator, taking its
    // index name and map-reduce flag from the cached index metadata.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> AsyncDocumentQuery<T, TIndexCreator>();
    // Creates an async document query for T over the given index or collection, resolving the names from the type
    // and conventions, and passes isMapReduce as the group-by flag.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> AsyncDocumentQuery<T>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for async operations on the counters of a document in a session, with methods to get all counters
// or specific counters by name.
public interface IAsyncSessionDocumentCounters : ISessionDocumentCountersBase
{
    // Asynchronously returns a dictionary of the document's counter names to their values, using the session's
    // counter cache and requesting missing values from the server.
    Task<Dictionary<string, long?>> GetAllAsync(CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously returns the nullable value of the named counter, using the session's counter cache and
    // requesting it from the server when it is not known.
    Task<long?> GetAsync(string counter, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously returns a dictionary of the given counter names to their nullable values, using the session's
    // counter cache and requesting unknown values from the server.
    Task<Dictionary<string, long?>> GetAsync(IEnumerable<string> counters, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
    // through the out parameter.

    // Asynchronously streams the results of an async document query of time series aggregation results and returns
    // an async enumerator of TimeSeriesStreamResult of TimeSeriesAggregationResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<TimeSeriesStreamResult<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncDocumentQuery<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>> query, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously streams the results of an async raw document query of time series aggregation results and
    // returns an async enumerator of TimeSeriesStreamResult of TimeSeriesAggregationResult of T.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<TimeSeriesStreamResult<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>> query, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously streams the results of an async raw document query of time series aggregation results and
    // returns an async enumerator of TimeSeriesStreamResult of TimeSeriesAggregationResult of T, returning
    // StreamQueryStatistics through the out parameter.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<TimeSeriesStreamResult<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously streams the results of an async document query of time series aggregation results and returns
    // an async enumerator of TimeSeriesStreamResult of TimeSeriesAggregationResult of T, returning
    // StreamQueryStatistics through the out parameter.
    Task<IAsyncEnumerator<TimeSeriesStreamResult<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>>>> StreamAsync<T>(IAsyncDocumentQuery<TimeSeriesAggregationResult<T>> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface that declares session operations for working with document attachments, such as getting attachment
// names.
public interface IAttachmentsSessionOperationsBase
{
    /// Copies specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    'SaveChanges' is called.
    void Copy(object sourceEntity, string sourceName, object destinationEntity, string destinationName);
    /// Copies specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    'SaveChanges' is called.
    void Copy(string sourceDocumentId, string sourceName, string destinationDocumentId, string destinationName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for deletion. The attachment will be deleted when 'SaveChanges' is
    ///    called.
    void Delete(string documentId, string name);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for deletion. The attachment will be deleted when 'SaveChanges' is
    ///    called.
    void Delete(object entity, string name);
    /// Returns the attachments info of a document.
    AttachmentName[] GetNames(object entity);
    /// Moves specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    'SaveChanges' is called.
    void Move(object sourceEntity, string sourceName, object destinationEntity, string destinationName);
    /// Moves specified source document attachment to destination document. The operation will be executed when
    ///    'SaveChanges' is called.
    void Move(string sourceDocumentId, string sourceName, string destinationDocumentId, string destinationName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for rename. The attachment will be renamed when 'SaveChanges' is
    ///    called.
    void Rename(object entity, string name, string newName);
    /// Marks the specified document's attachment for rename. The attachment will be renamed when 'SaveChanges' is
    ///    called.
    void Rename(string documentId, string name, string newName);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session.
    void Store(string documentId, StoreAttachmentParameters parameters);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session.
    void Store(string documentId, string name, Stream stream, string contentType);
    /// Stores attachment to be sent in the session.
    void Store(object entity, string name, Stream stream, string contentType);
}
```

```csharp
/// It gives the ability to construct queries with the usage of 'IDocumentQuery`1' interface
public interface IDocumentQueryBuilder
{
    /// It provides low-level querying capabilities with the usage of 'TIndexCreator' index. It gives a lot of
    ///    flexibility and control over the process of building a query.
    IDocumentQuery<T> DocumentQuery<T, TIndexCreator>();
    /// It provides low-level querying capabilities with the usage of an index or directly querying documents from a
    ///    specified collection (note: it might require an auto index to be created). It gives a lot of flexibility
    ///    and control over the process of building a query.
    IDocumentQuery<T> DocumentQuery<T>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface that declares the session operations for streaming documents that match a query, including
// streaming raw query output into a Stream.
public interface IDocumentsSessionOperations
{
    /// Creates a stream to fetch documents that satisfy criteria 'query'. Documents are converted to CLR type 'T'
    ///    along the way and wrapped in 'StreamResult`1' containing document metadata.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IQueryable<T> query);
    // Executes the query on the server as a stream and returns an enumerator of StreamResult of T, each wrapping a
    // document with its metadata, and outputs a StreamQueryStatistics object.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IQueryable<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats);
    // Executes the query on the server as a stream and returns an enumerator of StreamResult of T, each wrapping a
    // document with its metadata.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IDocumentQuery<T> query);
    // Executes the query on the server as a stream and returns an enumerator of StreamResult of T, each wrapping a
    // document with its metadata.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IRawDocumentQuery<T> query);
    // Executes the query on the server as a stream and returns an enumerator of StreamResult of T, each wrapping a
    // document with its metadata, and outputs a StreamQueryStatistics object.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IRawDocumentQuery<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats);
    // Executes the query on the server as a stream and returns an enumerator of StreamResult of T, each wrapping a
    // document with its metadata, and outputs a StreamQueryStatistics object.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(IDocumentQuery<T> query, out StreamQueryStatistics streamQueryStats);
    /// Creates a stream to fetch documents whose IDs match the given parameters. Documents are converted to CLR type
    ///    'T' along the way and wrapped in 'StreamResult`1' containing document metadata.
    IEnumerator<StreamResult<T>> Stream<T>(string startsWith, string matches, int start, int pageSize, string startAfter);
    /// Feeds the query results into given stream 'output'.
    void StreamInto<T>(IDocumentQuery<T> query, Stream output);
    // Executes the raw document query on the server and copies the response stream to the supplied output stream.
    void StreamInto<T>(IRawDocumentQuery<T> query, Stream output);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface that declares GroupBy methods on a document query.
public interface IGroupByDocumentQueryBase<T, TSelf>
{
    // Groups the query results using the key selector. The method is marked obsolete with the error flag, advising
    // to use session.Query with Linq grouping instead.
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, T>> GroupBy<TKey>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector);
    // Groups the query results using the key and element selectors. The method is marked obsolete with the error
    // flag, advising to use session.Query with Linq grouping instead.
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, TElement>> GroupBy<TKey, TElement>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, Func<T, TElement> elementSelector);
    // Groups the query results using the key selector and an equality comparer. The method is marked obsolete with
    // the error flag, advising to use session.Query with Linq grouping instead.
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, T>> GroupBy<TKey>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, IEqualityComparer<TKey> comparer);
    // Groups the query results using the key selector, element selector and an equality comparer. The method is
    // marked obsolete with the error flag, advising to use session.Query with Linq grouping instead.
    IEnumerable<IGrouping<TKey, TElement>> GroupBy<TKey, TElement>(Func<T, TKey> keySelector, Func<T, TElement> elementSelector, IEqualityComparer<TKey> comparer);
}
```

```csharp
// Synchronous session counters implementation that wraps an AsyncSessionDocumentCounters instance and forwards
// each call to it.
public sealed class SessionDocumentCounters : ISessionDocumentCounters
{
    // Creates the counters object for the document with the given id, backed by an AsyncSessionDocumentCounters
    // for that session and id.
    SessionDocumentCounters(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, string documentId);
    // Creates the counters object for the given entity, backed by an AsyncSessionDocumentCounters for that session
    // and entity.
    SessionDocumentCounters(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, object entity);
    // Forwards to the async counters object to queue a delete of the named counter. Throws ArgumentNullException
    // if the name is null or whitespace.
    void Delete(string counter);
    // Synchronously gets the value of the named counter, returned as null when the counter does not exist, by
    // forwarding to the async counters object.
    long? Get(string counter);
    // Synchronously gets the values of the named counters as a dictionary from counter name to nullable value, by
    // forwarding to the async counters object.
    Dictionary<string, long?> Get(IEnumerable<string> counters);
    // Synchronously gets all counters of the document as a dictionary from counter name to value, by forwarding to
    // the async counters object.
    Dictionary<string, long?> GetAll();
    // Forwards to the async counters object to queue an increment of the named counter by delta, which defaults to
    // 1.
    void Increment(string counter, long delta);
}
```

```csharp
// Sealed session time series class that implements the typed, rollup and incremental time series interfaces by
// forwarding to an AsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries.
public sealed class SessionDocumentTimeSeries<TValues> : ISessionDocumentTimeSeries, ISessionDocumentRollupTypedTimeSeries<TValues>, ISessionDocumentTypedTimeSeries<TValues>, ISessionDocumentIncrementalTimeSeries, ISessionDocumentTypedIncrementalTimeSeries<TValues>
{
    // Creates the time series object for the document id and time series name, backed by an
    // AsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries.
    SessionDocumentTimeSeries(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, string documentId, string name);
    // Creates the time series object for the given entity and time series name, backed by an
    // AsyncSessionDocumentTimeSeries.
    SessionDocumentTimeSeries(InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, object entity, string name);
    // Sets the values of the rollup entry from its members, then appends it using its timestamp, values and tag.
    void Append(TimeSeriesRollupEntry<TValues> entry);
    // Returns time series entries in the optional range by calling the overload with no includes. The defaults are
    // start 0 and pageSize int.MaxValue.
    TimeSeriesEntry[] Get(DateTime? from, DateTime? to, int start, int pageSize);
    // Synchronously gets time series entries in the given range through the async time series object, applying the
    // includes action. The defaults are start 0 and pageSize int.MaxValue.
    TimeSeriesEntry[] Get(DateTime? from, DateTime? to, Action<ITimeSeriesIncludeBuilder> includes, int start, int pageSize);
}
```

```csharp
/// Abstract implementation for in memory session operations
public abstract class SessionTimeSeriesBase
{
    // Appends an entry with the timestamp, a single value and optional tag by wrapping the value in an array and
    // calling the values overload.
    void Append(DateTime timestamp, double value, string tag);
    // Appends an entry with the timestamp and optional tag, converting the typed values object to doubles, or
    // using it directly when it is a sequence of doubles.
    void Append<TValues>(DateTime timestamp, TValues value, string tag);
    // Queues an append of the timestamp, values and optional tag as a deferred time series command in the session.
    // Throws InvalidOperationException if the document was deleted in the session.
    void Append(DateTime timestamp, IEnumerable<double> values, string tag);
    // Deletes the time series entry at the given timestamp by calling the range overload with the same timestamp
    // as from and to.
    void Delete(DateTime at);
    // Queues a delete of time series entries in the optional from and to range as a deferred command, and removes
    // affected ranges from the session cache. Throws InvalidOperationException if the document was deleted in the
    // session.
    void Delete(DateTime? from, DateTime? to);
    // Increments the incremental time series at the timestamp, converting the typed values object to doubles, or
    // using it directly when it is a sequence of doubles.
    void Increment<TValues>(DateTime timestamp, TValues value);
    // Queues an increment of the incremental time series at the timestamp by the sequence of double values as a
    // deferred command. Throws InvalidOperationException if the document was deleted in the session.
    void Increment(DateTime timestamp, IEnumerable<double> values);
    // Increments the incremental time series by the sequence of double values at the current UTC time.
    void Increment(IEnumerable<double> values);
    // Increments the incremental time series at the timestamp by the single value, passed as a one-element array.
    void Increment(DateTime timestamp, double value);
    // Increments the incremental time series by the single value at the current UTC time.
    void Increment(double value);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for a lazy operation that creates a multi-get request, handles its response, and exposes the
// result, query result and retry flag.
public interface ILazyOperation
{
    // Gets the QueryResult produced by the lazy operation.
    QueryResult QueryResult { get; }
    // Gets whether the lazy operation requires the request to be retried.
    bool RequiresRetry { get; }
    // Gets the result object produced by the lazy operation.
    object Result { get; }
    // Creates the GetRequest for the lazy operation using the given JSON operation context.
    GetRequest CreateRequest(JsonOperationContext ctx);
    // Processes the GetResponse returned for the lazy operation's request.
    void HandleResponse(GetResponse response);
}
```
