```csharp
// Query token that writes a counters include clause for a source path and either a single counter name or all
// counters.
public sealed class CounterIncludesToken : QueryToken
{
    // Prefixes the source path with the alias and a dot, or sets the path to the alias when the path is empty.
    void AddAliasToPath(string alias);
    // Creates a token that includes all counters from the given source path.
    static CounterIncludesToken All(string sourcePath);
    // Creates a token that includes the named counter from the given source path.
    static CounterIncludesToken Create(string sourcePath, string counterName);
    // Writes counters followed by an opening parenthesis, the source path if not empty, the quoted counter name
    // unless all counters are included, and a closing parenthesis.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Query token that writes an explanations clause, optionally referencing an options parameter.
public sealed class ExplanationToken : QueryToken
{
    // Creates an explanation token that references the given options parameter name.
    static ExplanationToken Create(string optionsParameterName);
    // Writes explanations followed by parentheses containing a dollar sign and the options parameter name when it
    // is not null.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Query token that writes the fields to fetch of a select clause, with optional projection aliases.
public sealed class FieldsToFetchToken : QueryToken
{
    // Read-only array of the field names to fetch.
    string[] FieldsToFetch;
    // Read-only flag indicating whether the fields are a custom function, in which case projection aliases are not
    // written.
    bool IsCustomFunction;
    // Read-only array of projection aliases corresponding to the fields to fetch.
    string[] Projections;
    // Read-only alias of the source, passed to the constructor.
    string SourceAlias;
    // Creates the token. Throws ArgumentNullException if fieldsToFetch is null or empty, or if a non-null
    // projections array differs in length from fieldsToFetch when not a custom function.
    static FieldsToFetchToken Create(string[] fieldsToFetch, string[] projections, bool isCustomFunction, string sourceAlias);
    // Writes the fields separated by commas, quoting RQL keywords and appending as and the projection when it
    // differs from the field name, unless it is a custom function.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Query token that writes the from clause of a query, either from a collection or from an index, with an
// optional alias.
public sealed class FromToken : QueryToken
{
    // Gets the alias written after the as keyword in the from clause, or null.
    string Alias { get; }
    // Gets the collection name used for a dynamic query.
    string CollectionName { get; }
    // Gets the index name used when querying an index.
    string IndexName { get; }
    // Gets whether the query is dynamic, true when the collection name is not null.
    bool IsDynamic { get; }
    // Creates a from token for the given index name, collection name and optional alias.
    static FromToken Create(string indexName, string collectionName, string alias);
    // Writes from followed by the quoted escaped collection name, or from index and the quoted index name, plus as
    // and the alias if set. Throws NotSupportedException if both names are null.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Query token that writes a highlight clause for a field with fragment length, fragment count and optional

{

    static HighlightingToken Create(string fieldName, int fragmentLength, int fragmentCount, string optionsParameterName);
    // Writes highlight with the field name, fragment length and count separated by commas, followed by a dollar
    // sign and the options parameter name when it is not null.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
```

```csharp
// Where token that writes a moreLikeThis clause, using either a document parameter, nested where tokens, or
// true, with an optional options parameter.
public sealed class MoreLikeThisToken : WhereToken
{
    // Creates the token with an empty linked list of where tokens.
    MoreLikeThisToken();
    // Name of the query parameter that holds the document to compare, written with a dollar prefix when not null.
    string DocumentParameterName;
    // Name of the query parameter that holds the options, written as a second argument with a dollar prefix when
    // not null.
    string OptionsParameterName;
    // Read-only linked list of where tokens written inside moreLikeThis when no document parameter name is set.
    LinkedList<QueryToken> WhereTokens;
    // Returns the same token instance and does not use the alias.
    WhereToken AddAlias(string alias);
    // Writes moreLikeThis with the document parameter, the where tokens or true, and the options parameter if set.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Query token that holds a spatial shape expression, either a circle or a WKT shape, as RQL text written into
// the query.
public sealed class ShapeToken : QueryToken
{
    // Creates a ShapeToken whose text is spatial.circle with the radius, latitude and longitude parameter names,
    // adding 'Kilometers' or 'Miles' as a unit argument when radiusUnits has a value.
    static ShapeToken Circle(string radiusParameterName, string latitudeParameterName, string longitudeParameterName, SpatialUnits? radiusUnits);
    // Creates a ShapeToken whose text is spatial.wkt with the given parameter name, adding 'Kilometers' or 'Miles'
    // as a unit argument when units has a value.
    static ShapeToken Wkt(string shapeWktParameterName, SpatialUnits? units);
    // Appends the stored shape expression text to the writer.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Where token for a vector.search clause. It stores quantization types, similarity threshold, number of
// candidates, document id flag and embeddings generation task identifiers, and writes them as RQL.
public sealed class VectorSearchToken : WhereToken
{
    // Stores the field name, parameter name, quantization types, similarity threshold, candidate count, document
    // id flag and task identifiers, and sets Options from isExact. Throws InvalidOperationException if both task
    // identifiers are non-null.
    VectorSearchToken(string fieldName, string parameterName, VectorEmbeddingType sourceQuantizationType, VectorEmbeddingType targetQuantizationType, float? similarityThreshold, int? numberOfCandidatesForQuerying, bool isExact, bool isDocumentId, string embeddingsGenerationTaskIdentifier, string embeddingsGenerationTaskIdentifierByValue);
    // Returns this token unchanged when the field is the document id field; otherwise returns a new
    // VectorSearchToken with the field name prefixed by the alias and the same settings, options and operator.
    WhereToken AddAlias(string alias);
    // Writes the vector.search RQL expression, wrapped in exact and boost when set, with the field or quantization
    // method, the parameter or embedding source, and similarity threshold and candidate count when either is set.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Enum of operators that a WhereToken can apply to a field in a query.
public enum WhereOperator
{
    // Selects equality comparison, written as =.
    Equals,
    // Selects inequality comparison, written as !=.
    NotEquals,
    // Selects greater-than comparison, written as >.
    GreaterThan,
    // Selects greater-than-or-equal comparison, written as >=.
    GreaterThanOrEqual,
    // Selects less-than comparison, written as <.
    LessThan,
    // Selects less-than-or-equal comparison, written as <=.
    LessThanOrEqual,
    // Selects membership test against a list parameter, written as in.
    In,
    // Selects a test that all values of a list parameter match, written as all in.
    AllIn,
    // Selects a range test between a from parameter and a to parameter, written as between ... and ....
    Between,
    // Selects a full-text search clause, written as search(field, parameter) with an optional and argument.
    Search,
    // Selects a Lucene query clause, written as lucene(field, parameter).
    Lucene,
    // Selects a prefix match clause, written as startsWith(field, parameter).
    StartsWith,
    // Selects a suffix match clause, written as endsWith(field, parameter).
    EndsWith,
    // Selects a field existence check, written as exists(field).
    Exists,
    // Selects a spatial within clause, written as spatial.within(field, shape).
    Spatial_Within,
    // Selects a spatial contains clause, written as spatial.contains(field, shape).
    Spatial_Contains,
    // Selects a spatial disjoint clause, written as spatial.disjoint(field, shape).
    Spatial_Disjoint,
    // Selects a spatial intersects clause, written as spatial.intersects(field, shape).
    Spatial_Intersects,
    // Selects a regular expression clause, written as regex(field, parameter).
    Regex,
    // Selects a vector search clause, written with the vector.search prefix.
    Vector_Search,
}
```

```csharp
// Query token for a where condition. It holds a field name, a parameter name, a WhereOperator and
// WhereOptions, and writes them as an RQL clause.
public class WhereToken : QueryToken
{
    // The options of this token, such as exact, boost, fuzzy, proximity, search operator, range parameter names,
    // method call and spatial shape.
    WhereToken.WhereOptions Options;
    // Gets the name of the field the condition applies to.
    string FieldName { get; }
    // Gets the name of the query parameter holding the value to compare with.
    string ParameterName { get; }
    // Gets the operator applied by this condition.
    WhereOperator WhereOperator { get; }
    // Returns this token unchanged when the field is the document id field; otherwise returns a new WhereToken
    // with the field name prefixed by the alias and the same parameter, operator and options.
    WhereToken AddAlias(string alias);
    // Creates a WhereToken with the given operator, field name, parameter name and options, using default options
    // when options is null.
    static WhereToken Create(WhereOperator op, string fieldName, string parameterName, WhereToken.WhereOptions options);
    // Writes the condition as RQL, wrapping it in boost, fuzzy, proximity and exact calls as set in the options
    // and emitting the operator-specific syntax.
    void WriteTo(StringBuilder writer);
}
```

```csharp
// Enum of server-side methods that can supply the compared value in a where condition.
public enum WhereToken.MethodsType
{
    // Selects the cmpxchg method, written as cmpxchg(parameters) with an optional property access.
    CmpXchg,
    // Selects the now method, written as now() with an optional first parameter.
    Now,
    // Selects the today method, written as today().
    Today,
}
```

```csharp
// Describes a method call used in place of a parameter in a where condition, with its type, parameters and
// optional property.
public sealed class WhereToken.WhereMethodCall
{
    // Initializes an empty WhereMethodCall with no values set.
    WhereMethodCall();
    // The MethodsType of the call: CmpXchg, Now or Today.
    WhereToken.MethodsType MethodType;
    // The parameter names passed to the method call.
    string[] Parameters;
    // The property name appended after the method call, or null for none.
    string Property;
}
```

```csharp
// Options for a WhereToken: exact matching, from and to parameter names, search operator, boost, fuzzy,
// proximity, method call and spatial shape with distance error.
public sealed class WhereToken.WhereOptions
{
    // Creates options with the Exact flag set to the given value.
    WhereOptions(bool exact);
    // Creates options with the Exact flag and the from and to parameter names used by a between condition.
    WhereOptions(bool exact, string from, string to);
    // Creates options with the given SearchOperator.
    WhereOptions(SearchOperator search);
    // Creates options with the given spatial shape and distance error percentage.
    WhereOptions(ShapeToken shape, double distance);
    // Creates options holding a WhereMethodCall built from the method type, parameters and property, and sets the
    // Exact flag.
    WhereOptions(WhereToken.MethodsType methodType, string[] parameters, string property, bool exact);
    // Optional boost factor; when set, the clause is written wrapped in boost.
    decimal? Boost { get; set; }
    // The distance error percentage used for spatial clauses; written into the clause when it differs from the
    // default.
    double DistanceErrorPct { get; }
    // Indicates whether the clause is written wrapped in exact.
    bool Exact { get; }
    // The name of the parameter holding the lower bound of a between condition.
    string FromParameterName { get; }
    // Optional fuzzy value; when set, the clause is written wrapped in fuzzy.
    decimal? Fuzzy { get; set; }
    // The method call used in place of a parameter value, or null when none is set.
    WhereToken.WhereMethodCall Method { get; }
    // Optional proximity value; when set, the clause is written wrapped in proximity.
    int? Proximity { get; set; }
    // The search operator used by a search clause; And causes the clause to be written with an and argument.
    SearchOperator? SearchOperator { get; }
    // The name of the parameter holding the upper bound of a between condition.
    string ToParameterName { get; }
    // The spatial ShapeToken written in spatial where clauses.
    ShapeToken WhereShape { get; }
    // Returns a new WhereOptions instance with no options set.
    static WhereToken.WhereOptions Default();
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Extensions`

```csharp
// Static class with extension methods for Task: cancellation, timeouts, result chaining and failure checks.
public static class TaskExtensions
{
    // If the task is faulted, waits on it so its exception is thrown; returns the task.
    static Task AssertNotFailed(Task task);
    // Continues after the task completes, asserting it did not fail, then calls the function and returns its inner
    // Task of T unwrapped.
    static Task<T> ContinueWithTask<T>(Task task, Func<Task<T>> result);
    // Continues after the task completes, asserting it did not fail, then calls the function and returns its inner
    // Task unwrapped.
    static Task ContinueWithTask(Task task, Func<Task> result);
    // Adds a continuation that runs only when the task faults and reads its Exception, so the exception is not
    // left unobserved; returns that continuation task.
    static Task IgnoreUnobservedExceptions(Task task);
    // Waits for the task or the timeout; returns true if the task completed first. With a null or infinite timeout
    // it awaits the task and returns true.
    static Task<bool> WaitWithTimeout(Task task, TimeSpan? timeout);
    // Returns the task unchanged when the token is the default token; otherwise returns a continuation using the
    // token that waits for the task result.
    static Task WithCancellation(Task task, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns the task unchanged when the token is the default token; otherwise returns a continuation using the
    // token that returns the task result.
    static Task<T> WithCancellation<T>(Task<T> task, CancellationToken token);
    // Returns a task that, after the source task completes without failure, yields the given result value.
    static Task<T> WithResult<T>(Task task, T result);
    // Returns a task that, after the source task completes without failure, yields the value returned by the given
    // function.
    static Task<T> WithResult<T>(Task task, Func<T> result);
    // Returns a task that, after the source task completes without failure, completes with the result of the given
    // Task of T.
    static Task<T> WithResult<T>(Task task, Task<T> result);
}
```

```csharp
// Cache entry holding a copy of a response in unmanaged memory with its change vector, size, flags, generation
// and a reference count.
public sealed class HttpCache.HttpCacheItem : IDisposable
{
    // Creates an item with one usage and LastServerUpdate set to the current UTC time.
    HttpCacheItem();
    // The unmanaged memory allocation that holds the cached data; returned to the pool when the last reference is
    // released.
    AllocatedMemoryData Allocation;
    // The HttpCache that owns this item.
    HttpCache Cache;
    // The change vector stored with the cached response.
    string ChangeVector;
    // The ItemFlags of the item, such as NotFound or AggressivelyCached.
    HttpCache.ItemFlags Flags;
    // The cache generation recorded when the item was created or last marked not modified.
    int Generation;
    // The UTC time when the item was created or last confirmed as not modified.
    DateTime LastServerUpdate;
    // Pointer to the start of the cached data in unmanaged memory; null for not-found items.
    Byte* Ptr;
    // The size in bytes of the cached data.
    int Size;
    // Increments the usage count; returns false if the memory had already been released, otherwise true.
    bool AddRef();
    // Releases the item's own initial reference once, via an internal release routine.
    void Dispose();
    // Decrements the usage count and, when no usages remain, returns the allocation to the pool and reduces the
    // cache total size.
    void ReleaseRef();
}
```

```csharp
// Flags enum describing the kind of an HTTP cache item.
public enum HttpCache.ItemFlags
{
    // Value 0; no flags are set.
    None,
    // Value 1; marks an item that records a not-found response.
    NotFound,
    // Value 16; marks an item as aggressively cached.
    AggressivelyCached,
}
```

```csharp
// Struct that wraps a cache item and releases its reference when disposed, remembering the cache generation at
// creation.
public struct HttpCache.ReleaseCacheItem : IDisposable
{
    // Stores the item and the item's cache generation at that moment.
    ReleaseCacheItem(HttpCache.HttpCacheItem item);
    // The wrapped cache item.
    HttpCache.HttpCacheItem Item;
    // Time since the item's last server update; TimeSpan.MaxValue when the item is null.
    TimeSpan Age { get; }
    // True when the item's generation differs from the cache generation recorded when this struct was created.
    bool MightHaveBeenModified { get; }
    // Releases one reference of the wrapped item, if it is not null.
    void Dispose();
    // Sets the item's generation to the recorded cache generation and its last server update to the current UTC
    // time, if the item is not null.
    void NotModified();
}
```

```csharp
// Chooses a server node from a Topology, tracking node failures and speed-test records to select preferred,
// fastest or session-based nodes.
public sealed class NodeSelector : IDisposable
{
    // Creates a selector with an internal state built from the given topology.
    NodeSelector(Topology topology);
    // True when the speed-test mode value is greater than 1.
    bool InSpeedTestPhase { get; }
    // Gets the topology of the current selector state.
    Topology Topology { get; }
    // Disposes the timer used to update the fastest node, if one exists.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns the index and node of the recorded fastest node if it has no failures and is a Member; otherwise
    // schedules a speed test and returns the preferred node.
    ValueTuple<int, ServerNode> GetFastestNode();
    // Starts at the index of sessionId modulo node count and returns the first node with no failures and role
    // Member, wrapping around; falls back to the preferred node. Throws AllTopologyNodesDownException if there are
    // no nodes.
    ValueTuple<int, ServerNode> GetNodeBySessionId(int sessionId);
    // Returns the first node with no recorded failures and role Member; if none, returns the first node without
    // failures or rotates through nodes. Throws DatabaseDoesNotExistException if there are no nodes.
    ValueTuple<int, ServerNode> GetPreferredNode();
    // Increments the failure count of the node at the given index; does nothing if the index is out of range.
    void OnFailedRequest(int nodeIndex);
    // Returns false if topology is null or its Etag is not newer than the current one (unless forceUpdate is
    // true); otherwise replaces the internal state with one built from the topology and returns true.
    bool OnUpdateTopology(Topology topology, bool forceUpdate);
    // Increments the fastest record of the node at the index if the index and node still match the state; when it
    // reaches 10, or concurrent tests exceed the limit, selects a fastest node.
    void RecordFastest(int index, ServerNode node);
    // Resets the failure count of the given node to zero; does nothing if the node is not in the topology.
    void RestoreNodeIndex(ServerNode node);
    // Does nothing if the update timer exists; otherwise switches to the speed-test phase and creates a timer that
    // repeats the switch.
    void ScheduleSpeedTest();
}
```

```csharp
// Extends DatabaseState with flags and settings for a database, such as disabled state, lock mode, encryption,
// topology, replication factor and configuration presence.
public class DatabaseInfo : DatabaseState
{
    // Initializes a new instance of DatabaseInfo.
    DatabaseInfo();
    // The lock mode of the database.
    DatabaseLockMode LockMode;
    // Gets or sets a dictionary mapping string keys to DeletionInProgressStatus values.
    Dictionary<string, DeletionInProgressStatus> DeletionInProgress { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the database is disabled.
    bool Disabled { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether dynamic nodes distribution is enabled for the database.
    bool DynamicNodesDistribution { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the Studio environment of the database.
    StudioConfiguration.StudioEnvironment Environment { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the database has a data archival configuration.
    bool HasDataArchivalConfiguration { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the database has an expiration configuration.
    bool HasExpirationConfiguration { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the database has a refresh configuration.
    bool HasRefreshConfiguration { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the database has a revisions configuration.
    bool HasRevisionsConfiguration { get; set; }
```

```csharp
// Status of a sharding bucket migration, from pending to ownership transferred.
public enum MigrationStatus
{
    // First status in the migration sequence.
    Pending,
    // The source is in the process of sending the bucket.
    Moving,
    // The source finished sending; destination member nodes confirm having all documents, while writes still go to
    // the source shard.
    Moved,
    // All destination member nodes confirmed receiving the bucket; the mapping is updated so traffic goes to the
    // destination and the source starts cleanup.
    OwnershipTransferred,
}
```

```csharp
// Header message sent when opening a TCP connection; carries database name, source node tag, operation type
// and version, authorization info, licensed features, and holds protocol version constants and feature lookup
// methods.
public sealed class TcpConnectionHeaderMessage
{
    // Initializes a new empty TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage();
    // Constant with value 10; the baseline protocol version for the Cluster operation.
    static int ClusterBaseLine;
    // Equal to ClusterWithTcpCompression; the current protocol version for the Cluster operation.
    static int ClusterTcpVersion;
    // Constant with value 52000; the Cluster protocol version that adds multi-tree support.
    static int ClusterWithMultiTree;
    // Constant with value 54000; the Cluster protocol version that adds TCP data compression.
    static int ClusterWithTcpCompression;
    // Constant with value -2; the baseline protocol version for the Drop operation.
    static int DropBaseLine;
    // Constant with value 41200; a supported Heartbeats protocol version.
    static int Heartbeats41200;
    // Constant with value 42000; a supported Heartbeats protocol version.
    static int Heartbeats42000;
    // Constant with value 20; the baseline protocol version for the Heartbeats operation.
    static int HeartbeatsBaseLine;
    // Equal to HeartbeatsWithTcpCompression; the current protocol version for the Heartbeats operation.
    static int HeartbeatsTcpVersion;
    // Constant with value 54000; the Heartbeats protocol version that adds TCP data compression.
    static int HeartbeatsWithTcpCompression;
    // Constant with value -1; the baseline protocol version for the None operation.
    static int NoneBaseLine;
    // Constant with value -1; the baseline protocol version for the Ping operation.
    static int PingBaseLine;
    // Constant with value 40300; the Replication protocol version that adds the missing attachments feature.
    static int ReplicationAttachmentMissing;
    // Constant with value 41300; the Replication protocol version that adds the missing attachments feature for
    // the 4.1 line.
    static int ReplicationAttachmentMissingVersion41;
    // Constant with value 31; the baseline protocol version for the Replication operation.
    static int ReplicationBaseLine;
    // Detailed replication hub access information carried in the header message.
    DetailedReplicationHubAccess ReplicationHubAccess;
    // Equal to ReplicationWithTimeSeriesDocumentChangeVector; the current protocol version for the Replication
    // operation.
    static int ReplicationTcpVersion;
    // Constant with value 53001; the Replication protocol version that adds deduplicated attachments.
    static int ReplicationWithDeduplicatedAttachments;
    // Constant with value 42300; the Replication protocol version that adds the pull replication option.
    static int ReplicationWithPullOption;
    // Constant with value 72000; the Replication protocol version that adds remote attachments.
    static int ReplicationWithRemoteAttachments;
    // Constant with value 60000; the Replication protocol version that adds revision tombstones with id.
    static int ReplicationWithRevisionTombstones;
    // Constant with value 50000; the Replication protocol version that adds time series.
    static int ReplicationWithTimeSeries;
    // Constant with value 72001; the Replication protocol version that adds time series with document change
    // vector.
    static int ReplicationWithTimeSeriesDocumentChangeVector;
    // Server identifier string carried in the header.
    string ServerId;
    // Constant with value 40; the baseline protocol version for the Subscription operation.
    static int SubscriptionBaseLine;
    // Constant with value 50000; the Subscription protocol version that adds counter includes.
    static int SubscriptionCounterIncludes;
    // Constant with value 41400; the Subscription protocol version that adds includes.
    static int SubscriptionIncludes;
    // Equal to TcpConnectionsWithCompression; the current protocol version for the Subscription operation.
    static int SubscriptionTcpVersion;
    // Constant with value 51000; the Subscription protocol version that adds time series includes.
    static int SubscriptionTimeSeriesIncludes;
    // Constant with value 53000; the protocol version that adds TCP connection data compression for Subscription
    // and Replication.
    static int TcpConnectionsWithCompression;
    // Constant with value 50; the baseline protocol version for the TestConnection operation.
    static int TestConnectionBaseLine;
    // Equal to TestConnectionBaseLine; the current protocol version for the TestConnection operation.
    static int TestConnectionTcpVersion;
    // Authorization information (authorize method and target) sent with the header.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.AuthorizationInfo AuthorizeInfo { get; set; }
    // Name of the database the connection is for.
    string DatabaseName { get; set; }
    // String of additional information carried in the header.
    string Info { get; set; }
    // Licensed features sent with the header message.
    LicensedFeatures LicensedFeatures { get; set; }
    // The OperationTypes value stating what the TCP connection is used for.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.OperationTypes Operation { get; set; }
    // Protocol version of the operation requested in the header.
    int OperationVersion { get; set; }
    // Tag of the source node of the connection.
    string SourceNodeTag { get; set; }
    // Returns the protocol version for the given operation type at the given index in its supported list; Ping and
    // None return -1, Drop returns -2, and an unknown type throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException.
    static int GetOperationTcpVersion(TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.OperationTypes operationType, int index);
    // Returns the SupportedFeatures registered for the operation type and protocol version; throws
    // ArgumentException when that version is not found.
    static TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures GetSupportedFeaturesFor(TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.OperationTypes type, int protocolVersion);
    // Compares the version with the supported versions of the operation type and returns Supported, NotSupported
    // or OutOfRange, setting current to the compared version; throws ArgumentException for an unregistered type.
    static TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedStatus OperationVersionSupported(TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.OperationTypes operationType, int version, out int current);
}
```

```csharp
// Enum of the TCP connection operation types that a connection header can request.
public enum TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.OperationTypes
{
    // No operation; uses protocol baseline version -1.
    None,
    // Requests that the connection be dropped; uses protocol baseline version -2.
    Drop,
    // Selects a data subscription connection.
    Subscription,
    // Selects a replication connection.
    Replication,
    // Selects a cluster connection.
    Cluster,
    // Selects a heartbeats connection.
    Heartbeats,
    // Selects a ping connection; uses protocol baseline version -1.
    Ping,
    // Selects a connection test; uses protocol baseline version 50.
    TestConnection,
}
```

```csharp
// Describes the set of features available for a given TCP protocol version, with one feature object per
// operation type.
public sealed class TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures
{
    // Creates an instance and stores the given version in ProtocolVersion.
    SupportedFeatures(int version);
    // Indicates whether data compression is supported; set to true only in the feature sets of protocol versions
    // that support compression.
    bool DataCompression;
    // The protocol version number these features describe, assigned in the constructor.
    int ProtocolVersion;
    // Features of the cluster operation; null when the version does not describe a cluster operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.ClusterFeatures Cluster { get; set; }
    // Features of the drop operation; null when the version does not describe a drop operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.DropFeatures Drop { get; set; }
    // Features of the heartbeats operation; null when the version does not describe a heartbeats operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.HeartbeatsFeatures Heartbeats { get; set; }
    // Features of the none operation; null when the version does not describe it.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.NoneFeatures None { get; set; }
    // Features of the ping operation; null when the version does not describe a ping operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.PingFeatures Ping { get; set; }
    // Features of the replication operation; null when the version does not describe a replication operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.ReplicationFeatures Replication { get; set; }
    // Features of the subscription operation; null when the version does not describe a subscription operation.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.SubscriptionFeatures Subscription { get; set; }
    // Features of the test connection operation; null when the version does not describe it.
    TcpConnectionHeaderMessage.SupportedFeatures.TestConnectionFeatures TestConnection { get; set; }
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Util`

```csharp
// Static helper that runs asynchronous tasks synchronously.
public static class AsyncHelpers
{
    // Static flag, true by default; when true and there is no current SynchronizationContext, RunSync waits on the
    // task directly instead of using a private message loop.
    static bool UseTaskAwaiterWhenNoSynchronizationContextIsAvailable;
    // Runs the given task-returning function to completion synchronously. An OperationCanceledException is
    // rethrown as a TimeoutException; an aggregate exception with a single inner exception is rethrown as that
    // inner exception.
    static void RunSync(Func<Task> task);
    // Runs the given function returning Task of T synchronously and returns its result. An
    // OperationCanceledException becomes a TimeoutException; an aggregate exception with a single inner exception
    // is rethrown as that inner exception.
    static T RunSync<T>(Func<Task<T>> task);
}
```

```csharp
/// Provide a way for interested party to tell whether implementers have been disposed
public interface IDisposalNotification : IDisposable
{
    /// Whether the instance has been disposed
    bool WasDisposed { get; }
    /// Called after dispose is completed
    event EventHandler AfterDispose;
}
```

```csharp
public interface IAiConversationOperations
{
    /// Retrieves the list of action-tool requests that the AI agent needs you to execute.
    IEnumerable<AiAgentActionRequest> RequiredActions();
    /// Synchronously executes one turn of the conversation with output format options.
    AiAnswer<TAnswer> RunWithSchema<TAnswer>(AiOutputOptions outputOptions);
    /// Synchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation, deriving the output schema from 'sampleObject' for
    ///    this turn only.
    AiAnswer<TAnswer> RunWithSchema<TAnswer>(TAnswer sampleObject);
    /// Synchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with an explicit JSON schema override for this turn
    ///    only.
    AiAnswer<TAnswer> RunWithSchema<TAnswer>(string schema);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with output format options, allowing the caller to
    ///    override the agent's default output schema or disable structured output.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> RunWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(AiOutputOptions outputOptions, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation, deriving the output schema from 'sampleObject' for
    ///    this turn only.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> RunWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(TAnswer sampleObject, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with an explicit JSON schema override for this turn
    ///    only.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> RunWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(string schema, CancellationToken token);
    /// Synchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming and output format options.
    AiAnswer<TAnswer> StreamWithSchema<TAnswer>(string streamPropertyPath, Action<string> streamedChunksCallback, AiOutputOptions outputOptions);
    /// Synchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming and output format options.
    AiAnswer<TAnswer> StreamWithSchema<TAnswer>(Expression<Func<TAnswer, string>> streamPropertyPath, Action<string> streamedChunksCallback, AiOutputOptions outputOptions);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming and output format options.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> StreamWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(string streamPropertyPath, Func<string, Task> streamedChunksCallback, AiOutputOptions outputOptions, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming and output format options.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> StreamWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(Expression<Func<TAnswer, string>> streamPropertyPath, Func<string, Task> streamedChunksCallback, AiOutputOptions outputOptions, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming, using a sample object to derive the
    ///    output schema.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> StreamWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(string streamPropertyPath, Func<string, Task> streamedChunksCallback, TAnswer sampleObject, CancellationToken token);
    /// Asynchronously executes one "turn" of the conversation with streaming, using a sample object to derive the
    ///    output schema.
    Task<AiAnswer<TAnswer>> StreamWithSchemaAsync<TAnswer>(Expression<Func<TAnswer, string>> streamPropertyPath, Func<string, Task> streamedChunksCallback, TAnswer sampleObject, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
public enum CommandType
{
    // Command type for moving an attachment to another document or name; returned by
    // MoveAttachmentCommandData.Type.
    AttachmentMOVE,
    // Command type for copying an attachment to another document or name; returned by
    // CopyAttachmentCommandData.Type.
    AttachmentCOPY,
    // Command type for putting a compare exchange value in a batch; cluster-wide sessions accept this command
    // type.
    CompareExchangePUT,
    // Command type for deleting a compare exchange value in a batch; cluster-wide sessions accept this command
    // type.
    CompareExchangeDELETE,
    // Command type for forcing creation of a revision for a document; used by the revisions command data in a
    // batch.
    ForceRevisionCreation,
    // Command type for time series commands that include increment operations; excluded from the document-
    // modifying deferred command lookup.
    TimeSeriesWithIncrements,
    // Command type for copying time series data; excluded from the document-modifying deferred command lookup.
    TimeSeriesCopy,
    // Command type for the batch patch command that patches multiple documents by id with one script; when
    // deferred, the session registers it as a patch for each id.
    BatchPATCH,
    // Client-side key type used in the deferred commands dictionary to look up any deferred command registered for
    // a document id. It is not a command sent to the server.
    ClientAnyCommand,
    // Client-side key type used in the deferred commands dictionary to look up a deferred command that modifies
    // the document itself, excluding attachment, counter and time series commands.
    ClientModifyDocumentCommand,
    // Command type written as a heartbeat message by bulk insert while streaming to the server.
    HeartBeat,
    // Command type for the batch command that checks tracked entities for concurrency; batch result processing
    // skips its result, and cluster sessions reject it.
    BatchTrackChanges,
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class LinqPathProvider
{
    /// Get the member expression from the expression
    static MemberExpression GetMemberExpression(Expression expression);
    /// Get the path from the expression, including considering dictionary calls
    LinqPathProvider.Result GetPath(Expression expression, bool isFilterActive);
    /// Get the actual value from the expression
    object GetValueFromExpression(Expression expression, Type type);
    // Tries to evaluate an expression to a value without conversion, handling constants, member access, member
    // init, new, lambda and some RavenQuery calls; outputs the value and returns true when supported.
    static bool GetValueFromExpressionWithoutConversion(Expression expression, out object value);
    // Returns the name with the member's own name replaced by the name from a JsonProperty or DataMember attribute
    // when one is present and not empty; otherwise returns the name unchanged.
    static string HandlePropertyRenames(MemberInfo member, string name);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.CmpXchg.
    static bool IsCompareExchangeCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the call is RavenQuery.Counter or a Get call on ISessionDocumentCounters.
    static bool IsCounterCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.Id.
    static bool IsIdCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.Include.
    static bool IsIncludeCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.Metadata.
    static bool IsMetadataCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.Now.
    static bool IsNowCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true for calls on time series queryable types, or returning one, whose return type is a time series
    // raw or aggregation result; throws InvalidOperationException for other return types in those cases.
    static bool IsTimeSeriesCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
    // Returns true if the method call is RavenQuery.Today.
    static bool IsTodayCall(MethodCallExpression mce);
}
```

```csharp
    void Init(IRavenQueryProvider provider, QueryStatistics queryStats, LinqQueryHighlightings highlightings, string indexName, string collectionName, Expression expression, InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IMaintenanceOperation
{
    // Returns the command that executes this maintenance operation using the given conventions and context.
    RavenCommand GetCommand(DocumentConventions conventions, JsonOperationContext context);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IMaintenanceOperation<T>
{
    // Returns the typed command that executes this maintenance operation and produces a result of type T.
    RavenCommand<T> GetCommand(DocumentConventions conventions, JsonOperationContext context);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IOperation
{
    // Returns the command that executes this operation against the store using the given conventions, context and
    // cache.
    RavenCommand GetCommand(IDocumentStore store, DocumentConventions conventions, JsonOperationContext context, HttpCache cache);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IOperation<T>
{
    // Returns the typed command that executes this operation against the store and produces a result of type T.
    RavenCommand<T> GetCommand(IDocumentStore store, DocumentConventions conventions, JsonOperationContext context, HttpCache cache);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IOperationProgress
{
    // Merges another operation progress into this one; the interface also defines CanMerge, and some
    // implementations throw NotSupportedException.
    void MergeWith(IOperationProgress progress);
}
```

```csharp
public interface IOperationResult
{
    // Merges another operation result into this one; the interface also defines CanMerge, and some implementations
    // throw NotSupportedException.
    void MergeWith(IOperationResult result);
}
```

```csharp
public class Operation : IObserver<OperationStatusChange>
{
    // Gets how the operation status is fetched, either through the Changes API or by polling; initialized from the
    // conventions OperationStatusFetchMode.
    OperationStatusFetchMode StatusFetchMode { get; }
    // Marks the internal result task so that unobserved exceptions are ignored.
    void OnCompleted();
    // Handles a status change: in progress invokes the progress event, completed stops processing and sets the
    // result, faulted stops processing and sets an exception built from the error result.
    void OnNext(OperationStatusChange change);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class AttachmentStream : Stream
{
    // Always returns true.
    bool CanRead { get; }
    // Returns the CanSeek value of the underlying stream.
    bool CanSeek { get; }
    // Always returns false.
    bool CanWrite { get; }
    // Reads from the underlying stream asynchronously into the buffer.
    Task<int> ReadAsync(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, CancellationToken cancellationToken);
    // Seeks in the underlying stream and returns the new position.
    long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin);
    // Throws NotSupportedException because the attachment stream is not writable.
    void SetLength(long value);
    // Throws NotSupportedException because the attachment stream is not writable.
    Task WriteAsync(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, CancellationToken cancellationToken);
}
```

```csharp
    string GetMentorNode();
    // Returns TaskId cast to an unsigned 64-bit integer; a debug assertion requires TaskId to be non-zero.
    UInt64 GetTaskKey();
    // Returns true if other is null or if its FullBackupFrequency or IncrementalBackupFrequency differs from this
    // instance; otherwise false.
    bool HasBackupFrequencyChanged(PeriodicBackupConfiguration other);
    // Always returns true.
    bool IsResourceIntensive();
    // Returns the base audit JSON plus Name, TaskId, Disabled, MentorNode, PinToMentorNode, both backup
    // frequencies and the audit JSON of RetentionPolicy.
    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
    // Returns true with a null message if the configuration has a backup destination or is Disabled. Otherwise
    // returns false with a message that the configuration is enabled without any destinations.
    bool ValidateDestinations(out string message);
}
```

```csharp
public class CdcSinkConfiguration : IDynamicJson, IDatabaseTask
{
    /// Collects the active configured tables (including embedded tables recursively) as a flat, deduplicated list of
    ///    physical (schema, table) identities. A source table can be mapped several times at once (a root collection
    ///    and/or embedded arrays), but must be read/captured exactly ONCE and then fanned out to all its processors.
    ///    Disabled root tables - and their embedded tables - are excluded, so they are neither initial-loaded nor
    ///    change-captured.
    List<CdcSinkConfiguration.TableInfo> CollectAllTablesFlat(string defaultSchema);
    /// Walks every embedded table in the configuration tree (depth-first, recursive). A single traversal with a
    ///    stack guard — all callers that need to visit embedded tables should use this instead of rolling their own
    ///    recursion.
    static void ForEachEmbeddedTable(List<CdcSinkEmbeddedTableConfig> embedded, Action<CdcSinkEmbeddedTableConfig> action);
    // Returns the text CDC Sink to followed by ConnectionStringName.
    string GetDefaultTaskName();
    // Returns the ConnectionString of the Connection, or null if Connection is null.
    string GetDestination();
    // Returns the MentorNode value.
    string GetMentorNode();
    // Returns TaskId cast to an unsigned 64-bit integer; a debug assertion requires TaskId to be non-zero.
    UInt64 GetTaskKey();
    // Always returns false.
    bool IsResourceIntensive();
}
```

```csharp
public class CdcSinkEmbeddedTableConfig : IFillFromBlittableJson, IDynamicJson
{
    // Deserializes the given JSON into a CdcSinkEmbeddedTableConfig and copies its table, column, key, relation
    // type, patch, delete, embedded and linked table settings into this instance.
    void FillFromBlittableJson(BlittableJsonReaderObject json);
}
```

```csharp
public abstract class EtlConfiguration<T> : IDynamicJson, IDatabaseTask
{
    // Abstract read-only property that returns the EtlType of the configuration.
    EtlType EtlType { get; }
    // Abstract method that returns the default task name for the configuration.
    string GetDefaultTaskName();
    // Abstract method that returns a string describing the ETL destination.
    string GetDestination();
    // Reads the EtlType field from the JSON and parses it into an EtlType. Throws InvalidOperationException if the
    // field is missing and NotSupportedException if the value is unknown.
    static EtlType GetEtlType(BlittableJsonReaderObject etlConfiguration);
    // Returns the MentorNode value.
    string GetMentorNode();
    // Returns TaskId cast to an unsigned 64-bit integer; a debug assertion requires TaskId to be non-zero.
    UInt64 GetTaskKey();
    // Always returns false.
    bool IsResourceIntensive();
    // Returns the result of ToJson.
    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
    // Abstract method that returns whether the ETL destination uses an encrypted communication channel.
    bool UsingEncryptedCommunicationChannel();
}
```

```csharp

{

    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
}
```

```csharp

{

    // Returns Database, an at sign and the comma-separated TopologyDiscoveryUrls of the connection, caching the
    // result.
    string GetDestination();
    // Returns the result of ToJson.
    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
    // Returns false if any TopologyDiscoveryUrls entry of the connection starts with http:, otherwise true.
    bool UsingEncryptedCommunicationChannel();
}
```

```csharp
    bool IsValidConnection();

    DynamicJsonValue ToAuditJson();
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class QueryData
{
    // Gets or sets the declare tokens passed to the query when the projection is built.
    IEnumerable<DeclareToken> DeclareTokens { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the alias of the query source, used to detect when a projected field starts with that alias.
    string FromAlias { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the query had any include; it is passed when the fields to fetch token is updated.
    bool HadAnyInclude { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the projection is a custom function; it is passed when the fields to fetch token is
    // created.
    bool IsCustomFunction { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets a flag named IsMapReduce; the property is not read elsewhere in the client code.
    bool IsMapReduce { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the load tokens passed to the query, whose aliases are checked when resolving a projected field
    // alias.
    List<LoadToken> LoadTokens { get; set; }
}
```

```csharp
public class AsyncDocumentQuery<T> : AbstractDocumentQuery<T, AsyncDocumentQuery<T>>, IAbstractDocumentQueryImpl<T>, IAsyncRawDocumentQuery<T>, IDocumentQueryGenerator, IAsyncDocumentQuery<T>
{
    // Throws InvalidOperationException if the index name or collection name differs from the current query;
    // otherwise returns a new async document query of the result type.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<TResult> AsyncQuery<TResult>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
public class AsyncDocumentSession : InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations, IAsyncAdvancedSessionOperations, IDocumentQueryGenerator
{
    /// Creates a new Raven query inspector for the specified type
    RavenQueryInspector<S> CreateRavenQueryInspector<S>();
    /// Internal method to lazily load entities with includes asynchronously
    Lazy<Task<Dictionary<string, T>>> LazyAsyncLoadInternal<T>(string[] ids, string[] includes, Action<Dictionary<string, T>> onEval, CancellationToken token);
    // Throws ArgumentNullException if ids is null, then builds a load operation with the includes, counters,
    // revisions, time series and compare exchange values, executes it and returns the documents by id.
    Task<Dictionary<string, T>> LoadAsyncInternal<T>(string[] ids, string[] includes, string[] counterIncludes, bool includeAllCounters, IEnumerable<AbstractTimeSeriesRange> timeSeriesIncludes, string[] compareExchangeValueIncludes, string[] revisionIncludesByChangeVector, DateTime? revisionsToIncludeByDateTime, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class DocumentInfo : IDisposable
{
    /// If set to true, the session will ignore this document when SaveChanges() is called, and won't perform and
    ///    change tracking
    bool IgnoreChanges { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets whether the document info represents a new document.
    bool IsNewDocument { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the metadata dictionary instance for the document.
    IMetadataDictionary MetadataInstance { get; set; }
    // Returns the last modified date from the metadata, or throws InvalidOperationException when it is missing.
    DateTime GetLastModified();
    // Creates a DocumentInfo from the document, reading the id and change vector from its metadata, and throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the metadata, id or change vector is missing.
    static DocumentInfo GetNewDocumentInfo(BlittableJsonReaderObject document);
}
```

```csharp
public class DocumentQuery<T> : AbstractDocumentQuery<T, DocumentQuery<T>>, IDocumentQuery<T>, IRawDocumentQuery<T>, IDocumentQueryGenerator, IAbstractDocumentQueryImpl<T>
{
    // Throws NotSupportedException, because an async LINQ query cannot be created from DocumentQuery.
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<TResult> AsyncQuery<TResult>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
}
```

```csharp
public class DocumentSession : InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations, IDocumentQueryGenerator, IAdvancedSessionOperations
{
    /// Create a new query for
    IAsyncDocumentQuery<T> AsyncQuery<T>(string indexName, string collectionName, bool isMapReduce);
    // Returns a new RavenQueryInspector of the given type.
    RavenQueryInspector<S> CreateRavenQueryInspector<S>();
    /// Register to lazily load documents and include
    Lazy<Dictionary<string, T>> LazyLoadInternal<T>(string[] ids, string[] includes, Action<Dictionary<string, T>> onEval);
    // Throws ArgumentNullException if ids is null, then builds a load operation with the includes, counters,
    // revisions, time series and compare exchange values, executes it and returns the documents by id.
    Dictionary<string, T> LoadInternal<T>(string[] ids, string[] includes, string[] counterIncludes, bool includeAllCounters, IEnumerable<AbstractTimeSeriesRange> timeSeriesIncludes, string[] compareExchangeValueIncludes, string[] revisionIncludesByChangeVector, DateTime? revisionIncludeByDateTimeBefore);
}
```

```csharp
public abstract class InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations : IDisposable
{
    // Throws ObjectDisposedException if the session is disposed, or if dispose checks are enabled and the document
    // store is disposed.
    void AssertNotDisposed();
    // Returns true when every id is known missing or already loaded or included with its includes loaded; returns
    // false otherwise.
    bool CheckIfIdAlreadyIncluded(string[] ids, IEnumerable<string> includes);
    /// Gets the default value of the specified type.
    static object GetDefaultValue(Type type);
    // Increments NumberOfRequests and throws InvalidOperationException when it exceeds
    // MaxNumberOfRequestsPerSession.
    void IncrementRequestCount();
    // Adds the id to the set of known missing ids, unless NoTracking is enabled.
    void RegisterMissing(string id);
    // Adds the ids to the set of known missing ids, unless NoTracking is enabled.
    void RegisterMissing(IEnumerable<string> ids);
    // For each result and include path, registers an included document id as missing when it is not loaded, unless
    // NoTracking is enabled or the included document is a conflict.
    void RegisterMissingIncludes(BlittableJsonReaderArray results, BlittableJsonReaderObject includes, ICollection<string> includePaths);
    /// Tracks the entity inside the unit of work
    T TrackEntity<T>(DocumentInfo documentFound);
    /// Tracks the entity.
    T TrackEntity<T>(string id, BlittableJsonReaderObject document, BlittableJsonReaderObject metadata, bool noTracking);
}
```

### `Raven.Client.Documents.Session.Operations`

```csharp
public sealed class QueryOperation
{
    // Stops the supplied Stopwatch if not null and delegates to the TimeSpan overload using its elapsed time;
    // passes a null duration when the Stopwatch is null.
    static void EnsureIsAcceptable(QueryResult result, bool waitForNonStaleResults, Stopwatch duration, InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session);
    // Throws TimeoutException with a message stating the wait for non-stale results (including elapsed
    // milliseconds when a duration is given) if waitForNonStaleResults is true and the result is stale.
    static void EnsureIsAcceptable(QueryResult result, bool waitForNonStaleResults, TimeSpan? duration, InMemoryDocumentSessionOperations session);
    // Stops the internal stopwatch if one was started, then validates the result and saves it as the current query
    // results; throws IndexDoesNotExistException if the result is null.
    void EnsureIsAcceptableAndSaveResult(QueryResult result);
    // Starts the query stopwatch and returns null when the query does not wait for non-stale results; otherwise
    // returns the disposable from disabling aggressive caching for the session database.
    IDisposable EnterQueryContext();
    // Writes a debug log entry with the query text, index name, store identifier and, when the query has a non-
    // blank tag, that tag, if debug logging is enabled.
    void LogQuery();
```

```csharp
public class SmugglerResult : SmugglerProgressBase, IOperationResult
{
    // Gets or sets the legacy last attachment etag string, which is set during legacy imports and used by the
    // legacy migrator as the last attachment and attachment delete etags.
    string LegacyLastAttachmentEtag { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the legacy last document etag string, which is set during legacy imports and used as the last
    // document and document delete etag by legacy replication and migration.
    string LegacyLastDocumentEtag { get; set; }
    // Adds a message prefixed with the UTC time and the ERROR level to the messages list and sets it as the
    // current Message.
    void AddError(string message);
    // Adds a message prefixed with the UTC time and the INFO level to the messages list and sets it as the current
    // Message.
    void AddInfo(string message);
    // Adds a message prefixed with the UTC time and the WARNING level to the messages list and sets it as the
    // current Message.
    void AddWarning(string message);
    // Increments the errored count of Documents or RevisionDocuments depending on the item type in progress,
    // throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException for other types, and adds the exception message as an error.
    void OnCorruptedData(object sender, InvalidOperationException e);
    // Records the given type as the item type in progress and adds an info message that processing of that type
    // started.
    void StartProcessingForType(DatabaseItemType type);
    // Adds an info message that processing of the current item type finished, including the counts, and resets the
    // item type in progress to None.
    void StopProcessingActualType(SmugglerProgressBase.Counts counts);
}
```

```csharp
public class RequestExecutor : IDisposable
{
    // Static read-only string holding the assembly version from RavenVersionAttribute, sent in the client version
    // request header.
    static string ClientVersion;
    // Returns true when the static server certificate custom validation callback array is not empty.
    static bool HasServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback { get; }
    // Returns the InSpeedTestPhase value of the node selector, or false when there is no node selector.
    bool InSpeedTestPhase { get; }
    // Returns the DNS-safe host of the request URI for an HttpRequestMessage or WebRequest sender, the string
    // itself for a string sender, and an empty string for other types.
    static string ConvertSenderObjectToHostname(object sender);
    // Creates a RequestExecutor for a single node URL and enables client configuration updates on it.
    static RequestExecutor CreateForSingleNodeWithConfigurationUpdates(string url, string databaseName, X509Certificate2 certificate, DocumentConventions conventions);
    // Creates a RequestExecutor for a single node URL with topology updates and client configuration updates
    // disabled, and starts a single topology update.
    static RequestExecutor CreateForSingleNodeWithoutConfigurationUpdates(string url, string databaseName, X509Certificate2 certificate, DocumentConventions conventions);
    // Spawns health checks for the chosen node, marks the request failed, selects the preferred node, then forces
    // a topology update or runs a health check when topology updates are disabled, and returns the preferred node.
    Task<ServerNode> HandleServerNotResponsive(string url, ServerNode chosenNode, int nodeIndex, Exception e);
    // Returns the response content stream, wrapped in a decompression stream according to the Content-Encoding
    // header (gzip, brotli, zstd or deflate) or unwrapped if there is none.
    static Task<Stream> ReadAsStreamUncompressedAsync(HttpResponseMessage response);
}
```

```csharp
public enum DatabasePromotionStatus
{
    // Promotion status set for a node newly added to the database topology as a promotable, before it has been
    // promoted.
    WaitingForFirstPromotion,
    // Status set when the node did not respond, or when no resource limitation (CPU credits, early out of memory,
    // high dirty memory) is reported for it.
    NotResponding,
    // Status set when the promotable node's indexes are not up to date compared with the mentor node.
    IndexNotUpToDate,
    // Status set when the mentor node has not yet sent all documents to the promotable node.
    ChangeVectorNotMerged,
    // Member of the promotion status enum; no code in the client or server sources assigns it.
    WaitingForResponse,
    // Status meaning the promotable node has no detected lag; also the initial status reported for a node in the
    // database group.
    Ok,
    // Status reported for a node whose server report indicates it is out of CPU credits.
    OutOfCpuCredits,
    // Status reported for a node whose server report indicates an early out of memory state.
    EarlyOutOfMemory,
    // Status reported for a node whose server report indicates a high dirty memory state.
    HighDirtyMemory,
    // Status set when the promotable node has not yet received all compare exchange values up to the leader's raft
    // index.
    RaftIndexNotUpToDate,
}
```

```csharp
public class DatabaseRecord
{
    // Field holding the database state, a DatabaseStateStatus value of Normal or RestoreInProgress.
    DatabaseStateStatus DatabaseState;
    // Field holding an etag value that the backup task reads from the database record as the last raft index for a
    // backup.
    long EtagForBackup;
    // List of GenAiConfiguration tasks defined for the database; initialized to an empty list.
    List<GenAiConfiguration> GenAis;
    // Field holding the count of cluster transaction commands that were truncated.
    long TruncatedClusterTransactionCommandsCount;
    // Returns true when the Sharding configuration has a Shards collection with at least one entry.
    bool IsSharded { get; }
    // Adds or replaces the analyzer definition in the Analyzers dictionary under its name, creating the dictionary
    // (case-insensitive) if it is null.
    void AddAnalyzer(AnalyzerDefinition definition);
    // Adds or updates an index definition in the record, validating schema collections, skipping if unchanged or
    // the existing index lock mode is LockedIgnore, and throwing IndexAlreadyExistException on LockedError.
    void AddIndex(IndexDefinition definition, string source, DateTime createdAt, long raftIndex, int revisionsToKeep, IndexDeploymentMode globalDeploymentMode);
    // Adds the auto index definition to the record with the current UTC time, raft index 0 and parallel deployment
    // mode.
    void AddIndex(AutoIndexDefinition definition);
    // Adds or replaces the sorter definition in the Sorters dictionary under its name, creating the dictionary
    // (case-insensitive) if it is null.
    void AddSorter(SorterDefinition definition);
    // Removes the analyzer with the given name from the Analyzers dictionary, if the dictionary exists.
    void DeleteAnalyzer(string sorterName);
    // Removes the named index from the index collections of the record; does nothing if its lock mode is
    // LockedIgnore and throws IndexDeletionException if LockedError.
    void DeleteIndex(string name);
    // Removes the periodic backup configuration with the given task id and returns it, or returns null if not
    // found; throws InvalidOperationException for a server-wide backup task.
    PeriodicBackupConfiguration DeletePeriodicBackupConfiguration(long backupTaskId);
    // Removes the sorter with the given name from the Sorters dictionary, if the dictionary exists.
    void DeleteSorter(string sorterName);
    // Throws ArgumentException for an empty name and InvalidOperationException if the name starts with the server-
    // wide backup prefix or is already used by another ongoing task of the record.
    void EnsureTaskNameIsNotUsed(string taskName);
    // Returns the combined count of static indexes and auto indexes in the record.
    int GetIndexesCount();
    // Returns true if the Topology has nodes, or if every shard in the Sharding configuration has a topology with
    // nodes; otherwise false.
    bool ValidateTopologyNodes();
}
```

```csharp
public class DatabaseTopology : IDynamicJson
{
    // Returns replication destinations for the given node tag: none for a promotable, only its mentor for a rehab,
    // otherwise other members plus promotables and rehabs assigned to it, skipping nodes being deleted or absent
    // from the cluster topology.
    List<ReplicationNode> GetDestinations(string myTag, string databaseName, Dictionary<string, DeletionInProgressStatus> deletionInProgress, ClusterTopology clusterTopology, RachisState state);
    // Returns true if the node tag is among the topology Members, Rehabs or Promotables.
    bool RelevantFor(string nodeTag);
    // Removes the node tag from Members, Promotables and Rehabs and from the demotion reasons, promotable status,
    // predefined mentors and priority order.
    void RemoveFromTopology(string delDbFromNode);
    // Returns the tag of the node responsible for the given task, or null if the state is Candidate or Passive;
    // considers mentor node, last responsible node and task key, filling the explanations list.
    string WhoseTaskIsIt(RachisState state, IDatabaseTask task, Func<string> getLastResponsibleNode, List<string> explanations);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class ShardBucketMigration : IDatabaseTask
{
    // Holds the nullable index at which the migration was confirmed; used in the description of Moved and
    // OwnershipTransferred migrations.
    long? ConfirmationIndex;
    // Holds the list of destinations that confirmed receiving the migrated bucket; initialized to an empty list.
    List<string> ConfirmedDestinations;
    // Holds the list of locations where the source bucket data was confirmed as cleaned up; initialized to an
    // empty list.
    List<string> ConfirmedSourceCleanup;
    // Holds the number of the shard to which the bucket is migrated.
    int DestinationShard;
    // Holds the last change vector from the source shard recorded for the migration, as a string.
    string LastSourceChangeVector;
    // Holds the index of the migration; used in the task key and the task name.
    long MigrationIndex;
    // Holds the number of the shard from which the bucket is migrated.
    int SourceShard;
    // Returns the task name, in the form Bucket N migration from SOURCE to DESTINATION @ MigrationIndex.
    string GetDefaultTaskName();
    // Returns the value of the MentorNode field.
    string GetMentorNode();
    // Returns a hash combining source shard, destination shard, bucket and migration index, computed once and
    // cached.
    UInt64 GetTaskKey();
    // Returns false.
    bool IsResourceIntensive();
}
```

## Types of the Sparrow and Raven.Embedded assemblies that have no dedicated article

The client packages use the `Sparrow` assembly (JSON, size and utility types that appear in the signatures of the client) and the `Raven.Embedded` assembly (running a server inside your process). The types below are public in those assemblies and are not described in an article.

### `Sparrow`

```csharp
// Interface that exposes a read-only IsDisposed flag so callers can query whether an object has been disposed.
public interface IDisposableQueryable
{
    // Gets whether the object has been disposed.
    bool IsDisposed { get; }
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for enumerating a sequence of read-only byte spans with a count, reset and forward movement.

{
    // Gets the number of spans in the sequence.
    int Count { get; }
    // Advances to the next item and returns true with that read-only byte span in result, or false when there are
    // no more.
    bool MoveNext(out ReadOnlySpan<byte> result);
    // Resets the enumeration to its start.
    void Reset();
}
```

```csharp

{

    // Returns whether the item at index i is null.
    bool IsNull(int i);
}
```

```csharp
}
```

```csharp
// Stream wrapper that copies the most recently read bytes, up to a 4096-byte window, into a context memory
// buffer so the last data read can be retrieved later.
public sealed class PeepingTomStream : IDisposable
{
    // Creates the wrapper over the given stream and takes a memory buffer from the given JSON operation context.
    PeepingTomStream(Stream stream, JsonOperationContext context);
    // Constant 4096 giving the size in bytes of the window of recently read data that is retained.
    const int BufferWindowSize;
    // Returns the memory buffer to the context. It does not dispose the wrapped stream.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns a new byte array with the recently read data, up to the last 4096 bytes, trimmed to start at a byte
    // whose high bit is not set; returns an empty array if none is found.
    byte[] PeepInReadStream();
    // Reads from the wrapped stream into the buffer, records the bytes read into the retained window, and returns
    // the number of bytes read.
    int Read(Span<byte> buffer);
    // Asynchronously reads from the wrapped stream into the buffer, records the bytes read into the retained
    // window, and returns the number of bytes read.
    Task<int> ReadAsync(Memory<byte> buffer, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
/// An optimized representation of a substring.
public struct StringSegment : IEquatable<StringSegment>, IEquatable<string>
{
    // Creates a segment covering the entire given string, with offset 0 and length equal to the string length, or
    // 0 if the string is null.
    StringSegment(string buffer);
    // Creates a segment over the given string starting at offset with the given length; throws if the buffer is
    // null or the offset or length is out of range.
    StringSegment(string buffer, int offset, int length);
    /// A 'StringSegment' for 'Empty'.
    static StringSegment Empty;
    /// Gets the 'String' buffer for this 'StringSegment'.
    string Buffer { get; }
    /// Gets whether this 'StringSegment' contains a valid value.
    bool HasValue { get; }
    /// Gets the length of this 'StringSegment'.
    int Length { get; }
    /// Gets the offset within the buffer for this 'StringSegment'.
    int Offset { get; }
    /// Gets the value of this segment as a 'String'.
    string Value { get; }
    /// Gets a 'ReadOnlyMemory`1' from the current 'StringSegment'.
    ReadOnlyMemory<Char> AsMemory();
    /// Gets a 'ReadOnlySpan`1' from the current 'StringSegment'.
    ReadOnlySpan<Char> AsSpan();
    /// Compares substrings of two specified 'StringSegment' objects using the specified rules, and returns an
    ///    integer that indicates their relative position in the sort order.
    static int Compare(StringSegment a, StringSegment b, StringComparison comparisonType);
    /// Checks if the end of this 'StringSegment' matches the specified 'String' when compared using the specified
    ///    'comparisonType'.
    /// Throws ArgumentNullException: 'text' is null.
    bool EndsWith(string text, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns true if obj is a non-null StringSegment equal to this segment, otherwise false.
    bool Equals(object obj);
    /// Indicates whether the current object is equal to another object of the same type.
    bool Equals(StringSegment other);
    /// Indicates whether the current object is equal to another object of the same type.
    bool Equals(StringSegment other, StringComparison comparisonType);
    /// Determines whether two specified 'StringSegment' objects have the same value. A parameter specifies the
    ///    culture, case, and sort rules used in the comparison.
    static bool Equals(StringSegment a, StringSegment b, StringComparison comparisonType);
    /// Checks if the specified 'String' is equal to the current 'StringSegment'.
    bool Equals(string text);
    /// Checks if the specified 'String' is equal to the current 'StringSegment'.
    /// Throws ArgumentNullException: 'text' is null.
    bool Equals(string text, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the hash code of the segment string value, or 0 if the value is null.
    int GetHashCode();
    /// Gets the zero-based index of the first occurrence of the character 'c' in this 'StringSegment'. The search
    ///    starts at 'start' and examines a specified number of 'count' character positions.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'start' or 'count' is less than zero, or 'start' + 'count' is greater
    ///    than 'Length'.
    int IndexOf(Char c, int start, int count);
    /// Gets the zero-based index of the first occurrence of the character 'c' in this 'StringSegment'. The search
    ///    starts at 'start'.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'start' is greater than or equal to 'Length' or less than zero.
    int IndexOf(Char c, int start);
    /// Gets the zero-based index of the first occurrence of the character 'c' in this 'StringSegment'.
    int IndexOf(Char c);
    /// Reports the zero-based index of the first occurrence in this instance of any character in a specified array
    ///    of Unicode characters. The search starts at a specified character position and examines a specified number
    ///    of character positions.
    /// Throws ArgumentNullException: 'anyOf' is null.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'startIndex' or 'count' is less than zero, or 'startIndex' + 'count' is
    ///    greater than 'Length'.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex, int count);
    /// Reports the zero-based index of the first occurrence in this instance of any character in a specified array
    ///    of Unicode characters. The search starts at a specified character position.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'startIndex' is greater than or equal to 'Length' or less than zero.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex);
    /// Reports the zero-based index of the first occurrence in this instance of any character in a specified array
    ///    of Unicode characters.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf);
    /// Indicates whether the specified 'StringSegment' is null or an Empty string.
    static bool IsNullOrEmpty(StringSegment value);
    /// Reports the zero-based index position of the last occurrence of a specified Unicode character within this
    ///    instance.
    int LastIndexOf(Char value);
    /// Checks if the beginning of this 'StringSegment' matches the specified 'String' when compared using the
    ///    specified 'comparisonType'.
    /// Throws ArgumentNullException: 'text' is null.
    bool StartsWith(string text, StringComparison comparisonType);
    /// Retrieves a 'StringSegment' that represents a substring from this 'StringSegment'. The 'StringSegment' starts
    ///    at the position specified by 'offset'.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'offset' is greater than or equal to 'Length' or less than zero.
    StringSegment Subsegment(int offset);
    /// Retrieves a 'StringSegment' that represents a substring from this 'StringSegment'. The 'StringSegment' starts
    ///    at the position specified by 'offset' and has the specified 'length'.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'offset' or 'length' is less than zero, or 'offset' + 'length' is greater
    ///    than 'Length'.
    StringSegment Subsegment(int offset, int length);
    /// Retrieves a substring from this 'StringSegment'. The substring starts at the position specified by 'offset'
    ///    and has the remaining length.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'offset' is greater than or equal to 'Length' or less than zero.
    string Substring(int offset);
    /// Retrieves a substring from this 'StringSegment'. The substring starts at the position specified by 'offset'
    ///    and has the specified 'length'.
    /// Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException: 'offset' or 'length' is less than zero, or 'offset' + 'length' is greater
    ///    than 'Length'.
    string Substring(int offset, int length);
    /// Returns the 'String' represented by this 'StringSegment' or String.Empty if the 'StringSegment' does not
    ///    contain a value.
    string ToString();
    /// Removes all leading and trailing whitespaces.
    StringSegment Trim();
    /// Removes all trailing whitespaces.
    StringSegment TrimEnd();
    /// Removes all leading whitespaces.
    StringSegment TrimStart();
}
```

```csharp
// Holds the address and size of an unmanaged memory block and exposes it as a Memory of bytes.
public sealed class UnmanagedMemory
{
    // Creates an instance storing the given address and size.
    UnmanagedMemory(Byte* address, int size);
    // The pointer to the start of the unmanaged memory block.
    Byte* Address;
    // The size in bytes of the unmanaged memory block.
    int Size;
    // Gets a Memory of bytes over the block, created on first access through an unmanaged memory manager and then
    // cached.
    Memory<byte> Memory { get; }
```

```csharp
// Read-only struct wrapping a byte pointer, supporting addition of an int offset.
public struct UnmanagedPointer
{
    // Creates a pointer wrapper holding the given address.
    UnmanagedPointer(Byte* address);
    // The wrapped byte pointer.
    Byte* Address;
}
```
