```csharp
// Read-only struct holding either a byte pointer with a length or an inline long or double value (length -1),
// and providing span, memory and stream conversions.
public struct UnmanagedSpan
{
    // Creates an instance storing the long value and sets Length to -1.
    UnmanagedSpan(long l);
    // Creates an instance storing the double value and sets Length to -1.
    UnmanagedSpan(double d);
    // Creates an instance with the given address and length.
    UnmanagedSpan(Byte* address, int length);
    // Creates an instance with the address of the given pointer and the given length.
    UnmanagedSpan(UnmanagedPointer pointer, int length);
    // The byte pointer to the start of the span; it shares storage with Long and Double.
    Byte* Address;
    // The double value stored in the same eight bytes as Address and Long.
    double Double;
    // Static instance with a null address and length 0.
    static UnmanagedSpan Empty;
    // The length in bytes of the span, or -1 when the instance holds a long or double value.
    int Length;
    // The long value stored in the same eight bytes as Address and Double.
    long Long;
    // Returns a Memory of bytes over the address and length through an unmanaged memory manager.
    Memory<byte> AsMemory();
    // Returns a span starting at Address plus offset with length reduced by offset.
    UnmanagedSpan Slice(int offset);
    // Returns a span starting at Address plus position with the given length.
    UnmanagedSpan Slice(int position, int length);
    // Returns a ReadOnlySpan of bytes over the address and length.
    ReadOnlySpan<byte> ToReadOnlySpan();
    // Returns a Span of bytes over the address and length.
    Span<byte> ToSpan();
    // Returns an UnmanagedMemoryStream over the address and length.
    UnmanagedMemoryStream ToStream();
    // Returns the long value as text when Length is -1; otherwise returns the address in hexadecimal and the
    // length.
    string ToString();
    // Returns an empty string when Length is 0; otherwise decodes the bytes as UTF-8 and returns the string.
    string ToStringValue();
}
```

### `Sparrow.Collections`

```csharp
// Thread-safe set of items backed by a concurrent dictionary, with an interlocked counter used for the
// emptiness check.
public sealed class ConcurrentSet<T> : IEnumerable<T>
{
    // Creates an empty set using the default equality comparer.
    ConcurrentSet();
    // Creates an empty set using the given equality comparer.
    ConcurrentSet(IEqualityComparer<T> comparer);
    // Gets the number of items in the set.
    int Count { get; }
    // Gets whether the set has no items, based on an interlocked counter of additions and removals.
    bool IsEmpty { get; }
    // Adds the item if it is not already present; does nothing otherwise.
    void Add(T item);
    // Removes all items and resets the emptiness counter.
    void Clear();
    // Returns whether the item is in the set.
    bool Contains(T item);
    // Returns an enumerator over the items in the set.
    IEnumerator<T> GetEnumerator();
    // Returns the item count formatted with thousands separators using the invariant culture.
    string ToString();
    // Adds the item and returns true if it was added, or false if it was already present.
    bool TryAdd(T item);
    // Removes the item and returns true if it was removed, or false if it was not present.
    bool TryRemove(T item);
    // Adds every item from the given sequence to the set; throws ArgumentNullException if the sequence is null.
    void UnionWith(IEnumerable<T> other);
}
```

```csharp
// Array-backed list implementing IList that provides by-reference element access, version-checked enumeration
// and several clearing and trimming helpers; Insert is not supported.
public sealed class FastList<T> : IList<T>
{
    // Creates an empty list with no backing storage allocated.
    FastList();
    // Creates an empty list with the given initial capacity; throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if capacity is
    // negative.
    FastList(int capacity);
    // Gets or sets the size of the backing array; the setter throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if the value is
    // less than the item count and reallocates the array otherwise.
    int Capacity { get; set; }
    // Gets the number of items in the list.
    int Count { get; }
    // Always returns false.
    bool IsReadOnly { get; }
    // Appends the item to the end, growing the backing array when it is full.
    void Add(T item);
    // Appends the item, passed by readonly reference, to the end, growing the backing array when it is full.
    void AddByRef(ref T item);
    // Returns a Span over the backing array limited to the current item count, sharing storage with the list.
    Span<T> AsUnsafeSpan();
    /// Removes all objects from the Stack but clearing the backing array unless the type is native.
    void Clear();
    // Returns whether the item is found in the list using the default equality comparison.
    bool Contains(T item);
    // Copies the items into dest, enlarging its capacity if needed, and sets the destination size to match.
    void CopyTo(FastList<T> dest);
    // Copies the items into the given array starting at arrayIndex.
    void CopyTo(T[] array, int arrayIndex);
    // Returns a reference to the element at the given unsigned index of the backing array, without a check against
    // the item count.
    T& GetAsRef(UInt32 index);
    // Returns a reference to the element at the given index of the backing array, without a check against the item
    // count.
    T& GetAsRef(int index);
    // Returns a struct enumerator over the items; it throws InvalidOperationException if the list is modified
    // during enumeration.
    FastList<T>.Enumerator<T> GetEnumerator();
    // Returns the zero-based index of the first matching item, or -1 if not found.
    int IndexOf(T item);
    // Always throws NotSupportedException.
    void Insert(int index, T item);
    // Removes the first matching item and returns true, or returns false if it is not found.
    bool Remove(T item);
    // Removes the item at index, shifting later items down; throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if the index is out
    // of range.
    void RemoveAt(int index);
    // Removes and returns the last item, or returns the default value if the list is empty.
    T RemoveLast();
    // Removes count items starting at index; throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if the range is invalid, and does
    // nothing if count is zero or less.
    void RemoveRange(int index, int count);
    // Sorts the items in the list using the given comparer.
    void Sort(IComparer<T> comparer);
    // Sorts the items in the list using the given struct sorter.
    void Sort<TSorter>(ref Sorter<T, TSorter> sorter);
    // Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException for the given index.
    void ThrowWhenIndexIsOutOfRange(int index);
    // Returns a new array containing a copy of the items.
    T[] ToArray();
    // Reduces the item count to size and clears the removed slots; throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if size is
    // negative or greater than the count.
    void Trim(int size);
    /// This method is like Clear but will not release the references contained in it; therefore the garbage
    ///    collector will not collect those objects even if they are not being used.
    void WeakClear();
}
```

```csharp
// Array-backed generic stack with by-reference push and peek, try-style operations and version-checked
// enumeration.
public sealed class FastStack<T> : IEnumerable<T>
{
    // Creates an empty stack.
    FastStack();
    // Creates an empty stack with the given initial capacity; throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if capacity is
    // negative.
    FastStack(int capacity);
    // Gets the number of items in the stack.
    int Count { get; }
    /// Removes all objects from the Stack but clearing the backing array unless the type is native.
    void Clear();
    // Returns whether the item is found in the stack using the default equality comparison.
    bool Contains(T item);
    // Appends all items of the source stack to this stack, growing the array if needed.
    void CopyFrom(FastStack<T> srcStack);
    // Copies the items into the array starting at arrayIndex, with the top item first, in the same order Pop would
    // return them.
    void CopyTo(T[] array, int arrayIndex);
    // Returns a struct enumerator that iterates from the top of the stack to the bottom and throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the stack is modified.
    FastStack<T>.Enumerator<T> GetEnumerator();
    // Returns the top item without removing it; throws InvalidOperationException if the stack is empty.
    T Peek();
    // Removes and returns the top item; throws InvalidOperationException if the stack is empty.
    T Pop();
    // Pushes the item, passed by readonly reference, onto the stack, doubling the array when full.
    void Push(ref T item);
    // Reserves a new top slot, doubling the array when full, and returns a reference to it.
    T& PushByRef();
    // Returns a new array with the items ordered from top to bottom, or an empty array if the stack is empty.
    T[] ToArray();
    // Returns a reference to the top item; throws InvalidOperationException if the stack is empty.
    T& TopByRef();
    // Returns true with the top item in result, or false with the default value if the stack is empty.
    bool TryPeek(out T result);
    // Returns true with the item at the given depth from the top in result, or false with the default value if
    // depth is not positive or exceeds the count.
    bool TryPeek(int depth, out T result);
    // Removes the top item and returns true with it in result, or returns false with the default value if the
    // stack is empty.
    bool TryPop(out T result);
    /// This method is like Clear but will not release the references contained in it; therefore the garbage
    ///    collector will not collect those objects even if they are not being used.
    void WeakClear();
}
```

```csharp
// Arena allocator that hands out power-of-two sized blocks from native memory buffers and recycles freed
// blocks until the arena is reset or disposed.
public sealed class ArenaMemoryAllocator : IDisposable
{
    // Creates an allocator with a native buffer of the given initial size (default 1 MB), storing the low memory
    // flag.
    ArenaMemoryAllocator(SharedMultipleUseFlag lowMemoryFlag, int initialSize);
    // When true, new arena buffers are sized to the next power of two of the request instead of a larger multiple.
    bool AvoidOverAllocation;
    // Counter of bytes allocated from the arena; it decreases when the last block is returned and is set to zero
    // by ResetArena and by RenewArena when it allocates.
    long TotalUsed;
    // Total size of the current arena buffer plus all older buffers still held.
    long Allocated { get; }
    // Returns a block of the requested size rounded up to a power of two, reusing a freed block when available and
    // growing the arena when needed. Throws if disposed, reset without renew, or size is negative or above 1 GB.
    AllocatedMemoryData Allocate(int size);
    // Releases the allocator's native buffers by calling Dispose with true.
    void Dispose();
    // Frees the older buffers and the current buffer once, on the first call, and suppresses finalization.
    void Dispose(bool disposing);
    // Tries to enlarge an allocation in place by the given amount, rounded to a power of two; returns false if it
    // is not the last block, exceeds the maximum size, lacks room, or a freed block of that size exists.
    bool GrowAllocation(AllocatedMemoryData allocation, int sizeIncrease);
    // Allocates a new native buffer of the current allocated size if the arena was reset and holds none, and
    // clears used counters.
    void RenewArena();
    // Rewinds the arena and clears the freed-block lists, frees older buffers, and may free the current buffer to
    // shrink it after low usage or enlarge it after high usage; RenewArena then reallocates it.
    void ResetArena();
    // Returns an allocation to the arena by moving the current pointer back if it is the last block, otherwise
    // adding it to a per-size freed list. Does nothing if disposed.
    void Return(AllocatedMemoryData allocation);
    // Returns a string with the human-readable allocated size and used size.
    string ToString();
}
```

```csharp
// Blittable JSON text writer that buffers output in a recyclable memory stream and copies it asynchronously to
// an output stream.
public sealed class AsyncBlittableJsonTextWriter : AbstractBlittableJsonTextWriter, IAsyncDisposable
{
    // Creates a writer over the given output stream; throws ArgumentNullException if the stream is null.
    AsyncBlittableJsonTextWriter(JsonOperationContext context, Stream stream, CancellationToken cancellationToken);
    // True when the internal buffer length is more than half its capacity.
    bool ShouldFlushAsync { get; }
    // Disposes the writer, flushes remaining buffered data to the output stream, and flushes the output stream if
    // anything was written.
    ValueTask DisposeAsync();
    // Copies buffered data to the output stream, clears the buffer, and returns the number of bytes written (0 if
    // empty).
    ValueTask<long> FlushAsync(CancellationToken token);
    // Flushes to the output stream only if the buffer is more than half full; returns the bytes written, or 0 if
    // no flush was needed.
    ValueTask<long> MaybeFlushAsync(CancellationToken token);
    // Flushes the buffer, then reads the given stream in chunks and flushes each chunk to the output until the
    // stream is exhausted.
    ValueTask WriteStreamAsync(Stream stream, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
// Builds a blittable JSON document from a JSON parser, writing parsed objects, arrays and values through a
// blittable writer.
public sealed class BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder : AbstractBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder
{
    // Creates a builder with the given context, parser state, reader, optional writer and optional modifier;
    // creates a new writer if none is supplied.
    BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder(JsonOperationContext context, JsonParserState state, IJsonParser reader, BlittableWriter<UnmanagedWriteBuffer> writer, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier);
    // Creates a builder with the given usage mode and debug tag, then calls Renew with them.
    BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder(JsonOperationContext context, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, string debugTag, IJsonParser reader, JsonParserState state, BlittableWriter<UnmanagedWriteBuffer> writer, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier);
    // Creates a builder with the given state, usage mode, debug tag, reader and optional writer, then calls Renew.
    BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder(JsonOperationContext context, JsonParserState state, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, string debugTag, IJsonParser reader, BlittableWriter<UnmanagedWriteBuffer> writer);
    // Optional modifier invoked while building to observe objects and property names.
    IBlittableDocumentModifier _modifier;
    // Property name and handler pair; when set in streaming mode, the handler receives string data of the first
    // property with that name.
    ValueTuple<string, Action<UnmanagedWriteBuffer>> PropertyToWatchForStreaming;
    // Returns the current size in bytes of the underlying writer. Throws if disposed.
    int SizeInBytes { get; }
    // Creates a reader for the built root array by reading the document and its array holder property; sets
    // NoCache from the argument and throws InvalidOperationException if no array is found.
    BlittableJsonReaderArray CreateArrayReader(bool noCache);
    // Returns a reader object over the document written by the underlying writer.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CreateReader();
    // Disposes the underlying writer and base builder state, once.
    void Dispose();
    // Writes the document metadata for the last written root value and token to the writer.
    void FinalizeDocument();
    // Continues parsing and building; returns false if there is nothing to do or more input is needed, true when
    // the current object or document is complete.
    bool Read();
    // Queues reading a root array document, which is stored under a placeholder property named underscore.
    void ReadArrayDocument();
    // Queues reading a nested object from the parser.
    void ReadNestedObject();
    // Queues reading a root object document.
    void ReadObjectDocument();
    // Queues reading a single property name and value, treating the read as partial.
    void ReadProperty();
    // Resets state, sets the debug tag and usage mode, rebinds the read function, renews the writer and resets the
    // modifier.
    void Renew(string debugTag, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode);
    // Clears the debug tag, mode, read function and building state, and resets the writer.
    void Reset();
    // Returns the string Building json for followed by the debug tag.
    string ToString();
}
```

```csharp
// Flags enum selecting how the builder writes data, such as validation and string compression.
public enum BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode
{
    // No flags; value 0.
    None,
    // Flag value 1; floating point values are validated while building.
    ValidateDouble,
    // Flag value 2; strings are compressed when written.
    CompressStrings,
    // Combination of ValidateDouble and CompressStrings.
    ToDisk,
    // Flag value 4; small strings are compressed when written.
    CompressSmallStrings,
    // Flag value 32; reads with the streaming behavior, which requires an unmanaged JSON parser, and writes
    // nothing.
    ForStreaming,
}
```

```csharp
// Struct enumerator over the items of a blittable JSON array, returning each element by index.

    BlittableJsonArrayEnumerator(BlittableJsonReaderArray reader);

    // Does nothing.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns a copy of this enumerator.

    // Returns a copy of this enumerator.
    IEnumerator<object> GetEnumerator();
    // Advances the position and returns true if it is still less than the array count.

    // Sets the position back to before the first element.

}
```

```csharp
// Reader over a vector stored in blittable format, exposing its element type, element size and length, and
// reading elements as typed arrays or converted values.
public sealed class BlittableJsonReaderVector : BlittableJsonReaderBase
{
    // Creates a vector reader over the given memory; throws ArgumentException if the buffer size is 0.
    BlittableJsonReaderVector(Byte* mem, int bufferSize, JsonOperationContext context);
    // Holds a dynamic JSON array of modifications for the vector; not set by the reader itself.
    DynamicJsonArray Modifications;
    // Size in bytes of one vector element, taken from the vector header.
    int ElementSize { get; }
    // Number of elements in the vector, taken from the header.
    int Length { get; }
    // The BlittableVectorType of the vector's elements, taken from the header.
    BlittableVectorType Type { get; }
    // Returns true if the vector element type exactly matches the type argument.
    bool IsOfType<T>();
    // Returns true if the vector element type can be read directly as the type argument, such as signed and
    // unsigned types of the same size.
    bool IsTypeCompatibleForDirectRead<T>();
    // Returns the vector memory as a read-only span of the type argument; throws InvalidOperationException if the
    // element type does not match exactly.
    ReadOnlySpan<T> ReadArray<T>();
    // Returns an enumerator that converts each stored element to the requested numeric type.
    BlittableJsonReaderVector.EnumerableAs<TOut> ReadAs<TOut>();
    /// Useful when we've to copy memory without known type.
    ReadOnlySpan<byte> ReadUnderlyingMemory();
    // Returns true and outputs a span over the data if the type argument is compatible for direct read; otherwise
    // outputs an empty span and returns false.
    bool TryReadArray<T>(out ReadOnlySpan<T> span);
}
```

```csharp
// Struct enumerator that reads vector elements and converts each to the requested unmanaged numeric type.

{

    // NotSupportedException for an unknown type.
    EnumerableAs(Byte* dataStart, int dataLength, int elementSize, BlittableVectorType type);
    // Number of elements, computed as data length divided by element size.
    int Count { get; }
    // Returns the current element converted to the output type.
    TOut Current { get; }
    // Does nothing.

    // Advances the position and returns true if it still lies within the data length.
    bool MoveNext();
    // Sets the position back to before the first element.
    void Reset();
}
```

```csharp
// Flags enum of byte values describing the type of a blittable value, with additional bits for offset size and
// property id size.
public enum BlittableJsonToken
{
    // Value 0x01; an object.
    StartObject,
    // Value 0x02; an array.
    StartArray,
    // Value 0x03; an integer.
    Integer,
    // Value 0x04; a lazily parsed number.
    LazyNumber,
    // Value 0x05; a string.
    String,
    // Value 0x06; a compressed string.
    CompressedString,
    // Value 0x07; a boolean.
    Boolean,
    // Value 0x08; a null value.
    Null,
    // Value 0x09; an embedded blittable document.
    EmbeddedBlittable,
    // Value 0x0A; a raw binary blob.
    RawBlob,
    // Value 0x0B; a vector, written by the builder for numeric arrays under the property named at-vector.
    Vector,
    // Value 0x0C; reserved.
    Reserved3,
    // Value 0x0D; reserved.
    Reserved4,
    // Value 0x0E; reserved.
    Reserved5,
    // Value 0x0F; reserved.
    Reserved6,
    // Mask 0x0F selecting the value type bits.
    TypesMask,
    // Bit 0x10; positions are stored in one byte.
    OffsetSizeByte,
    // Bit 0x20; positions are stored in two bytes.
    OffsetSizeShort,
    // Value 0x30; positions are stored in four bytes.
    OffsetSizeInt,
    // Mask 0x30 selecting the position size bits.
    PositionMask,
    // Bit 0x40; property ids are stored in one byte.
    PropertyIdSizeByte,
    // Bit 0x80; property ids are stored in two bytes.
    PropertyIdSizeShort,
    // Value 0xC0; property ids are stored in four bytes.
    PropertyIdSizeInt,
    // Mask 0xC0 selecting the property id size bits.
    PropertyIdMask,
}
```

```csharp
// Byte-backed enumeration of the element types a blittable vector can store, with each value encoding size and
// signed, unsigned or floating-point kind in its bits.
public enum BlittableVectorType
{
    // Vector element type of 1 byte per element (signed integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    SByte,
    // Vector element type of 2 bytes per element (signed integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    Int16,
    // Vector element type of 4 bytes per element (signed integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    Int32,
    // Vector element type of 8 bytes per element (signed integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    Int64,
    // Vector element type of 1 byte per element (unsigned integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    Byte,
    // Vector element type of 2 bytes per element (unsigned integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    UInt16,
    // Vector element type of 4 bytes per element (unsigned integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    UInt32,
    // Vector element type of 8 bytes per element (unsigned integer), written by WriteVector for the matching CLR
    // type.
    UInt64,
    // Vector element type of 2 bytes per element (floating-point), corresponding to System.Half.
    Half,
    // Vector element type of 4 bytes per element (floating-point), corresponding to float.
    Float,
    // Vector element type of 8 bytes per element (floating-point), corresponding to double.
    Double,
}
```

```csharp
// Writes blittable JSON data into an unmanaged write buffer, tracking the current position and writing values,
// strings, metadata and vectors. Returns a reader over the result and releases its pooled memory on disposal.
public sealed class BlittableWriter<TWriter> : IDisposable
{
    // Creates a writer on the given operation context using the supplied buffer, and obtains a 32-byte scratch
    // memory block from the context.
    BlittableWriter(JsonOperationContext context, TWriter writer);
    // Creates a writer on the given operation context with a default write buffer, and obtains a 32-byte scratch
    // memory block from the context.
    BlittableWriter(JsonOperationContext context);
    // Returns the current write position, the number of bytes written so far in the document.
    int Position { get; }
    // Returns the size in bytes reported by the underlying unmanaged write buffer.
    int SizeInBytes { get; }
    // Static method that sets the thread-local property array offset buffer to null and clears and nulls the
    // thread-local integer buffer.
    static void CleanPropertyArrayOffset();
    // Ensures the written data is a single chunk, wraps it in a new BlittableJsonReaderObject, and resets the
    // writer buffer to its default so the reader owns the data.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CreateReader();
    // Disposes the write buffer and returns the compression and scratch memory blocks to the context, setting them
    // to null.
    void Dispose();
    // Resets the document number, disposes the write buffer, and returns the compression and scratch memory blocks
    // to the context.
    void Reset();
    // Resets the document number and position, disposes the current buffer and gets a new stream from the context
    // sized to the last document size, and reacquires scratch memory if released.
    void ResetAndRenew();
    // Writes the array element count followed by each element offset (relative to the metadata start) and type
    // byte, setting the offset size flag on the list token. Returns the metadata start position.
    int WriteArrayMetadata(FastList<int> positions, FastList<BlittableJsonToken> types, ref BlittableJsonToken listToken);
    // Writes the cached property names, then the root offset and property names position in reverse variable-size
    // form, and finally the document token as one byte.
    void WriteDocumentMetadata(int rootOffset, BlittableJsonToken documentToken);
    // Writes a single zero byte as a null value and returns the position where it was written.
    int WriteNull();
    // Writes the given integer little-endian using 1, 2 or 4 bytes according to sizeOfValue.
    void WriteNumber(int value, int sizeOfValue);
    // Sorts the properties, then writes the property count and, for each property, its offset, property id and
    // type byte, sizing offsets and ids by the largest value. Returns a WriteToken with the metadata start and
    // object token.
    BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.WriteToken WriteObjectMetadata(FastList<AbstractBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.PropertyTag> properties, long firstWrite, int maxPropId);
    // Writes the value as a zigzag variable-size long and returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(long value);
    // Writes the unsigned value as its invariant-culture string form and returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(UInt64 value);
    // Writes the boolean as a variable-size integer, 1 for true and 0 for false, and returns the starting
    // position.
    int WriteValue(bool value);
    // Writes the double as its round-trip invariant-culture string, appending .0 when it has no decimal point or
    // exponent and is not NaN or infinity. Returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(double value);
    // Writes the decimal as its invariant-culture string, appending .0 when it has no decimal point. Returns the
    // starting position.
    int WriteValue(decimal value);
    // Converts the float to double and writes it using the double overload.
    int WriteValue(float value);
    // Writes the inner string value of the LazyNumberValue.
    int WriteValue(LazyNumberValue value);
    // Writes the single byte to the buffer and returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(byte value);
    // Encodes the string as UTF-8, finds escape positions, and writes it with the given usage mode, returning the
    // starting position and the resulting token.
    int WriteValue(string str, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode);
    // Writes the LazyStringValue with no usage mode and no initial compressed size, discarding the token. Returns
    // the starting position.
    int WriteValue(LazyStringValue str);
    // Writes the LazyStringValue; uses the escape-position overload when escape positions exist, otherwise writes
    // the size and raw buffer including its escape data. Returns the starting position and sets the token.
    int WriteValue(LazyStringValue str, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, int? initialCompressedSize);
    // Writes a compressed string as its uncompressed size, compressed size, and compressed buffer including escape
    // data, setting the token to CompressedString. Returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(LazyCompressedStringValue str, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode);
    // Writes the size and buffer, optionally compressing it according to the usage mode or initial compressed
    // size, followed by a zero escape count. Sets the token to String or CompressedString and returns the starting
    // position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, int? initialCompressedSize);
    // Writes the size and buffer, optionally compressed, followed by the escape position count and positions from
    // the list. Sets the token and returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size, FastList<int> escapePositions, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, int? initialCompressedSize);
    // Writes the size as a variable-size int, the buffer bytes, and a zero escape count. Returns the starting
    // position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size);
    // Writes the size, the buffer bytes, and the escape positions from the array, or a zero count when the array
    // is null or empty. Returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size, int[] escapePositions);
    // Writes the size, the buffer bytes, and the escape positions from the list, or a zero count when it is null
    // or empty. Returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size, FastList<int> escapePositions);
    // Writes the size and buffer, optionally compressed, followed by the escape position count and positions from
    // the array, or a zero count when null. Sets the token and returns the starting position.
    int WriteValue(Byte* buffer, int size, int[] escapePositions, out BlittableJsonToken token, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, int? initialCompressedSize);
    // Writes the int as a variable-size value of seven bits per byte, returning the number of bytes written.
    int WriteVariableSizeInt(int value);
    // Writes the int as a variable-size value with the encoded bytes in reverse order, returning the number of
    // bytes written.
    int WriteVariableSizeIntInReverse(int value);
    // Writes the long as a zigzag-encoded variable-size value, returning the number of bytes written.
    int WriteVariableSizeLong(long value);
    // Writes a vector header with the element type matching T and the length, then the elements. Throws
    // NotSupportedException for unsupported element types. Returns the starting position.
    int WriteVector<T>(ReadOnlySpan<T> vector);
}
```

```csharp
/// This class allows us to cache the properties from multiple documents That way, we don't need to recompute the
///    sort order. However, because different documents may have different fields, we're tracking which
///    properties we have seen and which are new. If the order in which we read the properties doesn't match the
///    cached version, we'll clear the cache up to the point the properties match. This is done so we'll not
///    write properties that don't belong to that document, but to previous documents handled in the same batch
public sealed class CachedProperties
{
    // Creates the cache for the given context and obtains its per-core sort cache.
    CachedProperties(JsonOperationContext context);
    // Static field holding the threshold used by NeedClearPropertiesCache; initial value 512.
    static int CachedPropertiesSize;
    // Count of property names discovered so far in the current document.
    int PropertiesDiscovered;
    // Resets the sort caches and clears the known property names, sort order, name-to-id map, counters and
    // document number.
    void Clear();
    // Clears all cached properties and then renews the sort cache.
    void ClearRenew();
    // Returns the cached PropertyName for the given name, registering it if unseen and counting it as discovered,
    // reordering ids if it is out of order.
    CachedProperties.PropertyName GetProperty(LazyStringValue propName);
    // Returns the property name string stored at the given index of the discovered names list.
    LazyStringValue GetProperty(int index);
    // Returns true when the number of discovered properties exceeds CachedPropertiesSize.
    bool NeedClearPropertiesCache();
    // Increments the document number and resets the discovered property count to zero.
    void NewDocument();
    // Obtains the sort cache array from the per-core container, or allocates a new fixed-size array when none is
    // available.
    void Renew();
    // Clears each cached sort and returns the sort cache array to the per-core container, then sets it to null.
    void Reset();
    // Sorts the property tags by name, reusing a cached sort when the same properties appear in the same order,
    // and drops duplicate properties keeping the latest.
    void Sort(FastList<AbstractBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.PropertyTag> properties);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for writers that emit blittable JSON as text, with methods for objects, arrays, property names,
// strings, numbers, booleans, null, dates, commas and newlines.
public interface IBlittableJsonTextWriter
{
    // Writes a boolean value.
    void WriteBool(bool val);
    // Writes a comma separator.
    void WriteComma();
    // Writes a nullable DateTime, taking a flag indicating whether it is UTC, and returns an int.
    int WriteDateTime(DateTime? value, bool isUtc);
    // Writes a DateTime, taking a flag indicating whether it is UTC, and returns an int.
    int WriteDateTime(DateTime value, bool isUtc);
    // Writes a LazyNumberValue as a number.
    void WriteDouble(LazyNumberValue val);
    // Writes a double value.
    void WriteDouble(double val);
    // Writes the end of a JSON array.
    void WriteEndArray();
    // Writes the end of a JSON object.
    void WriteEndObject();
    // Writes a long integer value.
    void WriteInteger(long val);
    // Writes a newline.
    void WriteNewLine();
    // Writes a null value.
    void WriteNull();
    // Writes a BlittableJsonReaderObject.
    void WriteObject(BlittableJsonReaderObject obj);
    // Writes a property name given as a UTF-8 byte span.
    void WritePropertyName(ReadOnlySpan<byte> prop);
    // Writes a property name given as a LazyStringValue.
    void WritePropertyName(LazyStringValue prop);
    // Writes a property name given as a string.
    void WritePropertyName(string prop);
    // Writes a property name given as a StringSegment.
    void WritePropertyName(StringSegment prop);
    // Writes the start of a JSON array.
    void WriteStartArray();
    // Writes the start of a JSON object.
    void WriteStartObject();
    // Writes a string value; the skipEscaping parameter defaults to false.
    void WriteString(string str, bool skipEscaping);
    // Writes a LazyStringValue as a string; the skipEscaping parameter defaults to false.
    void WriteString(LazyStringValue str, bool skipEscaping);
    // Writes a LazyCompressedStringValue as a string.
    void WriteString(LazyCompressedStringValue str);
    // Writes a value of the given BlittableJsonToken type from the supplied object.
    void WriteValue(BlittableJsonToken token, object val);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for unmanaged write buffers that accept bytes, values and spans, report their size in bytes, and
// can expose their content as a single chunk.
public interface IUnmanagedWriteBuffer : IDisposableQueryable, IDisposable
{
    // Gets the number of bytes written to the buffer.
    int SizeInBytes { get; }
    // Ensures the content is a single chunk and stores its pointer and size in the state StringBuffer and
    // StringSize in the UnmanagedWriteBuffer implementation; the stream implementation throws
    // NotSupportedException.
    void EnsureSingleChunk(JsonParserState state);
    // Ensures the content is a single chunk and returns its pointer and size through out parameters; the stream
    // implementation throws NotSupportedException.
    void EnsureSingleChunk(out Byte* ptr, out int size);
    // Writes count bytes from the byte array starting at the given index.
    void Write(byte[] buffer, int start, int count);
    // Writes the given number of bytes from the unmanaged pointer.
    void Write(Byte* buffer, int length);
    // Writes an unmanaged value passed by reference.
    void Write<T>(ref T value);
    // Writes the elements of an unmanaged read-only span passed by reference.
    void Write<T>(ref ReadOnlySpan<T> buffer);
    // Writes a single byte.
    void WriteByte(byte data);
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract pool of JsonOperationContext instances using per-core and global caches. It disposes contexts when
// memory is low, when they exceed size limits, or after idle periods, and reacts to low memory notifications.
public abstract class JsonContextPoolBase<T> : ILowMemoryHandler, IMemoryContextPool
{
    // Allocates a context of type T and returns it as a JsonOperationContext together with the IDisposable that
    // returns it to the pool.
    IDisposable AllocateOperationContext(out JsonOperationContext context);
    // Throws if the pool was disposed, then takes a context from the per-core cache or global stack, or creates a
    // new one. Returns an IDisposable that returns the context to the pool.
    IDisposable AllocateOperationContext(out T context);
    // Has an empty body with a comment stating there is nothing to do currently.
    void Clean();
    // Cancels the pool, stops the cleanup timer and disposes all pooled contexts; does nothing if already
    // disposed.
    void Dispose();
    // Raises the low memory flag and increments the generation; on ExtremelyLow severity it also disposes the
    // pooled contexts that are not in use.
    void LowMemory(LowMemorySeverity lowMemorySeverity);
    // Lowers the low memory flag and the extremely-low-memory flag.
    void LowMemoryOver();
}
```

```csharp
// Buffer of unmanaged memory with address, size and Valid and Used counters, used when reading data into the
// context.
public sealed class JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer
{
    // Creates the buffer over the given address and size, sets Valid and Used to zero.
    MemoryBuffer(Byte* address, int size, int generation, JsonOperationContext context);
    // Pointer to the start of the buffer memory.
    Byte* Address;
    // Constant equal to 32 kilobytes, the default buffer size.
    const int DefaultSize;
    // The UnmanagedMemory wrapping the buffer address and size.
    UnmanagedMemory Memory;
    // Size of the buffer in bytes.
    int Size;
    // Number of bytes of the buffer already consumed; advanced by the parser buffer offset and reset to 0 when
    // refilled.
    int Used;
    // Number of bytes in the buffer that hold valid data; set to the count read when refilled.
    int Valid;
}
```

```csharp
// Disposable that returns the allocated memory to the parent operation context on disposal.
public struct JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer.ReturnBuffer : IDisposable
{
    // Stores the allocated memory and parent context; throws ArgumentNullException if allocatedMemory or parent is
    // null, and in debug builds also if buffer is null.
    ReturnBuffer(AllocatedMemoryData allocatedMemory, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer, JsonOperationContext parent);
    // Stores the allocated memory and parent context; throws ArgumentNullException if either is null.
    ReturnBuffer(AllocatedMemoryData allocatedMemory, JsonOperationContext parent);
    // Returns the allocated memory to the parent context and clears it; throws ObjectDisposedException if the
    // parent was disposed. Does nothing if already released.
    void Dispose();
}
```

```csharp
// Sealed class that holds a compressed UTF-8 string buffer with its compressed and uncompressed sizes, and
// decompresses it on demand using LZ4 or small-string compression.
public sealed class LazyCompressedStringValue
{
    // Creates an instance storing the optional cached string, the compressed buffer pointer, both sizes, and the
    // owning JSON operation context.
    LazyCompressedStringValue(string str, Byte* buffer, int uncompressedSize, int compressedSize, JsonOperationContext context);
    // Pointer to the compressed bytes; the escape positions data follows the compressed bytes in the same buffer.
    Byte* Buffer;
    // Size in bytes of the compressed data.
    int CompressedSize;
    // Cached string form; set from the constructor argument or by the first conversion to string, and null before
    // that if none was supplied.
    string String;
    // Size in bytes of the data after decompression.
    int UncompressedSize;
    // Returns a new instance with the same cached string, buffer and sizes, bound to the specified context.
    LazyCompressedStringValue CloneForConcurrentRead(JsonOperationContext context);
    // Decompresses the value into memory obtained from the external context, or from the instance context if null,
    // and returns the allocation. Throws FormatException if the decompressed size differs from UncompressedSize.
    AllocatedMemoryData DecompressToAllocatedMemoryData(JsonOperationContext externalContext);
    // Decompresses the value into an allocated buffer, returns the buffer address and outputs the allocation. Uses
    // the external context if provided, otherwise the instance context.
    Byte* DecompressToTempBuffer(out AllocatedMemoryData allocatedData, JsonOperationContext externalContext);
    // Decompresses the value into an allocated buffer and returns an UnmanagedPointer to its address, outputting
    // the allocation. Uses the external context if provided, otherwise the instance context.
    UnmanagedPointer DecompressToUnmanagedPointer(out AllocatedMemoryData allocatedData, JsonOperationContext externalContext);
    // Returns true if obj is an equal LazyCompressedStringValue, LazyStringValue or string; returns false for null
    // and other types.
    bool Equals(object obj);
    // Returns true if the uncompressed sizes match and the contents are equal, comparing compressed bytes directly
    // when compressed sizes match, otherwise comparing the decompressed data.
    bool Equals(LazyCompressedStringValue other);
    // Returns a hash computed (XXHash32 or XXHash64 by pointer size) over the compressed bytes.
    int GetHashCode();
    // Converts the value to a string and returns the substring starting at the specified index.
    string Substring(int startIndex);
    // Converts the value to a string and returns the substring with the specified start index and length.
    string Substring(int startIndex, int length);
    /// Returns uncompressed data in form of LazyStringValue
    LazyStringValue ToLazyStringValue();
    // Returns the decompressed UTF-8 content as a string, caching it in String.
    string ToString();
}
```

```csharp
// Sealed builder that lets callers incrementally write a blittable JSON document by calling explicit start,
// property, value and end methods on a BlittableWriter.
public sealed class ManualBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder<TWriter> : AbstractBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder
{
    // Creates a builder for the context with the specified usage mode (None if null) and writer (a new
    // BlittableWriter for the context if null).
    ManualBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder(JsonOperationContext context, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode? mode, BlittableWriter<TWriter> writer);
    // Creates a reader over the written document and returns the array held under the root array property. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if no array is found.
    BlittableJsonReaderArray CreateArrayReader();
    // Returns a BlittableJsonReaderObject over the document written by the underlying writer.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CreateReader();
    // Disposes the underlying writer and then the base builder.
    void Dispose();
    // Writes the document metadata using the last written token and its position as the root, then clears the
    // builder state.
    void FinalizeDocument();
    // Sets the usage mode and resets and renews the underlying writer.
    void Reset(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode);
    // Pushes a ReadArrayDocument state for a root array document, registering the root array holder property.
    void StartArrayDocument();
    // Pushes a ReadArray state to begin writing an array.
    void StartWriteArray();
    // Begins writing an object. Throws InvalidOperationException unless the current state is ReadObjectDocument,
    // ReadPropertyValue or ReadArray.
    void StartWriteObject();
    // Pushes a ReadObjectDocument state to begin writing a root object document.
    void StartWriteObjectDocument();
    // Ends the current array, writing its metadata and registering it in the enclosing state. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException if the state is not valid for ending an array.
    void WriteArrayEnd();
    // Writes the blittable document bytes of the specified reader object as an embedded blittable value, using its
    // base pointer and size.
    void WriteEmbeddedBlittableDocument(BlittableJsonReaderObject document);
    // Writes the bytes at the pointer with the given size as an embedded blittable value and registers it in the
    // current state.
    void WriteEmbeddedBlittableDocument(Byte* ptr, int size);
    // Ends the current object, writing its metadata and registering it in the enclosing object or array. Throws
    // InvalidOperationException on an invalid state.
    void WriteObjectEnd();
    // Registers a property name from the string for the next value. Throws InvalidOperationException unless the
    // current state expects a property name.
    void WritePropertyName(string propertyName);
    // Registers the specified property name for the next value. Throws InvalidOperationException unless the
    // current state expects a property name.
    void WritePropertyName(LazyStringValue property);
    // Writes the bytes at the pointer with the given size as a RawBlob value in the current object or array.
    void WriteRawBlob(Byte* ptr, int size);
    // Writes the value according to the given BlittableJsonToken, dispatching to the matching write method;
    // vectors are written by element type. Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException for unsupported tokens or vector
    // types.
    void WriteValue(BlittableJsonToken token, object value);
    // Writes a boolean value in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(bool value);
    // Writes a long value as an Integer token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(long value);
    // Writes a float value as a LazyNumber token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(float value);
    // Writes the value as an Integer token if it is at most long.MaxValue, otherwise as a LazyNumber token.
    void WriteValue(UInt64 value);
    // Writes a double value as a LazyNumber token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(double value);
    // Writes a decimal value as a LazyNumber token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(decimal value);
    // Writes a LazyNumberValue as a LazyNumber token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValue(LazyNumberValue value);
    // Writes a string value using the builder usage mode, which may produce a string or compressed string token.
    void WriteValue(string value);
    // Writes a LazyStringValue in the current object property or array with usage mode None.
    void WriteValue(LazyStringValue value);
    // Writes a LazyCompressedStringValue in the current object property or array with usage mode None.
    void WriteValue(LazyCompressedStringValue value);
    // Writes a null value in the current object property or array.
    void WriteValueNull();
    // Writes the span of unmanaged values as a Vector token in the current object property or array.
    void WriteVector<T>(ReadOnlySpan<T> array);
}
```

```csharp
// Pool of unmanaged memory buffers kept in 32 stacks by power-of-two size class; sizes over 1 MB are allocated
// and freed directly.
public class UnmanagedBuffersPool : IDisposable
{
    // Creates a pool with the logger, debug tag and database name (empty string if null) and initializes 32 free-
    // segment stacks.
    UnmanagedBuffersPool(IRavenLogger logger, string debugTag, string databaseName);
    // Returns a pooled allocation rounded up to a power of two, or a newly allocated one if none is free; sizes
    // over 1 MB are allocated exactly.
    AllocatedMemoryData Allocate(int size);
    // Marks the pool as disposed, frees all pooled memory and suppresses finalization; does nothing if already
    // disposed.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns the total size in bytes of the free segments currently held in the pool.
    long GetAllocatedMemorySize();
    // Returns the number of bits needed to represent the size, or -1 if the size exceeds 1 MB.
    static int GetIndexFromSize(int size);
    // If severity is ExtremelyLow, frees all pooled memory and logs the amount freed at debug level; otherwise
    // does nothing.
    void LowMemory(LowMemorySeverity lowMemorySeverity);
    // Does nothing.
    void LowMemoryOver();
    // Returns the allocation to the pool, or frees it if its size is over 1 MB or the pool is disposed. Throws
    // ArgumentNullException if null.
    void Return(AllocatedMemoryData returned);
}
```

```csharp
// Struct write buffer that accumulates bytes in a linked chain of memory segments allocated from a
// JsonOperationContext.
public struct UnmanagedWriteBuffer : IUnmanagedWriteBuffer
{
    // Creates a buffer whose first segment uses the specified allocated memory, bound to the context.
    UnmanagedWriteBuffer(JsonOperationContext context, AllocatedMemoryData allocatedMemoryData);
    // Returns true if the buffer has no head segment or its head segment address has been cleared by disposal.
    bool IsDisposed { get; }
    // Returns the total number of bytes written; in debug builds throws ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int SizeInBytes { get; }
    // Resets the head segment used and accumulated sizes to zero and drops the link to previous segments.
    void Clear();
    // Copies the written bytes from the given start offset to the pointer, walking segments backwards, and returns
    // the number of bytes copied.
    int CopyTo(int start, Byte* pointer);
    // Copies all written bytes in order to the pointer and returns the number of bytes copied.
    int CopyTo(Byte* pointer);
    // Returns every segment allocation to the context and clears the head; does nothing if already disposed.
    void Dispose();
    // Ensures the data is in a single chunk and sets the state StringBuffer and StringSize to its pointer and
    // size.
    void EnsureSingleChunk(JsonParserState state);
    // Ensures the data is in a single chunk and outputs its pointer and size.
    void EnsureSingleChunk(out Byte* ptr, out int size);
    // Writes count bytes from the array starting at the start index, allocating further segments as needed.
    void Write(byte[] buffer, int start, int count);
    // Writes count bytes from the pointer, allocating further segments as needed; does nothing for count zero.
    void Write(Byte* buffer, int count);
    // Writes the raw bytes of the unmanaged value, allocating a new segment if the current one lacks space.
    void Write<T>(ref T value);
    // Writes the raw bytes of the span elements to the buffer.
    void Write<T>(ref ReadOnlySpan<T> vector);
    // Writes one byte, allocating a new segment if the current one is full.
    void WriteByte(byte data);
}
```

```csharp
// Sealed list-backed collection of objects used to build or modify a JSON array, with tracking of removed
// indexes; disposes any disposable items.
public sealed class DynamicJsonArray : IEnumerable<object>, IDisposable
{
    // Creates an empty array with an empty Items list.
    DynamicJsonArray();
    // Creates an array whose Items contain the elements of the specified collection.
    DynamicJsonArray(IEnumerable collection);
    // List of objects held by the array.
    List<object> Items;
    // Public integer field initialized to 0, used as an index while iterating the items when the array is
    // converted by the object JSON parser.
    int ModificationsIndex;
    // List of indexes recorded by RemoveAt; null until the first removal.
    List<int> Removals;
    // Boolean flag; when set, the parser sets SourceIndex to the original array length instead of -1.
    bool SkipOriginalArray;
    // Index into the source array, initialized to -1.
    int SourceIndex;
    // Returns the number of items in Items.
    int Count { get; }
    // Adds the object to Items; in debug builds throws InvalidOperationException if it is a Raven.Server Document.
    void Add(object obj);
    // Removes all items from Items.
    void Clear();
    // Disposes each item that implements IDisposable.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns the enumerator of Items.
    IEnumerator<object> GetEnumerator();
    // Records the index in the Removals list, creating it if needed, without removing from Items.
    void RemoveAt(int index);
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for a JSON parser that reads tokens, validates floats, produces error state text and accepts a
// string-read callback.
public interface IJsonParser : IDisposable
{
    // Write-only property that sets the delegate called when a string is read, receiving an UnmanagedWriteBuffer
    // and a partial flag.
    OnStringReadDelegate OnStringRead { set; }
    // Returns a string describing the current parser state for error reporting.
    string GenerateErrorState();
    // Reads the next token and returns a boolean indicating whether a token was read.
    bool Read();
    // Validates a floating-point value that was read.
    void ValidateFloat();
}
```

```csharp
// Sealed class holding the current state of a JSON parser: string buffer and size, numeric value, current
// token type, continuation and escape positions.
public sealed class JsonParserState
{
    // Creates a parser state with default field values and an empty escape positions list.
    JsonParserState();
    // Nullable size of the compressed string data; set to null by Reset.
    int? CompressedSize;
    // Continuation marker for a token that was partially read.
    JsonParserTokenContinuation Continuation;
    // Constant 5, the size used per control character item.
    const int ControlCharacterItemSize;
    // Token type currently held by the state.
    JsonParserToken CurrentTokenType;
    // Constant 5, the size used per escape position item.
    const int EscapePositionItemSize;
    // List of integer positions of escaped characters found in the current string.
    FastList<int> EscapePositions;
    // Long value of the current integer token.
    long Long;
    // Pointer to the current string bytes.
    Byte* StringBuffer;
    // Size in bytes of the current string.
    int StringSize;
    // Finds escape positions in the string bytes and stores them in EscapePositions, delegating to
    // StringUtils.FindEscapedPositions.
    void FindEscapedPositions(Byte* str, int len, int previousComputedMaxSize);
    // Clears the buffer pointer, sizes, long value, token type, continuation, escape positions and buffered vector
    // state.
    void Reset();
    // Returns the number of bytes needed to encode the value as a variable-size integer in 7-bit groups.
    static int VariableSizeIntSize(int value);
    // Writes the escape position count and each position as variable-size ints to the buffer and returns the
    // number of bytes written.
    int WriteEscapePositionsTo(Byte* buffer);
    // Writes the value as a variable-size integer to the destination pointer and advances the pointer.
    static void WriteVariableSizeInt(ref Byte* dest, int value);
}
```

```csharp
// Enum of token types that a JSON parser can produce, each a distinct bit value except None, which is 0.
public enum JsonParserToken
{
    // No token; value 0.
    None,
    // Null literal token; value 1 shifted left by 1.
    Null,
    // False literal token; value 1 shifted left by 2.
    False,
    // True literal token; value 1 shifted left by 3.
    True,
    // String token; value 1 shifted left by 4.
    String,
    // Floating-point number token; value 1 shifted left by 5.
    Float,
    // Integer number token; value 1 shifted left by 6.
    Integer,
    // Separator token; value 1 shifted left by 7.
    Separator,
    // Start of an object token; value 1 shifted left by 8.
    StartObject,
    // Start of an array token; value 1 shifted left by 9.
    StartArray,
    // End of an array token; value 1 shifted left by 10.
    EndArray,
    // End of an object token; value 1 shifted left by 11.
    EndObject,
    // Binary blob token; value 1 shifted left by 12.
    Blob,
}
```

```csharp
// Enum of continuation states that record which kind of token the JSON parser was partway through when its
// input buffer ran out.
public enum JsonParserTokenContinuation
{
    // No continuation is pending; the parser reads normally.
    None,
    // Enum value declared in the enum but not referenced anywhere in the source.
    PleaseRefillBuffer,
    // Continuation state for a partially read NaN token.
    PartialNaN,
    // Continuation state for a partially read positive Infinity token.
    PartialPositiveInfinity,
    // Continuation state for a partially read negative Infinity token.
    PartialNegativeInfinity,
    // Continuation state for a partially read null token.
    PartialNull,
    // Continuation state for a partially read true token.
    PartialTrue,
    // Continuation state for a partially read string token.
    PartialString,
    // Continuation state for a partially read number token.
    PartialNumber,
    // Continuation state for a partially read UTF-8 preamble (byte order mark).
    PartialPreamble,
    // Continuation state for a partially read false token.
    PartialFalse,
}
```

```csharp
// JSON parser that walks an in-memory object graph and reports it as a sequence of JSON tokens through the
// parser state.
public sealed class ObjectJsonParser : IJsonParser
{
    // Creates the parser with the given parser state and operation context.
    ObjectJsonParser(JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext ctx);
    // Write-only property whose setter always throws NotSupportedException.
    OnStringReadDelegate OnStringRead { set; }
    // Releases the state buffer back to the context once; later calls do nothing.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns the string form of the last element enumerated from the element stack, which is the bottom-most
    // (first pushed) element, or an empty string if the stack is empty.
    string GenerateErrorState();
    // Advances to the next token of the object graph; throws EndOfStreamException if no elements remain and throws
    // if the parser is disposed.
    bool Read();
    // Returns any state buffer to the context, clears the element stack and seen-values set, and pushes the given
    // root if it is not null.
    void Reset(object root);
    // Does nothing, since all float values are treated as valid.
    void ValidateFloat();
}
```

```csharp
// Delegate type invoked with an unmanaged write buffer and a flag indicating whether the string data is
// partial.
public sealed class OnStringReadDelegate : MulticastDelegate
{
    // Compiler-generated delegate constructor taking a target object and a method pointer.
    OnStringReadDelegate(object object, IntPtr method);
    // Begins asynchronous invocation of the delegate with the buffer, partial flag, callback and state object.
    IAsyncResult BeginInvoke(UnmanagedWriteBuffer buffer, bool partial, AsyncCallback callback, object object);
    // Ends an asynchronous invocation started with BeginInvoke.
    void EndInvoke(IAsyncResult result);
    // Invokes the delegate with the given buffer and partial flag.
    void Invoke(UnmanagedWriteBuffer buffer, bool partial);
}
```

```csharp
// JSON parser that reads UTF-8 bytes from an unmanaged buffer and produces tokens into a JsonParserState,
// resuming across buffer boundaries.
public sealed class UnmanagedJsonParser : IJsonParser
{
    // Creates the parser with the operation context, parser state and debug tag, and allocates an initial write
    // stream from the context.
    UnmanagedJsonParser(JsonOperationContext ctx, JsonParserState state, string debugTag);
    // Static byte array holding the UTF-8 encoding preamble (byte order mark).
    static byte[] Utf8Preamble;
    // Gets the current read position within the input buffer.
    int BufferOffset { get; }
    // Gets the size of the current input buffer in bytes.
    int BufferSize { get; }
    // Write-only property that sets the delegate stored for string-read notifications.
    OnStringReadDelegate OnStringRead { set; }
    // Copies up to count bytes from the unread input buffer to the output pointer and advances the position;
    // returns whether the full count was copied and the number of bytes copied.
    ValueTuple<bool, int> Copy(Byte* output, int count);
    // Writes up to count bytes from the unread input buffer to the stream and advances the position; returns
    // whether the full count was copied and the number of bytes written.
    ValueTuple<bool, int> Copy(Stream dest, int count);
    // Writes the bytes from the given offset up to the current position of the input buffer to the destination
    // stream.
    void CopyParsedChunk(Stream dest, int from);
    // Disposes the internal unmanaged write buffer.
    void Dispose();
    // Returns a string with the current line and character position and the text of the input buffer.
    string GenerateErrorState();
    // Marks that a preamble may precede the next document and replaces the internal write buffer with a new stream
    // of the previous size.
    void NewDocument();
    // Parses the next token from the input buffer into the parser state; returns false when the buffer is
    // exhausted before a token completes.
    bool Read();
    // Sets the input buffer to the unused portion of the memory buffer, from its Used offset for Valid minus Used
    // bytes, and resets the position to zero.
    void SetBuffer(JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer inputBuffer);
    // Sets the input buffer to the memory buffer address plus the given offset with the given size, and resets the
    // position to zero.
    void SetBuffer(JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer inputBuffer, int offset, int size);
    // Sets the input buffer to the given pointer and size, and resets the position to zero.
    void SetBuffer(Byte* inputBuffer, int size);
    // Advances the position by up to count bytes without copying; returns whether the full count was skipped and
    // the number of bytes skipped.
    ValueTuple<bool, int> Skip(int count);
    // Parses the buffered number text as a double and throws InvalidDataException if it cannot be parsed; infinity
    // is accepted only for the Infinity or -Infinity text.
    void ValidateFloat();
}
```

```csharp
// Static helpers for reading tokens, strings, longs, properties, objects and arrays from a stream through an
// UnmanagedJsonParser.
public static class UnmanagedJsonParserHelper
{
    // Reads the next token, refilling the parser buffer from the stream as needed; returns false at end of stream
    // after an EndObject token and throws EndOfStreamException otherwise.
    static bool Read(PeepingTomStream stream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Reads a JSON array from the stream and yields each element as a BlittableJsonReaderObject; throws if the
    // first token is not StartArray or the JSON is invalid.
    static IEnumerable<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ReadArrayToMemory(JsonOperationContext context, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Asynchronously reads a JSON array from the stream and yields each element as a BlittableJsonReaderObject;
    // throws if the first token is not StartArray or the JSON is invalid.
    static IAsyncEnumerable<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ReadArrayToMemoryAsync(JsonOperationContext context, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Asynchronously reads the next token, refilling the parser buffer from the stream as needed; returns false at
    // end of stream after an EndObject token and throws EndOfStreamException otherwise.
    static Task<bool> ReadAsync(PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer, CancellationToken token);
    // Asynchronously reads a JSON array into the builder and returns it as a BlittableJsonReaderArray; throws
    // EndOfStreamException if the stream ends early.
    static ValueTask<BlittableJsonReaderArray> ReadJsonArrayAsync(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder builder, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer, CancellationToken token);
    // Reads the next token and returns its long value; throws if the stream ends or the token is not an integer.
    static long ReadLong(JsonOperationContext context, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Reads a nested object into the builder from the stream and finalizes the document; throws
    // EndOfStreamException if the stream ends early.
    static void ReadObject(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder builder, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Asynchronously reads a nested object into the builder from the stream and finalizes the document; throws
    // EndOfStreamException if the stream ends early.
    static Task ReadObjectAsync(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder builder, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer, CancellationToken token);
    // Reads a property into the builder from the stream and finalizes the document; throws EndOfStreamException if
    // the stream ends early.
    static void ReadProperty(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder builder, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Asynchronously reads a property into the builder from the stream and finalizes the document; throws
    // EndOfStreamException if the stream ends early.
    static Task ReadPropertyAsync(BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder builder, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer, CancellationToken token);
    // Reads the next token and returns its string value, or null for a null token; throws if the stream ends or
    // the token is neither string nor null.
    static string ReadString(JsonOperationContext context, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser, JsonParserState state, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Throws InvalidOperationException with the given message, the parser error state and the last data read from
    // the stream.
    static void ThrowInvalidJson(string msg, PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream, UnmanagedJsonParser parser);
    // Throws InvalidOperationException containing the last data read from the stream.
    static void ThrowInvalidJson(PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream);
    // Throws InvalidDataException stating the response is invalid JSON, with the last data read from the stream.
    static void ThrowInvalidJsonResponse(PeepingTomStream peepingTomStream);
}
```

### `Sparrow.Logging`

```csharp
// Logger abstraction exposing level checks and logging methods for Trace, Debug, Info, Warn, Error and Fatal.
public interface IRavenLogger
{
    // Gets whether the Debug level is enabled.
    bool IsDebugEnabled { get; }
    // Gets whether the Error level is enabled.
    bool IsErrorEnabled { get; }
    // Gets whether the Fatal level is enabled.
    bool IsFatalEnabled { get; }
    // Gets whether the Info level is enabled.
    bool IsInfoEnabled { get; }
    // Gets whether the Trace level is enabled.
    bool IsTraceEnabled { get; }
    // Gets whether the Warn level is enabled.
    bool IsWarnEnabled { get; }
    // Logs a Debug level message.
    void Debug(string message);
    // Logs a Debug level message with format arguments.
    void Debug(string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Debug level message with an exception.
    void Debug(string message, Exception exception);
    // Logs a Debug level message with an exception, message and format arguments.
    void Debug(Exception exception, string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Debug level message with two typed arguments.
    void Debug<TArgument1, TArgument2>(string message, TArgument1 argument1, TArgument2 argument2);
    // Logs a Debug level message with three typed arguments.
    void Debug<TArgument1, TArgument2, TArgument3>(string message, TArgument1 argument1, TArgument2 argument2, TArgument3 argument3);
    // Logs a Error level message.
    void Error(string message);
    // Logs a Error level message with format arguments.
    void Error(string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Error level message with an exception.
    void Error(string message, Exception exception);
    // Logs a Error level message with an exception, message and format arguments.
    void Error(Exception exception, string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Error level message with two typed arguments.
    void Error<TArgument1, TArgument2>(string message, TArgument1 argument1, TArgument2 argument2);
    // Logs a Error level message with three typed arguments.
    void Error<TArgument1, TArgument2, TArgument3>(string message, TArgument1 argument1, TArgument2 argument2, TArgument3 argument3);
    // Logs a Fatal level message.
    void Fatal(string message);
    // Logs a Fatal level message with format arguments.
    void Fatal(string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Fatal level message with an exception.
    void Fatal(string message, Exception exception);
    // Logs a Fatal level message with an exception, message and format arguments.
    void Fatal(Exception exception, string message, object[] args);
    // Logs a Fatal level message with two typed arguments.
    void Fatal<TArgument1, TArgument2>(string message, TArgument1 argument1, TArgument2 argument2);
    // Logs a Fatal level message with three typed arguments.
```

### `Sparrow.Threading`

```csharp
// Runs a dispose action once, with concurrent callers waiting for the running dispose to finish. The type
// parameter selects error handling: ExceptionRetry retries on later calls after a failure, SingleAttempt
// stores the failure so later callers fail with it.
public sealed class DisposeOnce<TOperationMode> : IDisposable
{
    // Stores the dispose action and initializes the operation mode data. Throws NotSupportedException if the mode
    // type is neither ExceptionRetry nor SingleAttempt.
    DisposeOnce(Action action);
    // Returns true when the dispose has been started and completed; for SingleAttempt when not currently inside
    // Dispose, for ExceptionRetry when the dispose task completed without fault or cancellation.
    bool Disposed { get; }
    // Returns true if Disposed is true or a Dispose call is currently in progress in the operation mode.
    bool DisposedRequested { get; }
    /// Runs the dispose action. Ensures any threads that are running it concurrently wait for the dispose to finish
    ///    if it is in progress. If the dispose has already happened, the 'TOperationMode' defines how Dispose will
    ///    react. The two approaches differ only in error handling. When behavior is 'ExceptionRetry', we will retry
    ///    the Dispose until it succeeds. Retry, however, happens on successive calls to Dispose, rather than in a
    ///    single attempt. When behavior is 'SingleAttempt' or 'SingleAttemptWithWaitForDisposeToFinish', a failure
    ///    means all subsequent calls will fail by throwing the same exception that was thrown by the action.
    void Dispose();
}
```

```csharp
// Interface for the operation mode used by DisposeOnce, tracking whether a dispose call is in progress.
public interface IDisposeOnceOperationMode
{
    // Gets whether a dispose is currently in progress.
    bool DuringDispose { get; }
    // Marks the start of a Dispose call.
    void EnterDispose();
    // Initializes the operation mode state.
    void Initialize();
    // Marks the end of a Dispose call.
    void LeaveDispose();
}
```

```csharp
/// An atomic, thread-safe, multiple use flag that can be raised and lowered at will; meant to be single-user. DO
///    NOT PASS THIS AROUND.
public sealed class MultipleUseFlag
{
    // Copy constructor that always throws InvalidOperationException because copying a MultipleUseFlag is
    // forbidden.
    MultipleUseFlag(MultipleUseFlag other);
    // Creates a flag in the raised state if raised is true, otherwise lowered.
    MultipleUseFlag(bool raised);
    // Returns true if the flag is raised.
    bool IsRaised();
    /// Lowers the flag
    bool Lower();
    /// Lowers the flag. If already low, throws InvalidOperationException.
    void LowerOrDie();
    /// Raises the flag
    bool Raise();
    /// Raises the flag. If already up, throws InvalidOperationException.
    void RaiseOrDie();
}
```

```csharp
/// A thread-safe, multiple use flag that can be raised and lowered at will; meant to be shared between many
///    users.
public sealed class SharedMultipleUseFlag
{
    // Copy constructor that always throws NotImplementedException because copying is disallowed.
    SharedMultipleUseFlag(SharedMultipleUseFlag copy);
    // Creates a lowered flag.
    SharedMultipleUseFlag();
    // Creates a flag that is raised if raised is true, otherwise lowered.
    SharedMultipleUseFlag(bool raised);
    /// This flag is always lowered and should not be raised by anyone.
    static SharedMultipleUseFlag None;
    // Returns true if the underlying flag is raised.
    bool IsRaised();
    /// Lowers the flag
    bool Lower();
    /// Lowers the flag. If already low, throws InvalidOperationException.
    void LowerOrDie();
    /// Raises the flag
    bool Raise();
    /// Raises the flag. If already up, throws InvalidOperationException.
    void RaiseOrDie();
}
```

```csharp
// Operation mode struct for DisposeOnce that tracks dispose nesting depth with an interlocked counter; after a
// failed dispose, later callers fail with the stored exception.
public struct SingleAttempt : IDisposeOnceOperationMode
{
    // Returns true when the dispose depth counter is not zero.
    bool DuringDispose { get; }
    // Atomically increments the dispose depth counter.
    void EnterDispose();
    // Sets the dispose depth counter to zero.
    void Initialize();
    // Atomically decrements the dispose depth counter.
    void LeaveDispose();
}
```

```csharp
/// A thread-safe, single use flag that can be raised once; meant to be single-user. DO NOT PASS THIS AROUND.
public sealed class SingleUseFlag
{
    // Copy constructor that always throws InvalidOperationException because copying a SingleUseFlag is forbidden.
    SingleUseFlag(SingleUseFlag other);
    // Creates a flag in the raised state if raised is true, otherwise lowered.
    SingleUseFlag(bool raised);
    // Returns true if the flag is raised.
    bool IsRaised();
    /// Raises the flag
    bool Raise();
    /// Raises the flag. If already up, throws InvalidOperationException.
    void RaiseOrDie();
}
```

### `Sparrow.Utils`

```csharp
// Wraps a Lazy of T created from a factory and, when the factory throws, rethrows and recreates the lazy so a
// later access retries the factory.
public sealed class LazyWithExceptionRetry<T>
{
    // Stores the factory and creates the inner Lazy of T from it.
    LazyWithExceptionRetry(Func<T> factory);
    // Returns whether the inner lazy has created its value.
    bool IsValueCreated { get; }
    // Returns true if the last Value access threw and the factory is still being retried; reset to false after a
    // successful access.
    bool IsValueFaulted { get; }
    // Returns the lazily created value. On exception it marks the instance faulted, recreates the inner lazy for
    // retry and rethrows; after success the factory reference is cleared.
    T Value { get; }
}
```

```csharp
{

    // Creates the exception with the given message and inner exception by passing them to the base constructor.
    LockAlreadyDisposedException(string message, Exception innerException);
}
```
