```csharp
// Static class that allocates and frees unmanaged memory, tracks total and per-thread allocation counters,
// Lucene allocation counters, and registered memory-mapped files.
public static class NativeMemory
{
    // Concurrent dictionary of registered memory-mapped files, keyed by full file path, holding lazily created
    // FileMappingInfo entries.
    static ConcurrentDictionary<string, Lazy<NativeMemory.FileMappingInfo>> FileMapping;
    // Delegate used to obtain the current unmanaged thread id when a ThreadStats is created. Default returns the
    // constant 0xDEAD.
    static Func<UInt64> GetCurrentUnmanagedThreadId;
    // Enumerates the non-null ThreadStats instances of all threads that have thread-local allocation stats.
    static IEnumerable<NativeMemory.ThreadStats> AllThreadStats { get; }
    // Returns the ThreadStats instance held in the thread-local value for the current thread.
    static NativeMemory.ThreadStats CurrentThreadStats { get; }
    // Returns the running total of bytes allocated through AllocateMemory and not yet freed.
    static long TotalAllocatedMemory { get; }
    // Returns the running total of managed allocation bytes recorded for the Lucene term cache through the
    // increment and decrement methods.
    static long TotalLuceneManagedAllocationsForTermCache { get; }
    // Returns the running total of unmanaged bytes allocated for Lucene sorting and not yet freed.
    static long TotalLuceneUnmanagedAllocationsForSorting { get; }
    // Returns the running total of unmanaged bytes allocated for the Lucene term cache and not yet freed.
    static long TotalLuceneUnmanagedAllocationsForTermCache { get; }
    // Allocates the given number of bytes of unmanaged memory and adds the size to the total allocated counter.
    // Throws OutOfMemoryException with allocation details if allocation fails.
    static Byte* AllocateMemory(long size);
    // Allocates unmanaged memory like AllocateMemory(size), returns the current thread's ThreadStats through the
    // out parameter, and adds the size to that thread's Allocations.
    static Byte* AllocateMemory(long size, out NativeMemory.ThreadStats thread);
    // Adds the size to the Lucene sorting allocation counter and allocates unmanaged memory of that size.
    static Byte* AllocateMemoryForLuceneSorting(long size);
    // Adds the size to the Lucene term cache allocation counter and allocates unmanaged memory of that size.
    static Byte* AllocateMemoryForLuceneTermCache(long size);
    // Subtracts the given size from the counter of managed Lucene term cache allocations.
    static void DecrementLuceneManagedAllocations(long size);
    // Touches the thread-local ThreadStats value so the current thread is registered.
    static void EnsureRegistered();
    // Subtracts the size from the total allocated counter and releases the unmanaged memory at the pointer.
    static void Free(Byte* ptr, long size);
    // Frees the unmanaged memory like Free(ptr, size) and then updates the given ThreadStats: decrements
    // Allocations if it belongs to the current thread, otherwise records a release from another thread.
    static void Free(Byte* ptr, long size, NativeMemory.ThreadStats stats);
    // Subtracts the size from the Lucene sorting allocation counter and frees the unmanaged memory at the pointer.
    static void FreeMemoryByLuceneSorting(Byte* ptr, long size);
    // Subtracts the size from the Lucene term cache allocation counter and frees the unmanaged memory at the
    // pointer.
    static void FreeMemoryByLuceneTermCache(Byte* ptr, long size);
    // Adds the given size to the counter of managed Lucene term cache allocations.
    static void IncrementLuceneManagedAllocations(long size);
    // Sets the current thread's thread-local ThreadStats value to null.
    static void NotifyCurrentThreadAboutToClose();
    // Registers a mapped region start and size under the full path, creating an entry whose file type is derived
    // from the file name, and stores the allocated-size callback.
    static void RegisterFileMapping(string fullPath, IntPtr start, long size, Func<long> getAllocatedSize);
    // Removes the entry with the given name from the file mapping dictionary.
    static void UnregisterFileMapping(string name);
    // Removes the region starting at the given address from the named mapping, and removes the whole named entry
    // once it has no regions left.
    static void UnregisterFileMapping(string name, IntPtr start, long size);
}
```

```csharp
// Describes a registered file mapping: its file type, a callback for the allocated size, and a dictionary of
// mapped region start addresses to sizes.
public sealed class NativeMemory.FileMappingInfo
{
    // Creates an instance with an empty concurrent dictionary in Info.
    FileMappingInfo();
    // Gets or sets the kind of file mapped: Data, ScratchBuffer, CompressionBuffer or DecompressionBuffer.
    NativeMemory.FileType FileType { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the callback that returns the allocated size of the mapped file.
    Func<long> GetAllocatedSizeFunc { get; set; }
    // Gets or sets the concurrent dictionary mapping region start addresses to their sizes.
    ConcurrentDictionary<IntPtr, long> Info { get; set; }
}
```

```csharp
// Holds allocation statistics for a single thread, including its managed and unmanaged thread ids, allocation
// counters and a weak reference to the thread.
public sealed class NativeMemory.ThreadStats
{
    // Creates stats for the current thread, capturing its managed thread id, a new unique internal id and the
    // unmanaged thread id from GetCurrentUnmanagedThreadId.
    ThreadStats();
    // Counter of bytes allocated by this thread, decreased when memory is freed.
    long Allocations;
    // Public long field of the thread stats holding the memory currently allocated for processing; NativeMemory
    // itself does not modify it.
    long CurrentlyAllocatedForProcessing;
    // Static shared ThreadStats instance created with the private constructor, which does not capture any thread.
    static NativeMemory.ThreadStats Empty;
    // Id assigned from an incrementing static counter when the stats instance is created.
    int InternalId;
    // Managed thread id of the thread that created this instance.
    int ManagedThreadId;
    // Counter of bytes freed by threads other than the owning thread, interlocked-added in the free path.
    long ReleasesFromOtherThreads;
    // Unmanaged thread id obtained from GetCurrentUnmanagedThreadId when the instance is created.
    UInt64 UnmanagedThreadId;
    // Returns the name of the tracked thread if it is still referenced, otherwise the last captured name,
    // initially Unknown.
    string Name { get; }
    // Returns Allocations minus ReleasesFromOtherThreads.
    long TotalAllocated { get; }
    // Returns true if the referenced thread still exists and is alive. When the thread is found dead, it clears
    // the reference, saves its name and returns false.
    bool IsThreadAlive();
}
```

```csharp
// Disposable class that uses a timer to periodically scan stacks of pooled items and dispose items that were
// idle too long, or all items when the low memory flag is raised.
public sealed class NativeMemoryCleaner<TStack, TPooledItem> : IDisposable
{
    // Creates the cleaner, storing a weak reference to the cleanup target, the context getter, the low memory flag
    // and idle time, and starts a timer that fires with the given period as both due time and interval.
    NativeMemoryCleaner(object cleanupTarget, Func<object, ICollection<TStack>> getContexts, SharedMultipleUseFlag lowMemoryFlag, TimeSpan period, TimeSpan idleTime);
    // Timer callback that returns if another cleanup holds the lock. Otherwise it walks each stack and disposes
    // and nulls items that are idle past the idle time or all items under low memory, if InUse can be raised.
    void CleanNativeMemory(object state);
    // Disposes the timer and waits for its disposal handle to be signaled if the timer was active.
    void Dispose();
}
```

```csharp
// Abstract base class for pooled items that holds an InUse flag and the time the item entered the pool, and
// requires a Dispose implementation.
public abstract class PooledItem : IDisposable
{
    // Public field holding the DateTime at which the item entered the pool.
    DateTime InPoolSince;
    // Public MultipleUseFlag field marking whether the item is in use, initialized to a new flag.
    MultipleUseFlag InUse;
    // Abstract method that derived classes implement to release the item.
    void Dispose();
}
```

```csharp
// Generic class holding the Head node of a linked stack of StackNode items, plus a static node named
// HeaderDisposed.
public class StackHeader<T>
{
    // Default constructor with no logic.
    StackHeader();
    // Public field referencing the first StackNode of the stack.
    StackNode<T> Head;
    // Static readonly StackNode instance named HeaderDisposed, shared by all headers of the same type.
    static StackNode<T> HeaderDisposed;
}
```

```csharp
// Generic node holding a Value and a reference to the Next node in a singly linked stack.
public sealed class StackNode<T>
{
    // Default constructor with no logic.
    StackNode();
    // Public field referencing the next node.
    StackNode<T> Next;
    // Public field holding the value stored in the node.
    T Value;
}
```

```csharp
// Holds the security settings of an embedded server: certificate path, password, load executable and
// arguments, client certificate and server certificate thumbprint.
public sealed class ServerOptions.SecurityOptions
{
    // Gets the executable used to load the server certificate, passed to the server as the
    // Security.Certificate.Load.Exec setting. Set by the Secured overload taking an executable.
    string CertificateLoadExec { get; }
    // Gets the arguments for the certificate load executable, passed to the server as the
    // Security.Certificate.Load.Exec.Arguments setting.
    string CertificateLoadExecArguments { get; }
    // Gets the password of the certificate file, passed to the server as the Security.Certificate.Password setting
    // when not null.
    string CertificatePassword { get; }
    // Gets the path of the server certificate file, passed to the server as the Security.Certificate.Path setting
    // when not null.
    string CertificatePath { get; }
    // Gets the client certificate used by the embedded client when connecting, and whose thumbprint is configured
    // as the well-known admin certificate.
    X509Certificate2 ClientCertificate { get; }
    // Gets the expected server certificate thumbprint, compared with the certificate thumbprint in the embedded
    // client's remote certificate validation callback.
    string ServerCertificateThumbprint { get; }
}
```

## Members of the Sparrow, Raven.Embedded and Raven.TestDriver assemblies that no article mentions

These are public members of types of those assemblies that are described elsewhere, whose names appear nowhere else in this documentation.

### `Sparrow`

```csharp
public static class Memory
{
    // Compares size bytes at two pointers and returns 0 if the pointers are equal. Otherwise it returns a sequence
    // comparison result, using an AVX256 comparison when supported.
    static int CompareInline(Void* p1, Void* p2, int size);
    // Compares size bytes at two byte references, using an AVX256 comparison when supported and otherwise a span
    // sequence comparison.
    static int CompareInline(ref byte p1, ref byte p2, int size);
    // Compares two byte spans, using an AVX256 comparison on their starting references when supported and
    // otherwise comparing the spans with SequenceCompareTo.
    static int CompareInline(ReadOnlySpan<byte> p1, ReadOnlySpan<byte> p2, int size);
    // Copies n bytes from the source pointer to the destination pointer using an unaligned block copy.
    static void CopyUnaligned(Byte* dest, Byte* src, UInt32 n);
    // Returns whether the constant bytes equal the memory at the pointer for the constant's length. Throws
    // NotSupportedException if the length is 64 or more.
    static bool IsEqualConstant(ReadOnlySpan<byte> constant, Byte* ptr);
    // Returns whether size bytes at the constant pointer equal the memory at ptr. Throws NotSupportedException if
    // size is 64 or more.
    static bool IsEqualConstant(Byte* constant, int size, Byte* ptr);
    // Returns false if size differs from the constant length, otherwise whether the constant equals the memory at
    // the pointer. Throws NotSupportedException if the constant length is 64 or more.
    static bool IsEqualConstant(ReadOnlySpan<byte> constant, Byte* ptr, int size);
}
```

```csharp
public struct Size
{
    // Converts a value in the given unit to bytes by multiplying by the unit's byte factor. Throws
    // NotSupportedException for an unsupported unit.

    // Returns the stored byte size converted to the requested unit as a double.
    double GetDoubleValue(SizeUnit requestedUnit);
}
```

```csharp
public enum SizeUnit
{

    // Size unit equal to the OneGb byte factor, used when converting sizes.

    // Size unit equal to the OneTb byte factor, used when converting sizes.

}
```

```csharp
public struct TimeValue : IDynamicJson, IEquatable<TimeValue>
{
    // Returns true if the other value is a multiple of this one: for equal units, when its value divides evenly by
    // this value. For a month other value, when seconds per day divides evenly by this value. Otherwise false.
    bool IsMultiple(TimeValue multiple);
    // Returns 0 if from is not before to. Otherwise returns the number of this interval between the dates, using
    // month difference for months and seconds for seconds, and throws ArgumentException for other units.
    double TimesInInterval(DateTime from, DateTime to);
}
```

### `Sparrow.Json`

```csharp
public abstract class AbstractBlittableJsonTextWriter : IBlittableJsonTextWriter
{
    // Static byte array holding the UTF-8 bytes of the text false.
    static byte[] FalseBuffer;
    // Static byte array holding the UTF-8 bytes of the text null.
    static byte[] NullBuffer;
    // Static byte array holding the UTF-8 bytes of the text true.
    static byte[] TrueBuffer;
    // Returns the UTF-8 bytes of the quoted string NaN.
    static ReadOnlySpan<byte> NaNBuffer { get; }
    // Returns the UTF-8 bytes of the quoted string -Infinity.
    static ReadOnlySpan<byte> NegativeInfinityBuffer { get; }
    // Returns the UTF-8 bytes of a carriage return followed by a line feed.
    static ReadOnlySpan<byte> NewLineBuffer { get; }
    // Returns the UTF-8 bytes of the quoted string Infinity.
    static ReadOnlySpan<byte> PositiveInfinityBuffer { get; }
    // Writes true or false as JSON text into the buffer.
    void WriteBool(bool val);
    // Copies the given bytes into the output buffer as is, after ensuring buffer space.
    void WriteBufferFor(ReadOnlySpan<byte> buffer);
    // Writes a comma character to the buffer.
    void WriteComma();
    // Writes null and returns 0 if the value is null, otherwise writes the date in the default Raven format as a
    // quoted string and returns the number of bytes.
    int WriteDateTime(DateTime? value, bool isUtc);
    // Writes the date in the default Raven format as a quoted string without escaping and returns the number of
    // bytes written.
    int WriteDateTime(DateTime value, bool isUtc);
    // Writes NaN, Infinity or -Infinity as quoted strings for those special values, otherwise writes the number's
    // underlying string text unquoted.
    void WriteDouble(LazyNumberValue val);
    // Writes NaN, Infinity or -Infinity as quoted strings for those special values, otherwise formats the double
    // as UTF-8 text and writes it. Throws InvalidOperationException if formatting fails.
    void WriteDouble(double val);
    // Writes a closing square bracket to the buffer.
    void WriteEndArray();
    // Writes a closing curly bracket to the buffer.
    void WriteEndObject();
    // Writes the signed integer as decimal digits, with a leading minus sign for negative values.
    void WriteInteger(long val);
    // Writes the unsigned integer as decimal digits.
    void WriteInteger(UInt64 val);
    // Writes the given number of bytes from the native pointer to the output by delegating to the byte pointer
    // overload.
    void WriteMemoryChunk(IntPtr ptr, int size);
    // Copies size bytes from the pointer into the output buffer in chunks, flushing the buffer to the stream after
    // each chunk.
    void WriteMemoryChunk(Byte* ptr, int size);
    // Writes a carriage return and line feed to the buffer.
    void WriteNewLine();
    // Writes the text null to the buffer.
    void WriteNull();
    // Writes null if the object is null, otherwise writes the object as JSON with its properties in insertion
    // order, separated by commas.
    void WriteObject(BlittableJsonReaderObject obj);
    // Writes the given bytes to the buffer as raw string content, without adding a colon.
    void WritePropertyName(ReadOnlySpan<byte> prop);
    // Writes the property name as a quoted string followed by a colon.
    void WritePropertyName(LazyStringValue prop);
    // Writes the property name as a quoted string, using a cached lazy string of the context, followed by a colon.
    void WritePropertyName(string prop);
    // Writes the property name segment as a quoted string, using a cached lazy string of the context, followed by
    // a colon.
    void WritePropertyName(StringSegment prop);
    // Writes the bytes to the buffer without quoting or escaping, using a large-write path when the length is at
    // least the buffer size.
    void WriteRawString(ReadOnlySpan<byte> buffer);
    // Writes the buffer contents surrounded by quotes without escaping.
    void WriteRawStringWhichMustBeWithoutEscapeChars(Byte* buffer, int size);
    // Writes an opening square bracket to the buffer.
    void WriteStartArray();
    // Writes an opening curly bracket to the buffer.
    void WriteStartObject();
    // Writes the string as a quoted JSON string through a lazy string value of the context, with optional skipping
    // of escaping.
    void WriteString(string str, bool skipEscaping);
    // Writes null if the value is null, otherwise writes it as a quoted JSON string, with optional skipping of
    // escaping.
    void WriteString(LazyStringValue str, bool skipEscaping);
    // Writes the compressed string value as a quoted JSON string after decompressing it to a temporary buffer.
    void WriteString(LazyCompressedStringValue str);
    // Writes a value according to the blittable token: string, integer, array, object, number, boolean, null, blob
    // or vector. Throws DataMisalignedException for an unidentified token.
    void WriteValue(BlittableJsonToken token, object val);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class BlittableJsonReaderArray : BlittableJsonReaderBase, IEnumerable<object>, IDisposable
{
    // Pointer to the start of the array's data inside the parent object's memory.
    Byte* DataStart { get; }
    // Writes every item of the array to the given manual blittable document builder, passing each value with its
    // type token.
    void AddItemsToStream<T>(ManualBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder<T> writer);
    // Binary search over the array items as strings using the given comparison. Returns the index if found,
    // otherwise the bitwise complement of the insertion position.
    int BinarySearch(string key, StringComparison comparison);
    // Validates the blittable data by calling BlittableValidation on the parent object, if there is one.
    void BlittableValidation();
    // Returns an array reader that uses the given external context for allocations while still reading from the
    // original memory without copying. Throws InvalidOperationException if the root array cannot be found.
    BlittableJsonReaderArray CloneForConcurrentRead(JsonOperationContext externalContext);
    // Creates the Modifications array if it is null and sets its SkipOriginalArray flag to true.
    void EnsureArrayModifiable();
    // Returns the type token of the array elements, read from the array metadata.
    BlittableJsonToken GetArrayType();
    // Returns the item at the given index converted to type T. Throws IndexOutOfRangeException if the index is
    // outside the array.
    T GetByIndex<T>(int index);
    // Returns the item at the given index as a string, or null if the item is null. Other value types are
    // converted to string.
    string GetStringByIndex(int index);
    // Returns the item at the given index together with its type token, caching nested blittable readers unless
    // NoCache is set. Throws IndexOutOfRangeException if the index is outside the array.
    ValueTuple<object, BlittableJsonToken> GetValueTokenTupleByIndex(int index);
}
```

```csharp
public abstract class BlittableJsonReaderBase
{
    // Constant mask combining the blittable value type flags: Boolean, LazyNumber, Integer, Null, StartArray,
    // StartObject, String, CompressedString and Vector.
    const BlittableJsonToken TypesMask;
    // Returns true when the reader has a parent object.
    bool HasParent { get; }
    // Gets or sets a flag that disables caching of values read through this reader.
    bool NoCache { get; set; }
    // Returns true if the given context is the context this reader was created with.
    bool BelongsToContext(JsonOperationContext context);
    // Masks the token with TypesMask and returns the type. Throws ArgumentException if the type is not a valid
    // value type token.
    static BlittableJsonToken ProcessTokenTypeFlags(BlittableJsonToken currentType);
    // Reads a compressed string at the given position, using its uncompressed and compressed sizes, and returns it
    // as a lazy compressed string value.
    LazyCompressedStringValue ReadCompressStringLazily(int pos);
    // Reads the size at the given position and returns a nested blittable object reader over that memory,
    // inheriting the NoCache setting.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject ReadNestedObject(int pos);
    // Reads the string size at the given position and returns a lazy string value over the stored bytes.
    LazyStringValue ReadStringLazily(int pos);
    // Reads a variable-size integer at the given position and returns it, with the number of bytes consumed in
    // offset.
    int ReadVariableSizeInt(int pos, out int offset);
    // Reads a variable-size integer from the buffer at the given position and advances the position by the bytes
    // consumed.
    static int ReadVariableSizeInt(Byte* buffer, ref int pos);
    // Reads a variable-size integer from the buffer at the given position. Returns the value, the bytes consumed
    // in offset, and whether the read succeeded in success.
    static int ReadVariableSizeInt(Byte* buffer, int pos, out int offset, out bool success);
    // Reads a variable-size integer from the byte span at the given position in compact form. Returns the value,
    // the bytes consumed in offset, and the success flag.
    static int ReadVariableSizeInt(ReadOnlySpan<byte> buffer, int pos, out int offset, out bool success);
    // Reads a variable-size integer backwards from the given position, moving toward lower addresses. Throws
    // FormatException if the encoding exceeds the allowed shift.
    static int ReadVariableSizeIntInReverse(Byte* buffer, int pos, out byte offset);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class BlittableJsonReaderObject : BlittableJsonReaderBase, IDisposable
{
    // Pointer to the start of the object's memory. Throws InvalidOperationException if the object is a nested
    // object.
    Byte* BasePointer { get; }
    // Hash of the object's memory bytes computed with XXHash64 over its size.
    UInt64 DebugHash { get; }
    // Writes each property of the object, in insertion order, with its name and value to the given manual
    // blittable document builder.
    void AddItemsToStream<T>(ManualBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder<T> writer);
    // Debug-only check that throws InvalidOperationException if the object has pending modifications (removals or
    // properties), optionally also checking nested objects and arrays. Throws if both assertRemovals and
    // assertProperties are false.
    static void AssertNoModifications(BlittableJsonReaderObject data, string id, bool assertChildren, bool assertRemovals, bool assertProperties);
    // Returns a read-only span over the object's memory using its base pointer and size.
    ReadOnlySpan<byte> AsSpan();
    // Static method that wraps the given memory in a reader and runs its validation.
    static void BlittableValidation(JsonOperationContext context, Byte* mem, int size);
    // Validates the structure of the object's memory, throwing InvalidDataException when the root token, offsets
    // or tokens are invalid.
    void BlittableValidation();
    // Returns a new root reader over the same memory using the given external context, without copying data.
    // Throws InvalidOperationException if the object is not a root.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CloneForConcurrentRead(JsonOperationContext externalContext);
    // Returns a copy of the object using the same context as this object.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject CloneOnTheSameContext();
    // Computes an XXHash64 hash over the property count and all property names, taken in their stored order.
    UInt64 GetHashOfPropertyNames();
    // Returns a disposable structure holding property indexes ordered by their stored offsets, giving insertion
    // order.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject.InsertionOrderProperties GetPropertiesByInsertionOrder();
    // Fills the given PropertyDetails with the name, value and token of the property at the index, optionally
    // adding the value to the cache. Throws ArgumentOutOfRangeException if the index is invalid.
    void GetPropertyByIndex(int index, ref BlittableJsonReaderObject.PropertyDetails prop, bool addObjectToCache);
    // Returns the index of the property with the given name using binary search, or -1 if not found.
    int GetPropertyIndex(string name);
    // Returns the index of the property whose name matches the given lazy string using binary search, or -1 if not
    // found.
    int GetPropertyIndex(LazyStringValue comparer);
    // Returns the property names as an array in the order they are stored in the object's sorted metadata.
    string[] GetSortedPropertyNames();
    // Tries to get the named property and convert it to type T. Returns false with a default value if the property
    // is missing or the conversion fails.
    bool TryGetWithoutThrowingOnError<T>(string name, out T obj);
    // Tries to get the property named by the string segment and convert it to type T. Returns false with a default
    // value if the property is missing or the conversion fails.
    bool TryGetWithoutThrowingOnError<T>(StringSegment name, out T obj);
    // Writes the object as JSON text to the given stream using a blittable JSON text writer.
    void WriteJsonTo(Stream stream);
    // Writes the object as JSON text to the given stream asynchronously through the context and flushes the
    // writer.
    ValueTask WriteJsonToAsync(Stream stream, CancellationToken token);
}
```

```csharp
public class JsonOperationContext : PooledItem
{
    // Public field holding the CachedProperties instance of the context, created in the constructor and renewed or
    // reset with the context.
    CachedProperties CachedProperties;
    /// This flag means that this should be disposed, usually because we exceeded the maximum amount of memory budget
    ///    we have and need to return it to the system
    bool DoNotReuse;
    // Constant with the value 4096, the minimum initial size used when allocating a stream buffer.
    const int InitialStreamSize;
    // Returns the amount of memory allocated by the context's arena allocator.
    long AllocatedMemory { get; }
    // Returns true when the context has been disposed.
    bool Disposed { get; }
    // Returns the total memory used according to the context's arena allocator.
    long UsedMemory { get; }
    // Acquires path cache dictionaries (by segment and by index) from the context's active path caches.
    void AcquirePathCache(out Dictionary<StringSegment, object> pathCache, out Dictionary<int, object> pathCacheByIndex);
    // Rents an array of at least the given size from the shared array pool and tracks it in the context for later
    // return.
    T[] AllocatePooledArray<T>(int size);
    // Returns a lazy string value over the given pointer and size, reusing a pooled instance when available or
    // creating a new one.
    LazyStringValue AllocateStringValue(string str, Byte* ptr, int size);
    // Sets the arena allocator's AvoidOverAllocation flag to true and returns a scope that resets it to false when
    // disposed.
    JsonOperationContext.AvoidOverAllocationScope AvoidOverAllocation();
    // Returns a lazy string value holding the UTF-8 encoding of the given string, or null if the string is null.
    // Throws ObjectDisposedException if the context is disposed.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyString(string field);
    // Encodes the characters as UTF-8 into context memory, records escape positions, and returns a lazy string
    // value. Uses long-lived memory when longLived is true.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyString(ReadOnlySpan<Char> value, string strValue, bool longLived);
    // Copies the bytes of the span into context memory and returns a lazy string value, using long-lived memory
    // when longLived is true.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyString(Span<byte> span, bool longLived);
    // Copies the bytes into context memory, records JSON escape positions, and returns a lazy string value. Uses
    // long-lived memory when longLived is true.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyString(Byte* ptr, int size, bool longLived);
    // Returns a lazy string for the field name segment from the context's field-name cache, creating a long-lived
    // one and caching it when missing.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyStringForFieldWithCaching(StringSegment key);
    // Returns a lazy string for the field name from the context's field-name cache, creating a long-lived one and
    // caching it when missing.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyStringForFieldWithCaching(string field);
    // Copies the bytes byte-for-byte into context memory without the escape-position pass and returns a lazy
    // string value.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyStringRaw(Byte* ptr, int size, bool longLived);
    // Reads a variable-size length prefix at the pointer and returns a lazy string value over the following bytes,
    // with success indicating whether the length read succeeded.
    LazyStringValue GetLazyStringValue(Byte* ptr, out bool success);
    // Allocates memory of the requested size from the context's long-lived arena allocator. Throws
    // ObjectDisposedException if that allocator is not available after a reset.
    AllocatedMemoryData GetLongLivedMemory(int requestedSize);
    // Allocates memory of the requested size from the context's arena allocator and marks it with the current
    // context generation.
    AllocatedMemoryData GetMemory(int requestedSize);
    // Gets a memory buffer of the default size from the context and returns a disposable that returns the memory.
    JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer.ReturnBuffer GetMemoryBuffer(out JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Gets a memory buffer of the given size from the context and returns a disposable that returns the memory.
    // Throws if the context is disposed.
    JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer.ReturnBuffer GetMemoryBuffer(int size, out JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer buffer);
    // Allocates raw memory of the given size, outputs it in buffer, and returns a disposable that returns the
    // memory.
    JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer.ReturnBuffer GetRawMemoryBuffer(int size, out AllocatedMemoryData buffer);
    /// Generates new unmanaged stream. Should be disposed at the end of the usage.
    UnmanagedWriteBuffer GetStream(int initialSize);
    // Attempts to grow the given allocation by the given number of bytes using the arena allocator and returns
    // whether it succeeded.
    bool GrowAllocation(AllocatedMemoryData allocation, int sizeIncrease);
    // Parses JSON from the unmanaged buffer into a blittable object using the given usage mode and optional
    // modifier. Throws EndOfStreamException if the buffer ends before the JSON is complete.
    BlittableJsonReaderObject ParseBuffer(Byte* buffer, int length, string debugTag, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier);
    // Parses a JSON array string into a blittable array reader using the given usage mode and optional modifier.
    // Throws EndOfStreamException if the content ends before the JSON is complete.
    BlittableJsonReaderArray ParseBufferToArray(string value, string debugTag, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier);
    // Reads JSON from the web socket into a blittable object using the supplied buffer. Throws
    // EndOfStreamException if a read returns no bytes before the JSON is complete.
    ValueTask<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ParseToMemoryAsync(WebSocket webSocket, string debugTag, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer bytes, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier, CancellationToken token);
    // Reads JSON from the stream into a blittable object using the supplied buffer. Throws EndOfStreamException if
    // the stream ends early and ArgumentException if maxSize is exceeded.
    ValueTask<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ParseToMemoryAsync(Stream stream, string documentId, BlittableJsonDocumentBuilder.UsageMode mode, JsonOperationContext.MemoryBuffer bytes, IBlittableDocumentModifier modifier, CancellationToken? token, int maxSize);
    // Parses the stream into a blittable object using the ToDisk usage mode.
    ValueTask<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ReadForDiskAsync(Stream stream, string documentId, CancellationToken? token);
    // Parses the stream into a blittable object using the None usage mode.
    ValueTask<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ReadForMemoryAsync(Stream stream, string documentId, CancellationToken? token);
    // Reads a JSON object from the web socket into a blittable object, returning null if a Close message is
    // received.
    Task<BlittableJsonReaderObject> ReadFromWebSocketAsync(WebSocket webSocket, string debugTag, CancellationToken token);
    // Releases the given path cache dictionaries back to the context's active path caches.
    void ReleasePathCache(Dictionary<StringSegment, object> pathCache, Dictionary<int, object> pathCacheByIndex);
    // Returns the allocation to the arena allocator. Throws InvalidOperationException if the allocation belongs to
    // a previous context generation.
    void ReturnMemory(AllocatedMemoryData allocation);
    // Creates a new JsonOperationContext with initial size 4096, long-lived size 1024, a maximum of 8192 allocated
    // string values, and no low-memory flag.
    static JsonOperationContext ShortTermSingleUse();
    // Writes the JSON array at the parser's current position to the writer. Throws InvalidOperationException if
    // the current token is not StartArray.
    void WriteArray<TWriter>(TWriter writer, JsonParserState state, ObjectJsonParser parser);
    // Writes the blittable object as JSON to the stream with an async text writer and flushes it.
    ValueTask WriteAsync(Stream stream, BlittableJsonReaderObject json, CancellationToken token);
    // Writes the JSON object at the parser's current position to the writer. Throws InvalidOperationException if
    // the current token is not StartObject.
    void WriteObject<TWriter>(TWriter writer, JsonParserState state, ObjectJsonParser parser);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class LazyNumberValue : IComparable, IConvertible
{
    // Compares this value with a double, long or LazyNumberValue and returns the comparison result. Throws
    // NotSupportedException for other types.
    int CompareTo(object that);
    // Returns TypeCode.Object.
    TypeCode GetTypeCode();
    // Returns true if the cached double value is NaN or the stored text equals NaN.
    bool IsNaN();
    // Returns true if the cached double value is negative infinity or the stored text equals -Infinity.
    bool IsNegativeInfinity();
    // Returns true if the cached double value is positive infinity or the stored text equals Infinity.
    bool IsPositiveInfinity();
    // Returns true if the stored text is 0 and false if it is 1; otherwise throws InvalidCastException.
    bool ToBoolean(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to byte.
    byte ToByte(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to char.
    Char ToChar(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Creates a DateTime whose ticks are the value converted to long.
    DateTime ToDateTime(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns the value as a decimal.
    decimal ToDecimal(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns the value as a double.
    double ToDouble(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to short.
    Int16 ToInt16(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to int.
    int ToInt32(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns the value as a long.
    long ToInt64(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to sbyte.
    SByte ToSByte(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to float.
    float ToSingle(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns typeof(LazyNumberValue) regardless of the requested type.
    object ToType(Type conversionType, IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to ushort.
    UInt16 ToUInt16(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the value to double and casts it to uint.
    UInt32 ToUInt32(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns the value as an unsigned long.
    UInt64 ToUInt64(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Compares this value with a double, long or LazyNumberValue and returns the result, or null for other types.
    int? TryCompareTo(object that);
}
```

```csharp
public sealed class LazyStringValue : IComparable<string>, IEquatable<string>, IComparable<LazyStringValue>, IEquatable<LazyStringValue>, IDisposable, IComparable, IConvertible, IEnumerable<Char>, ISpanFormattable
{
    // Holds the memory block allocated for this value; it is returned to the context when the value is disposed or
    // renewed, if the context generation still matches.
    AllocatedMemoryData AllocatedMemoryData;
    // Holds the positions of characters that need escaping in the value; it is set to null when the value is
    // renewed.
    int[] EscapePositions;
    // Set to true when the value is disposed and reset to false when the value is renewed.
    bool IsDisposed;
    // Returns true if a string representation has already been materialized for this value.
    bool HasStringValue { get; }
    // Returns a read-only span over the value's underlying UTF-8 buffer using its byte size.
    ReadOnlySpan<byte> AsReadOnlySpan();
    // Returns a writable span over the value's underlying UTF-8 buffer using its byte size.
    Span<byte> AsSpan();
    // Sets the thread-static temporary char and comparison buffers to null so they can be released; also
    // registered as a thread-local cleanup handler.
    static void CleanBuffers();
    // Returns a copy for the given context that keeps the cached string and buffer reference; returns the
    // context's empty value when the size is 0.
    LazyStringValue CloneForConcurrentRead(JsonOperationContext context);
    // Returns a new lazy string copied from the buffer on the same context, or that context's empty value when the
    // size is 0.
    LazyStringValue CloneOnSameContext();
    // Compares ordinally with the given string, using the cached string if present or otherwise comparing the
    // UTF-8 bytes of the string.
    int CompareTo(string other);
    // Compares with another lazy string, returning 0 if both share the same buffer and size, otherwise comparing
    // their bytes.
    int CompareTo(LazyStringValue other);
    // Compares with a LazyStringValue or string; returns 1 for null and throws NotSupportedException for other
    // types or ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int CompareTo(object obj);
    // Compares two UTF-8 byte buffers ignoring ASCII case, falling back to an ordinal ignore-case comparison of
    // the decoded strings when non-ASCII bytes are met.
    static int CompareToOrdinalIgnoreCase(Byte* strA, int strALen, Byte* strB, int strBLen);
    // Returns true if the other value is equal to this one under an ordinal ignore-case comparison of their
    // buffers.
    bool EqualsOrdinalIgnoreCase(LazyStringValue other);
    // Returns the TypeCode of the materialized string value.
    TypeCode GetTypeCode();
    // Returns the index of the character within the whole value, or -1 if not found.
    int IndexOf(Char value);
    // Returns the index of the character using the given comparison type, via the cached string or the
    // materialized string.
    int IndexOf(Char value, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the index of the character searching from the start index to the end of the value.
    int IndexOf(Char value, int startIndex);
    // Returns the index of the character within the given range, using the cached string or decoding the buffer;
    // throws ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int IndexOf(Char value, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns the ordinal index of the string in the materialized string value.
    int IndexOf(string value);
    // Returns the index of the string in the materialized string, starting at the given index.
    int IndexOf(string value, int startIndex);
    // Returns the index of the string in the materialized string within the given start index and count.
    int IndexOf(string value, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns the index of the string from the start index using the given comparison type, on the materialized
    // string.
    int IndexOf(string value, int startIndex, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the index of the string within the given range using the given comparison type, on the materialized
    // string.
    int IndexOf(string value, int startIndex, int count, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the index of the string using the given comparison type, on the materialized string.
    int IndexOf(string value, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the index of the first occurrence of any of the characters, searching the whole value.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf);
    // Returns the index of the first occurrence of any of the characters, starting at the given index.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex);
    // Returns the index of the first occurrence of any of the characters within the given range; throws
    // ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int IndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns true if the single byte of the value is below 32 or between 127 and 159, and outputs that byte;
    // asserts the size is 1.
    bool IsControlCodeCharacter(out byte b);
    // Returns whether the materialized string is in Unicode normalization form C.
    bool IsNormalized();
    // Returns whether the materialized string is in the specified Unicode normalization form.
    bool IsNormalized(NormalizationForm normalizationForm);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of the character, searching backward from the end of the value.
    int LastIndexOf(Char value);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of the character, searching backward from the start index.
    int LastIndexOf(Char value, int startIndex);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of the character within the given range; throws
    // ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int LastIndexOf(Char value, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns the last ordinal index of the string in the value, using the cached string if present.
    int LastIndexOf(string value);
    // Returns the last index of the string using the given comparison type, on the materialized string.
    int LastIndexOf(string value, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the last index of the string searching backward from the start index, on the materialized string.
    int LastIndexOf(string value, int startIndex);
    // Returns the last index of the string within the given start index and count, on the materialized string.
    int LastIndexOf(string value, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns the last index of the string from the start index using the given comparison type, on the
    // materialized string.
    int LastIndexOf(string value, int startIndex, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the last index of the string within the given range using the given comparison type, on the
    // materialized string.
    int LastIndexOf(string value, int startIndex, int count, StringComparison comparisonType);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of any of the characters, searching backward from the end.
    int LastIndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of any of the characters, searching backward from the start index.
    int LastIndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex);
    // Returns the index of the last occurrence of any of the characters within the given range; throws
    // ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    int LastIndexOfAny(Char[] anyOf, int startIndex, int count);
    // Returns the materialized string left-padded with spaces to the total width.
    string PadLeft(int totalWidth);
    // Returns the materialized string left-padded with the given character to the total width.
    string PadLeft(int totalWidth, Char paddingChar);
    // Returns the materialized string right-padded with spaces to the total width.
    string PadRight(int totalWidth);
    // Returns the materialized string right-padded with the given character to the total width.
    string PadRight(int totalWidth, Char paddingChar);
    // Returns false if the prefix is longer than this value; otherwise compares the first bytes of the value with
    // the prefix ignoring case.
    bool StartsWithOrdinalIgnoreCase(LazyStringValue prefix);
    // Converts the materialized string to Boolean using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    bool ToBoolean(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Byte using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    byte ToByte(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Char using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    Char ToChar(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to DateTime using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    DateTime ToDateTime(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Decimal using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    decimal ToDecimal(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Double using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    double ToDouble(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Int16 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    Int16 ToInt16(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Int32 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    int ToInt32(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Int64 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    long ToInt64(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to SByte using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    SByte ToSByte(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to Single using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    float ToSingle(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Parses the materialized string as an enum when the target type is an enum; otherwise converts it to the
    // target type through the IConvertible implementation of string.
    object ToType(Type conversionType, IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to UInt16 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    UInt16 ToUInt16(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to UInt32 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    UInt32 ToUInt32(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Converts the materialized string to UInt64 using the IConvertible implementation of string with the given
    // format provider.
    UInt64 ToUInt64(IFormatProvider provider);
    // Returns the materialized string converted to upper case using the current culture.
    string ToUpper();
    // Returns the materialized string converted to upper case using the given culture.
    string ToUpper(CultureInfo culture);
    // Returns the materialized string converted to upper case using the invariant culture.
    string ToUpperInvariant();
    // Returns the materialized string with trailing white-space removed.
    string TrimEnd();
    // Returns the materialized string with trailing occurrences of the given character removed.
    string TrimEnd(Char trimChar);
    // Returns the materialized string with trailing occurrences of any of the given characters removed.
    string TrimEnd(Char[] trimChars);
    // Returns the materialized string with leading white-space removed.
    string TrimStart();
    // Returns the materialized string with leading occurrences of the given character removed.
    string TrimStart(Char trimChar);
    // Returns the materialized string with leading occurrences of any of the given characters removed.
    string TrimStart(Char[] trimChars);
    // Sets the value's byte size to the given size, resets the cached length and clears the cached string.
    void Truncate(int size);
    // Copies the characters into the destination span via TryFormat; returns false if the destination is shorter
    // than the value length.
    bool TryCopyTo(Span<Char> destination);
    // Writes the characters into the destination span and outputs the count written; returns false with 0 written
    // if the destination is too small; throws ObjectDisposedException if disposed.
    bool TryFormat(Span<Char> destination, out int charsWritten, ReadOnlySpan<Char> format, IFormatProvider provider);
}
```

```csharp
    // Total committable memory amount.

    // Total physical memory amount.

    // Total swap size.
    Size TotalSwapSize;
    // Total swap usage amount.
    Size TotalSwapUsage;
    // Swap usage amount of the process working set.
    Size WorkingSetSwapUsage;
}
```

```csharp
public static class StringUtils
{
    // Clears the buffer, then adds the positions of bytes that are quote, backslash or below 32 as offsets from
    // the previous escape; skips the work when the precomputed size shows no escapes.
    static void FindEscapedPositions(FastList<int> buffer, Byte* str, int len, int previousComputedMaxSize);
    // Finds escape positions in the buffer and expands control characters in place to backslash-u escape
    // sequences, growing len; throws if the extra space exceeds the precomputed size.
    static void FindEscapedPositionsAndEscapeControlsForBackwardCompatibility(JsonParserState state, Byte* str, ref int len, int previousComputedMaxSize);
    // Counts the bytes needing escape and returns (count + 1) times 5, the maximum size for storing the escape
    // positions.
    static int FindMaxEscapePositionSize(Byte* str, int size);
    // Counts the characters needing escape and returns (count + 1) times 5, the maximum size for storing the
    // escape positions.
    static int FindMaxEscapePositionSize(ReadOnlySpan<Char> str);
    // Creates a LazyStringValue by copying the bytes into context memory, escaping control characters in place,
    // and storing the escape positions after the data; the memory is long-lived when requested.
    static LazyStringValue GetLazyStringForBackwardCompatibility(JsonOperationContext context, Byte* ptr, int size, bool longLived);
    // Returns true if the bytes contain any control character.
    static bool HasControlCharacters(ReadOnlySpan<byte> str);
    // Returns true if the characters contain any control character.
    static bool HasControlCharacters(ReadOnlySpan<Char> str);
    // Returns true if the character is below 8, between 14 and 31, or equals 11.
    static bool IsControlCharacter(Char c);
}
```

```csharp
public static class StringUtils.Compatibility
{
    // Finds escape positions and expands control characters in place into backslash-u escape sequences, updating
    // len; throws if the extra size exceeds the precomputed size.
    static void FindEscapedPositionsAndEscapeControls(FastList<int> buffer, Byte* str, ref int len, int previousComputedMaxSize);
    // Returns the maximum size needed for escape positions and control character expansion, and outputs the number
    // of control characters found.
    static int FindMaxEscapePositionAndControlCharSize(Byte* str, int size, out int escapedCount);
    // Returns the maximum size needed for escape positions and control character expansion for the characters, and
    // outputs the number of control characters found.
    static int FindMaxEscapePositionAndControlCharSize(ReadOnlySpan<Char> str, out int controlCount);
}
```

### Protected members of Sparrow types

The listings above show public members. The following members of public Sparrow types are `protected` or `protected internal`, so they are visible only to classes that derive from the type. They are listed by name only; no description is given because they were not reviewed.

* `AbstractBlittableJsonDocumentBuilder`: `ClearState`, `_propertiesCache`, `_positionsCache`, `_tokensCache`, `_continuationState`
* `AbstractBlittableJsonTextWriter`: `EnsureBuffer`, `FlushInternal`, `DisposeInternal`, `_context`
* `ArenaMemoryAllocator`: `Finalize`
* `BlittableJsonReaderBase`: `ProcessTokenPropertyFlags`, `ProcessTokenOffsetFlags`, `ReadNumber`, `ReadVariableSizeLong`, `AssertContextNotDisposed`, `_parent`, `_mem`, `_context`
* `JsonContextPool`: `CreateContext`
* `JsonContextPoolBase`: `CreateContext`, `LowMemoryFlag`
* `JsonOperationContext`: `_arenaAllocator`, `LowMemoryFlag`
* `UnmanagedBuffersPool`: `Finalize`, `_debugTag`, `_databaseName`
